> **Version:** 1.110.0 (2026-09-21 — view-select-nonnull-narrowing: closes #539. Every repository read against a `v_*` view that ended in a blanket `as <Interface>Row[]` cast now builds its return rows field-by-field through a new `nonNull(value, column)` helper (`app/src/repositories/row-helpers.ts`) — a column the row interface declares non-null is wrapped (`nonNull(row.field, "snake_case_column")`), a column already declared nullable (`T | null`, `unknown`) passes through unwrapped. Eight sites across four files: `schedule.repository.ts`'s `findScheduleRows`/`findScheduleDayRows`, `routine.repository.ts`'s `findRoutineExecutionRows`/`findExerciseTemplateCatalog`, `statistics.repository.ts`'s `findSessionSummaries`/`findVisitFacts`/`findLegFacts`, and `training-session.repository.ts`'s `findRoutineTemplateSteps`. Each function's covering test gained a "throws instead of silently returning a null `<column>`" case proving the narrowing is live, not just that the happy path still passes; `training-session.repository.test.ts`'s pre-existing `renderingDb` tuple fixture for `findRoutineTemplateSteps` was one element short (missing `gameRulesetVersionKey`, added by D339/D340 after the fixture was written) — invisible under the old blanket cast, which the new per-column narrowing caught immediately (`durationValue` resolving to the shifted-in `defaultConfiguration` object and failing `nonNull`), fixed in the same commit. New **D348** (`decisions/database.md`). Canonical edit: `05-Database/05-Views/00-Overview.md` gains a paragraph on the convention, next to the existing "`drizzle-kit introspect` types every view column nullable" note it builds on; `00-File-Inventory.md`'s row updated (~2.9k → ~3.4k). Filed issue #545 (`discovered-work`): `findX01CheckoutDarts` (`statistics.repository.ts`) still ends in the identical blanket cast — missed by #539's own discovery grep because `X01CheckoutDartRow` starts with a digit the grep's letters-only prefix pattern didn't match — left unfixed, out of #539's named scope. `findScheduleIdsUsingRoutine`'s narrower `as { scheduleId: string }[]` cast (one projected field, not a full row interface) was deliberately left alone, same reasoning. Validation: `npm test` 4876/4876 across 262 files; `npm run check` 0 errors/0 warnings/0 hints across 699 files; `bash scripts/fallow-gate.sh` 0 above threshold; type-barrels/file-locations/no-inline-comments/test-coverage/alias-sync/agent-mirrors/skill-pointers/context-budget/doc-links/context-map/decision-ids/doc-sync gates all pass. `db:status`/`db:migrate`/`db:drift`/`db:introspect` not run — no `DATABASE_URL` in this session (D193); this task touched no SQL and no `schema.ts`-visible shape, so nothing in this change needed them.)

> **Version:** 1.109.0 (2026-09-20 — weekly-training-schedules PR-blockers: the fix pass that makes PR #536 landable, closing issues #537 and #538 and the four type-gate errors that stopped CI's `quality / app` job at its second step. (1) #537: `app/src/db/schema.ts` had never been regenerated for migration `0041`, so `schedule.repository.ts`'s four `@db/schema` imports did not resolve. `0041` was applied to the Neon dev branch and `drizzle-kit introspect` re-emitted `schema.ts` — the sanctioned path, run here because this session has a real `DATABASE_URL`, which the session that built the feature did not. The regenerated view objects then surfaced a second, previously masked error: view columns are nullable in Drizzle's introspection, so `findScheduleIdsUsingRoutine` returned `(string | null)[]` where it promised `string[]`; typed with the same `as …[]` assertion the repositories already use for view selects. (2) #538: `0041`'s `v_training_schedules.day_count` was an uncast `count(*)` — `bigint`, which node-postgres hands back as a string — and gained `::int`, matching its sibling `routine_minutes`. `0041` was edited in place under the new carve-out in **D344** (`decisions/database.md`): a migration is correctable in place only while unmerged *and* proven pending by both `db:status` and `db:status:prod`, both of which reported `0041` pending before the edit. Root `CLAUDE.md`'s never-modify-applied-migrations invariant is rewritten around that proof rather than around `0040`/`0041` by name; both are now applied, so the chain through `0041` is closed again. (3) With the type gate green, two further gates that CI had never reached failed: `scripts/fallow-gate.sh` on a `client.ts` → `sessions.ts` → `client.ts` import cycle the branch introduced by putting `unwrapOrThrow` in `client.ts`, fixed by moving the helper (and its tests) to `app/src/lib/client/api/unwrap.ts` — **D346** (`decisions/frontend/architecture.md`); and `astro check --minimumFailingSeverity hint` on a deprecated `toThrowError`, now `toThrow`. (4) `schema-view-drift.test.ts` failed on both of `0041`'s views against the first real introspection of them: Postgres stores `count(*)` as `count(*) AS count` and drops the relation qualifier in a single-relation select list. `pg_get_viewdef` confirmed both are Postgres's own echo, and `normalize` now undoes them, the qualifier strip scoped to JOIN-less bodies — **D345** (`decisions/testing.md`). Canonical edits: `05-Database/03-Migrations.md` (§0041's `day_count` cast; the "never executed against a live database" paragraph replaced by the verification run), `05-Database/06-Spec/02-Template-Layer.md` (same cast), `00-File-Inventory.md` (new `client/api/unwrap.ts` row), root `CLAUDE.md`. Verification: `database/verification/0041_training_schedule_checks.sql` ran against the live dev branch for the first time — ALL 9 CHECKS PASSED, closing the open item D342 recorded; `day_count` confirmed `integer` in `pg_attribute`. `npm run validate:app` exits 0 end to end (41 applied / 0 pending, drift clean, introspect, fallow gate, 4859 tests across 261 files, `check` 0 errors / 0 warnings / 0 hints); `refresh-graph.sh` warns that the `graphify` CLI is absent — CI-owned, per root `CLAUDE.md`. All 20 `run-all-gates` scripts pass.)

> **Version:** 1.108.0 (2026-09-20 — routine-step-kind-generalisation: closes Phase 2 of `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-18-routine-step-kind-generalisation.md` (Tasks 1-4 landed in prior sessions on this branch; this entry is Task 5, the docs/decisions/gates close-out). Any routine-eligible game can now be a routine step, pinned to a game ruleset version on its **template** (migration `0040`, renumbered from the plan's own `0039` — main had already taken that number for `x01-checkout-percentage`), started from the step's own data instead of a hardcoded TUOD constant, and rendered through a per-kind client adapter. Migration `0040`: `exercise_templates.game_ruleset_version_id` (composite FK on `(game_type_id, id)` mirroring `0035`, `NOT VALID` pair CHECK — deploy order means seed `0021`'s Finishing backfill runs after it, issue #378); both routine views gain `game_ruleset_version_key`. Seed `0021` backfills Finishing to `TUOD_V1`; seed `0022` adds "Score Training (timed)" (`SCORE_TRAINING_V1`) and "121 (timed)" (game type `ONE_TWENTY_ONE`'s `121_V2` — the plan's own seed SQL named the wrong lookup column, `'121'` the display name rather than `ONE_TWENTY_ONE` the `implementation_key`; corrected in the committed file, not as written). Server: `services/routines/game-step.ts`'s `ROUTINE_GAME_STEPS` eligibility table (`TUOD_V1`/`SCORE_TRAINING_V1`/`121_V2`) and `routineGameStepHook()` replace the hardcoded TUOD path in `training-session.service.ts`/`routine.service.ts` — this is exactly what closes issue #508 (a `discovered-work` finding filed against Phase 1's hardcoding). Client: a `StepAdapter` registry under `lib/training/routines/adapters/` (`STEP_ADAPTERS`, `resolveStepAdapter`, `stepAdapterKey`) replaces `routine-play.data.ts`'s four engine-typed fields and inline per-kind branching; `finishing-step.data.ts` generalises to `game-step.data.ts`'s `gameStep()`; the play page switches panels on `adapter.panel`, reusing the existing standalone Score Training/121 interface components rather than adding new ones (`08-Component-Inventory.md` needs no new row). Three new decisions: **D339** (`decisions/database.md`) the template-pin schema shape and the `NOT VALID`/follow-up-`VALIDATE` split; **D340** (`decisions/game-engine.md`) a routine step's timer is the game's own timed-mode clock, and eligibility is a hand-mirrored server/client table pair cross-checked only by `step-adapter.registry.test.ts`; **D341** (`decisions/frontend/architecture.md`) the adapter registry lives under `lib/`, not `modules/`, because it wraps play factories that import `@client/api`. Canonical edits: `05-Database/06-Spec/02-Template-Layer.md` (new column/FK/CHECK; corrected the false "pins … on its session" sentence — it pins on the template, copies onto the session), `05-Database/05-Views/00-Overview.md`, `05-Database/03-Migrations.md` (new `## 0040` section), `09-Training/01-Routines.md` §11 (delegation boundary + eligibility rule, closing the section's own long-open "should be specified when implemented" placeholder), §17 (Implemented paragraph), §26 (cross-reference), `07-Frontend/04-Modules-And-OOP.md` (registry row in the OOP Boundary table, 0.3.0 → 0.4.0), chain range `0001`–`0039` → `0001`–`0040` in root `CLAUDE.md`, `00-Context-Map.md`, `10-Database-Agent-Guide.md`, `database/CLAUDE.md`, `docs/CLAUDE.md`, `11-Neon-Integration.md`, `07-Frontend/09-Adding-A-Game.md`, `docs/architecture/README.md` (seed range `0001`–`0020` → `0001`–`0022` alongside it), `00-File-Inventory.md` (new "Routine step-kind generalisation" section for the eight new app files, three new SQL rows, migration/03-Migrations.md row text). Issue #508 should be cited as "Closes #508" on this branch's eventual PR — not closed here, since nothing has merged yet. Issue #517 (builder's step-minutes input allows 1-60 for a GAME step whose real bound is 3-30) stays open, confirmed still accurate, nothing further to do. > **Version:** 1.107.0 (2026-09-19 — x01-checkout-percentage: closes the double-out/checkout-accuracy correction across 501, TUOD and 121 under `VISUAL_BOARD` capture, superseding the 2026-09-05 design (D256). Three defects fixed: (1) `double-attempt.module.ts`'s `classifyDart` now counts a bounce-out/off-board dart and any bust-causing dart as a **miss** rather than excluding it, running the bust test ahead of the sector/reroute-exclusion rules, and treats the inner single band at 50 remaining as a missed bull while the outer band and the trebles there stay excluded setup shots — the ten reference darts in the design spec are each a named test case; (2) `checkout-visits.module.ts` is now the one shared per-visit-remaining builder for 501/TUOD/121, replacing three private builders inside the `*-play.data.ts` files, and `modules/stats/x01-checkout-sessions.module.ts`'s `checkoutVisitsFromRows` calls the same builder for the career read, so the live modals and the career stat can never disagree; (3) the persisted view backing the career read, `v_double_out_checkout_darts` (migration `0024`), is dropped and replaced by `v_x01_checkout_darts` (migration `0039`), which drops the view's own `SUM(d.score)` running total — wrong the instant a bust zeroes a leg's counted total while its darts keep their real board scores — and projects facts only, letting TUOD and 121 join the career stat for the first time (their ladder fold is game-engine logic `05-Views.md` forbids in SQL, so it now runs in the app read layer instead). The field this feeds is renamed `checkoutPercentage` end to end — `StatisticsOverviewResponse` (`06-API/04-Endpoint-Contracts.md` → 1.8.0), the three seat results, `stats.store.ts`/the routine summary read, and the `Checkout %` UI label — computed as `hits / (hits + misses)` over every dart across the whole session, rendered through a new `checkoutPercentageDisplay()` helper (Pattern 20) that dashes the zero-attempt case rather than `accuracyDisplay()`'s `"0.00%"`. New **D338** (`decisions/game-engine.md`, supersedes D256). Canonical edits: `06-Spec/05-Read-Model-Layer.md`, `06-API/04-Endpoint-Contracts.md`, `04-Architecture-patterns.md` (Patterns 18/20), `05-Views/00-Overview.md`. Merged `origin/main` rather than rebasing 19 commits: conflicts in `CLAUDE.md`, `database/CLAUDE.md`, `docs/CLAUDE.md`, `00-Context-Map.md`, `00-Context-Map-History.md`, `00-File-Inventory.md`, `database/README.md`, `decisions/game-engine.md`, `06-API/00-Overview.md`, `06-API/04-Endpoint-Contracts.md`, `07-Frontend/09-Adding-A-Game.md` and three app files, all resolved by keeping both sides. Two collisions with main followed: this branch's migration `0038` was renumbered `0039` (main issued its own `0038_custom_routines`) and its decision `D337` renumbered `D338` (main issued its own `D337` in `decisions/frontend/alpine.md`); `scripts/renumber-decision.sh` refuses the two-declaration post-merge state, so the seven citations were rewritten by hand. Both migrations were then applied to Neon `dev` and left applied: `db:drift` reports 39 applied matching the chain and 13 live `v_*` views matching it; `db:introspect`'s regeneration added and dropped no export and its six changed bodies were all column-order/constraint-rendering drift unrelated to `0038`/`0039`, so `schema.ts` was reverted. `0039_x01_checkout_darts_view_checks.sql` 9/9 and `0036_read_model_view_consumers_checks.sql` 8/8 passed on their first ever execution. Full suite 243 files/4692 tests; `npm run check` 0 errors/0 warnings/0 hints; `npm run format:check` clean; all 21 `scripts/check-*.sh` and the fallow gate pass. Full design: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-19-x01-checkout-percentage-design.md`; implementation plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-19-x01-checkout-percentage.md`. Validation: see the completion report.)

> **Version:** 1.106.0 (2026-09-19 — custom-routine-builder-whole-branch-fix: the single fix wave from the final whole-branch review before the PR — 2 blockers, 2 worth-taking-now. BLOCKER 1: `fn_routine_templates_duration_bounds()` read `COALESCE(NEW.routine_template_id, OLD.routine_template_id)`, but `NEW` is *unassigned* (not NULL) in a PL/pgSQL row trigger on `DELETE`, so it raised `42804` before `COALESCE` reached `OLD` — breaking `PUT /api/routines/:id` (whose `replaceRoutine` deletes the routine's steps inside its transaction), `DELETE /api/routines/:id` (cascade to `routine_steps`) and player deletion, each as an unmatched-`23514` rethrow to a 500 with the routine unchanged. The function now branches on `TG_OP`/`TG_TABLE_NAME` — `OLD.routine_template_id` on a step `DELETE`, `NEW.routine_template_id` on `INSERT`, both parents on a re-parenting `UPDATE`, `NEW.id`/`OLD.id` on `routine_templates` — and loops over the resulting id array, so the parent-is-gone early exit still fires when a template and its steps go in one transaction. `0038` was edited in place, not superseded by a `0039`: it is committed but applied to no database (D336/D193) and the invariant covers `0001`–`0037`. `database/verification/0038_custom_routine_checks.sql`'s check 8 (the only `DELETE` case) caught `check_violation` alone, so the `42804` escaped and aborted the whole script instead of reporting a FAIL — now `WHEN OTHERS`, reporting `SQLSTATE: SQLERRM`. New **D335** (`decisions/database.md`, append-only; D336 and D334 untouched). BLOCKER 2: `npx fallow`, a required CI gate (`quality.yml` Stale-usage gate), exited 1 on this branch's own files — `MAX_BUILDER_STEPS` and `DEFAULT_STEP_MINUTES` were exported from `routine-builder.data.ts` with no consumer outside the module (`export` dropped, both stay module-local), and `app/src/lib/client/api/types.ts` carried a stale `// fallow-ignore-next-line unused-type` for `RoutineSummaryData`, which Task 8 gave real consumers (suppression removed). ITEM 3: `chk_routine_templates_player_ownership` (`0038`) was not mirrored in `app/src/db/schema.ts` — `schema-view-drift.test.ts` compares views only and `check-constraint-mirror.sh` covers only `darts`/`exercise_stages`/`turns`, so nothing caught it; added to the `routineTemplates` constraint block. ITEM 4: the 30/60 bound lives in `routine-duration.module.ts` and in `0038`'s trigger with nothing comparing them — new static test `app/tests/db/routine-duration-bound-drift.test.ts` (the `schema-view-drift.test.ts` shape) asserts `0038`'s `IF v_total < 30 OR v_total > 60` matches `MIN_USER_ROUTINE_MINUTES`/`MAX_ROUTINE_MINUTES`, and that the raised `CONSTRAINT = 'trg_routine_templates_duration_bounds'` matches `routine.service.ts`'s `DURATION_BOUND_TRIGGER`, narrowing the silent coupling D334 flagged. `routine-builder.data.test.ts` gains one case pinning the module's export surface to `["routineBuilder"]` — `check-test-coverage.sh` correctly refused a source edit with no covering-test edit, and the export surface is what actually changed (both behaviours the constants encode were already covered through the factory). Docs: `05-Database/03-Migrations.md` §0038's trigger paragraph restated (its "reads only `NEW.routine_template_id`" sentence had become false); `05-Database/10-Database-Agent-Guide.md` §6 gains the `TG_OP` row-selection rule, 1.4.0 → 1.5.0. Nothing claims `0038` is applied or that the verification script has been run — neither is true. Validation: `npm test` 4154/4154 across 240 files under Node 22.23.2 (Node 18 exits 0 while vitest dies on `node:util`'s missing `styleText` — a silently-green false pass, confirmed by printing `node --version` in the same shell); `npm run check` 0 errors / 0 warnings / 0 hints across 638 files; `npm run build` succeeded (`dist` removed after); `npx fallow` exit 0, 0 above threshold, 7469 analyzed; `npm run format`/`format:check` clean; all `run-all-gates` "Always run" + `app/` + `decisions/` scripts OK. `db:status`/`db:migrate`/`db:introspect`/`db:verify` not run — no `DATABASE_URL` in this session (D193). Work done in the `.worktrees/custom-routine-builder` worktree on branch `feat/custom-routine-builder`; no push, no PR, per explicit instruction.)

> **Version:** 1.103.0 (2026-09-19 — custom-routine-builder-task-1-fix-round-1: code-review fix round 1/5 on 1.102.0's task, spec ✅ / quality not-approved, four findings. (1) CRITICAL: `database/verification/0038_custom_routine_checks.sql`'s helper used `CREATE TEMP FUNCTION IF NOT EXISTS verify_routine(...)`, BigQuery syntax with no Postgres equivalent — `psql` would have errored there and aborted the transaction, so the script could never emit a single PASS. Rewritten as the Postgres idiom `CREATE FUNCTION pg_temp.verify_routine(...)`, called as `pg_temp.verify_routine(...)`; no other non-Postgres syntax found on a full re-read. (2) `SET CONSTRAINTS ... IMMEDIATE` issued inside a sub-block that succeeds is not rolled back and persists for the rest of the transaction: case 4 (a 30-minute routine, expected to pass) left it IMMEDIATE, so case 5's bare `routine_templates` INSERT — which runs before its step exists — tripped the trigger at 0 minutes and case 5 recorded a false FAIL. Every case (2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 8) now resets to `SET CONSTRAINTS ... DEFERRED` as its first statement, independent of what any prior case left behind. (3) Check 10 asserted `count(*) = 4 AND bool_and(has_default_configuration)` over the whole `v_exercise_template_catalog`, but the view is deliberately broad (`et.is_system_template AND ext.is_published`, no `has_default_configuration` filter — narrowing to buildable templates is Task 4's `listExerciseTemplates` service-layer job, not the view's) and seed `0002`'s four `0198f200-*` GAME templates are system templates with no default configuration, so a seeded database returns 8 rows, 4 of them FALSE — the check's own expectation was wrong, the view was correct and is unchanged. Split into two checks: 10 asserts the four `0199b000-*` routine-composable templates are present and all TRUE; 11 asserts the view still surfaces a template with FALSE rather than filtering it out. Verification script check count 10 → 11 everywhere it's cited outside the append-only decision ledger (`database/README.md`, `05-Database/03-Migrations.md`); **D336**'s own "(10 checks)" text is left as written — decisions are append-only provenance, matching D305's/D321's own precedent of a Consequences line going stated-stale rather than being edited. (4) Three spec docs still described migration 0038's trigger as unbuilt: `06-Spec/02-Template-Layer.md`'s routine_steps Design Rationale ("Planned (unbuilt)") rewritten to "Built (migration `0038`, 2026-09-19)" with the actual trigger/constraint names and the real `v_routine_execution` column names (`routine_description`/`exercise_description`, not the placeholder `description` the pre-implementation text guessed); `05-Views/00-Overview.md`'s Implemented Views range bumped `0009`–`0036` → `0009`–`0038`, the `v_routine_execution` row gained its `0038` columns, `v_exercise_template_catalog` gained its own row (doc version 1.4.0 → 1.5.0); `06-Spec/05-Read-Model-Layer.md`'s `v_routine_execution` Exposes paragraph gained the three `0038` columns and a new `# v_exercise_template_catalog` section was added in full (Category/Purpose/Sources/Exposes/Design Rationale), stating explicitly that excluding a no-default template is the service's job, per the repo owner's ruling that the view must not change. `05-Database/03-Migrations.md`'s own `0038` section corrected from present-tense "proves ... against a live database" to D336's accurate "is what proves … once a human runs it" (the script has never been executed — no `DATABASE_URL` in this environment). All four spec/doc edits dated 2026-09-19 in their front-matter. No new **D**-numbered decision: these are corrections to what D305/D306/D321/D336 already decided, not a new design choice. `app/src/db/schema.ts` untouched this round — no finding named it. Validation: `cd app && npm test -- tests/scripts/verify-db.test.ts tests/db/schema-view-drift.test.ts` 96/96 (via `nvm exec 22.23.2`, this container's default Node 18 cannot run Vitest 4); full suite re-run clean; `npm run format`/`format:check` clean; `check-doc-links.sh`, `check-context-map.sh`, `check-context-budget.sh`, `check-agent-mirrors.sh`, `check-doc-sync.sh` (no migration/service/module file touched this round, so it reports no triggered path) all pass. `database/verification/0038_custom_routine_checks.sql` still not executed against a live database — no `DATABASE_URL` in this environment; its own correctness now rests on this round's manual trace of the deferred-constraint leak plus static structural checks (`verify-db.test.ts`: starts `BEGIN;`, ends `ROLLBACK;`, resolves lookups by `implementation_key`, registered in `database/README.md`). Work done in the `.worktrees/custom-routine-builder` worktree on branch `feat/custom-routine-builder`; no PR opened, per explicit instruction.)

> **Version:** 1.96.0 (2026-09-19 — scoreboard-band: closes issue #484, filed from the #280 pass in the same branch on the user's instruction. The third turned out not to be a third height — `SplitScoreboard`'s root is `flex-1 min-h-0`, so `h-2/5` was inert for exactly D326's reason, and the two-seat scoreboard has been filling the play area rather than the 40% its author wrote in 2026-08-22. #484's own body said `h-2/5` was a hard 40%; that claim is corrected on the issue and here. The surviving spelling becomes the rule and moves into the shells: `SplitScoreboard.astro` always applies `min-h-2/5 max-h-2/5`, `SinglePlayerDisplay.astro` applies it behind a new `pinnedHeight` prop (default `true`) that only `SplitScoreboardHalf.astro` turns off, and all 21 call sites pass no height class at all. A `global.css` primitive was the rejected alternative — it would have kept 21 repetitions under a nicer name. Two-seat screens change visibly; the 501 column's fit at 390×844 is arithmetic (~292px band, ~55px headroom), not a measurement, so an on-device look is owed. New **D327** (`decisions/frontend/style.md`); style guide 0.2.4 → 0.2.5; `08-Component-Inventory.md` rows for both shells. Validation: see the completion report.)

> **Version:** 1.95.0 (2026-09-19 — flex-item-sizing: closes issue #280 (migrated `FINDINGS.md` F15), which asked whether the `max-h-2/5 h-full` pair every game interface passes to `SinglePlayerDisplay` could simply lose its `h-full`. It can. `SinglePlayerDisplay`'s root carries `flex-1 min-h-0` inside a `flex flex-col` parent, so `flex-basis: 0%` supplies the item's main size and its own `height: 100%` is never consulted; the percentage chain is definite the whole way up (`html`/`body` are `h-dvh max-h-dvh`, D174), so there is no indefinite-height fallback case either. Twelve call sites drop the class — the nine interfaces the issue names plus `QuickSubtract.astro`, `DoublePatternPanel.astro` and `SwitchingPanel.astro`, which repeat the same pair and would have left the pattern alive. The `h-full` inside `SinglePlayerDisplay.astro` itself stays: that inner card div's parent is a plain block box. Folding the clamp into the component was rejected — `SplitScoreboardHalf.astro` renders the same component at `size="sm"`, where a 40% cap must not apply. The reasoning is spec-level and unverified on device: no WebKit is available here and the one Chromium build present (MDM-managed Edge) crashes in headless mode, so the repro that would have measured the box could not be run — the same limitation #280 recorded. `07-Frontend/07-Style-Guide.md` gains the general rule (0.2.3 → 0.2.4). New **D326** (`decisions/frontend/style.md`). `.astro` markup is not unit-tested (D101), so no test accompanies the change and `check-test-coverage.sh` passes for that reason. Validation: see the completion report.)

> **Version:** 1.94.0 (2026-09-19 — checkout-hint-chips: closes issue #248, "the checkout hint for 152 returns D16". Root cause is not the chart — `checkout-path.module.ts` holds `152: ["T20","T20","D16"]` and has since it was written — but the two-seat chip row in `SplitScoreboardHalf.astro`, which keyed its `x-for` by the chip label. Alpine keeps one node per key, so the repeated `T20` was dropped and 152 rendered as two chips. Reproduced with Alpine under jsdom before the fix (`T20D16`) and after (`T20T20D16`); the single-seat path was never affected, since `SinglePlayerDisplay` renders the whole hint string through `x-text`. Fix is the key: `(part, index)` keyed by `index`. `07-Frontend/03-Alpine-Patterns.md` gains the general rule under Binding Rules — a `:key` may never be the rendered value. No decision block: a uniqueness requirement of the framework is a fact, not a choice. `.astro` markup is not unit-tested (D101), so no test accompanies the component change; `check-test-coverage.sh` passes for the same reason. Validation: see the completion report.)

Root-caused rather than patched: a `./path` permission rule resolves against the session's current directory, so the deny bites only while the session sits in the main copy; `EnterWorktree({path})` moves the session into the worktree, where the pattern names a directory that does not exist. Live probe on Claude Code 2.1.267: from the repo root, `Read` and Bash `head` on `.worktrees/worktree-read-deny/CLAUDE.md` both denied; after entering, both allowed on the same file; after `ExitWorktree`, both denied again. > **Version:** 1.89.0 (2026-09-18 — retire-legacy-worktree-path: D312 moved worktrees to `.worktrees/` but grandfathered the old `.claude/worktrees/` "until the pre-D312 worktrees land". The last of them, `p5-decision-id`, landed with PR #451, so the clause's precondition is spent and this branch retires the path: the `.claude/worktrees/` line and its comment leave `.gitignore`, root `CLAUDE.md`'s worktree invariant drops the grandfather sentence, and `check-doc-links.sh`'s comment about both prefixes being gitignored is corrected to one. New `scripts/check-worktree-location.sh` replaces prose with a gate — it fails when a worktree inside the repo root sits anywhere but `.worktrees/`, and when the retired directory exists at all. Scoped deliberately: a checkout *beside* the repo (`dart-analytics-superpowers-plugin`, live on `claude/superpowers-plugin-migration`) is a separate working copy the plugin never claimed, so failing on it would have made the gate red on day one for something this rule was never about. A third check warns, non-fatally, when a `.worktrees/` worktree holds a branch already merged into `origin/main` — the prune signal D312's Consequences paragraph asked for, left advisory because worktrees are shared across concurrent sessions and another session's landed branch must not fail your commit. Proven in both directions before wiring: green on the compliant tree, then two `FAIL:` lines against a deliberately-created `.claude/worktrees/probe`, then green again after removal. One false positive was found and fixed during that probe — a freshly created branch still sitting on `origin/main`'s tip is trivially "merged" and warned about itself, so the advisory now skips a branch whose tip has not moved. Wired into `run-all-gates` §Always run, `quality.yml`'s structure job and `.husky/pre-commit`. `.claude/settings.json` is deliberately untouched: the `Read(./.claude/worktrees/**/*)` deny and the `claudeMdExcludes` entry stay as cheap insurance, because `EnterWorktree`'s `name` argument still hardcodes that path and can recreate it in the window before a gate runs. Root `CLAUDE.md` gains the compliant recipe as a new invariant — `git worktree add .worktrees/<name> -b <branch> origin/main` then `EnterWorktree` by `path` — which also corrects a belief carried into this branch: `.worktrees/` is *not* unreachable from a session, only `EnterWorktree({name})` is constrained, and this branch's own worktree was created that way as the proof. New **D319** (`decisions/context-system.md`). Validation: see the completion report.)

Re-confirmed on this branch's `main`: the three wake-lock merges have since aged out of the 80-merge window, so the confirmation run was widened to 100 and 120 merges, where the untuned patterns reproduce all three false positives and the tuned trigger holds zero at every window. `docs/superpowers/` deliberately does not satisfy the gate — non-canonical (D312), ranking below the code it describes. The `Stop` output contract was settled by live probe rather than from `strings`: both `{"decision":"block","reason":...}` and `hookSpecificOutput.additionalContext` are honoured on `Stop` in 2.1.236, and the former ships because its feedback arrives labelled `Stop hook feedback:`. > **Version:** 1.83.0 (2026-09-18 — remove-forked-skills, part 2: turns D311's prose overrides into *pairings* and moves the repo where the plugin was right. Root `CLAUDE.md`'s §Superpowers Skills loses the six-line branch-finish prose block and gains a four-row table naming, for each plugin skill that this repo deviates from, the file carrying the delta: `superpowers:finishing-a-development-branch` → the new `.claude/skills/finishing-a-dart-branch` skill; `superpowers:test-driven-development` → `app/CLAUDE.md` §Test-Driven Development; `superpowers:verification-before-completion` → `run-all-gates`; `superpowers:executing-plans`/`subagent-driven-development` → `capturing-discovered-work`. The branch-finish rule became a skill because prose is the weakest coupling available for it — the plugin skill says "Present the menu exactly as written" and "Integration is your human partner's decision", emphatic text arriving long after the line meant to override it; a paired skill fires at the same moment and costs one description line until invoked. It carries the delta only (Step 4 skipped, Option 2 every time, `run-all-gates` standing in for "the test suite", Step 6 unchanged), so unlike the deleted forks there is no upstream text to drift, and its distinct name shadows nothing. `app/CLAUDE.md`'s TDD section was also re-paired: it cited `superpowers:verification-before-completion` when the plugin ships a `test-driven-development` skill that is the actual counterpart. Two repo rules move to meet the plugin instead. Worktrees relocate from `.claude/worktrees/` to `.worktrees/` (root `CLAUDE.md` invariant, `app/CLAUDE.md`'s restatement, `.gitignore` — which keeps both paths while pre-D312 worktrees are live): the plugin's Step 6 claims cleanup only for `.worktrees/` or `worktrees/` and leaves anything else to "the host environment", so D293's path meant the plugin never removed a worktree it made while the invariant called one left behind a defect — six were live, four stale. And `superpowers:brainstorming` now runs unmodified, writing *and committing* its design doc to `docs/superpowers/specs/`, the single named exception to the "do not commit unless the user asks" invariant; `00-Context-Map.md` declares that tree non-canonical on the same terms as `docs/game-rules/`, so no `status:` front matter or per-spec inventory row is owed — the tree already has one row, marked historical. The discovered-work flow is explicitly preserved, not relaxed: incidental findings still become GitHub issues and are still never fixed in the same pass. Recorded as D312 in `decisions/context-system.md` (41 decisions), with the new skill added to `00-File-Inventory.md`. `validate:app` does not apply — no `app/` source changed.)

New **D306** (`decisions/api.md`): four planned write endpoints (`GET /api/exercise-templates`, `POST`/`PUT`/`DELETE /api/routines*`) alongside D299's existing unbuilt reads, `PUT` taking the full ordered step array rather than a partial-reorder patch. `09-training-routines.md` §7 gains the floor note and §20 a "Status (2026-09-17)" pointer to the roadmap and both decisions; `05-Database/06-Spec/02-Template-Layer.md` notes the planned trigger under `routine_steps`; `06-API/04-Endpoint-Contracts.md` gains the `GET /api/exercise-templates` read row, a Custom Routine Write Contracts section, and `CreateRoutineRequest`/`UpdateRoutineRequest`/`RoutineStepInput` DTO sketches; `07-Frontend/00-Overview.md` points at the planned builder and flags `@alpinejs/sort` as a proposed, not-yet-adopted dependency. Phases 3 (named, swappable weekly schedules) and 4 (copy-on-share sharing) are deliberately not numbered decisions — both have open questions the spec leaves for their own future brainstorm — and are instead appended to `DECISIONS.md`'s Deferred list. `00-File-Inventory.md`'s four touched rows got their `~Tokens` values refreshed from `scripts/check-context-budget.sh`'s own `chars/4/1000` formula (7.1k/2.3k/8.9k/3.6k) rather than estimated, plus a one-line summary each. Every inserted sentence marks the work "planned"/"unbuilt" — no schema migration, API route, or frontend component was written. Validation: `check-context-map.sh`, `check-doc-links.sh`, `check-context-budget.sh`, `check-decision-ids.sh` all pass. Work done on branch `claude/configurable-training-routines-8fw5fl`.)

> **Version:** 1.82.0 (2026-09-17 — p1-training-taxonomy: closes issues #297 and #348, plan P1 of the technical-debt sweep. #297: D265 retired "trivia" as a UI category — Quick Subtract became a flat card under `/training`, a sibling of Balanced Training — but only the page routes moved, leaving three source folders named after an information architecture that no longer existed. `lib/`, `modules/` and `components/layout/` now each carry one `training/` domain with three children — `trivia/`, `exercises/`, `routines/` — `app/tests/` mirrors `app/src/` path-for-path, `docs/architecture/09-training-routines.md` and `10-trivia.md` become `09-Training/01-Routines.md` and `09-Training/02-Trivia.md` under a new `09-Training/00-Overview.md`, and `docs/game-rules/{trivia,routines}/` move to `docs/game-rules/training/{trivia,routines}/`. Deliberately not moved, and D308 records why: `components/layout/games/` (games are not training), `services/exercise-rulesets/` (Worker side of the Worker/browser boundary, never a mirrored pair with `lib/training/exercises/`, and "exercise" is not a retired name) and `docs/game-rules/rulesets/`. The one structural change beyond renaming: the type-barrel chain gained a level, because `scripts/check-type-barrels.sh` rule 2 lets a barrel raise only a direct child and never a grandchild — so `lib/training/types.ts` and `modules/training/{types,interfaces}.ts` became pure barrels and today's declarations sank into `training/routines/`. New **D308** (`decisions/frontend/architecture.md`). #348: the `@forms` alias mapped to `app/src/forms/`, which never existed — no `*.form.ts` file, no importer, a resolve to a missing directory in both `app/tsconfig.json` and `app/vitest.config.ts` — yet the layer was documented as a non-negotiable convention across `app/CLAUDE.md` and seven architecture docs, including `07-Frontend/03-Alpine-Patterns.md`'s `# Form Pattern` section in full. Alias entries, the `.form.ts` suffix convention, its `$persist` permission and every remaining prose, table and diagram description deleted; D77, D84 and D86 left untouched, the ledger being append-only. New **D307** (`decisions/frontend/alpine.md`). Both decisions were renumbered as `main` claimed ids mid-flight — three times for the alpine one (D300→D302→D305→**D307**) and twice for the architecture one (D303→D306→**D308**) — the branch having fallen 12 commits behind, been merged up once, and finally been rebased onto `origin/main` at `0c1166b`, which dropped that merge, replayed the eight linear commits and resurfaced its conflicts: three textual doc conflicts against #411's frontend-doc reconciliation and four semantic ones where `main` had landed work at paths this branch had already moved: `lib/training/routine-start-error.ts` and its test (#403, re-landed by #412) and `tests/lib/training/finishing-step-seam.test.ts` (#415), all three relocated into `training/routines/`, plus `07-Frontend/06-Test-Strategy.md`'s new wrapped-store section (D304), whose four path citations named the pre-move locations and were repointed. The rebase added a fifth collision of its own: #417 edited `09-training-routines.md` inside the same range this branch renames it, so its §7 User-Created Routine Floor note and §20 Status (2026-09-17) block were carried across into `09-Training/01-Routines.md` by the rename, and its own **D305** (`decisions/database.md`, duration floor) and **D306** (`decisions/api.md`, routine write contract) are the ids that pushed this branch's pair to D307/D308. Plan gaps execution found, recorded rather than passed over: the plan's citation sweep ran `grep --include="*.md" --include="*.jsonc"`, which never scans `.ts`, so 19 TSDoc citations of the deleted `09-training-routines.md` survived in 14 files under `app/src/` — rewritten to `09-Training/01-Routines.md` here, comment text only, no code line touched; `00-File-Inventory.md`'s `decisions/frontend/alpine.md` row was never refreshed for D307 while its sibling row was for D308, so `grep -rn "D307"` found only the decision's own heading and the escalation index could not route to it — row updated, budget recomputed ~9.3k→~9.7k against the file's real 38,917 characters; and `09-Training/02-Trivia.md` still cited `01-Principles.md` bare after moving a level deeper, resolving one directory too high — now `../01-Principles.md`, matching the `../08-DartBot.md` the same edit had already fixed. The D224 coverage gate has nothing to trip on for the comment sweep, which is worth stating because it looks as though it should: `scripts/check_test_coverage.py` puts a changed source in scope only when `runtime_shape()` differs before and after, and that function strips comments first, so all 14 files fall out of scope — the two confirmation comments this branch already carries (`app/tests/lib/training/routines/balanced-training-play.data.test.ts`, `app/tests/services/exercise-rulesets/registry.test.ts`) were required because those sources *moved*, which the gate does see. Verified on the fix wave: `astro check` 604 files 0 errors/0 warnings/0 hints, Vitest 226 files / 4032 tests, `npx fallow` clean, `format:check` clean, and the doc/structure gates green. No `app/` runtime behaviour, database schema or migration changed, so `validate:app`'s `db:status`/`db:migrate`/`db:introspect` steps do not apply — there is no `DATABASE_URL` in this environment (D193). Work done in a worktree on branch `refactor/p1-training-taxonomy`.)

> **Version:** 1.83.0 (2026-09-18 — p2-play-context-dedup: closes issues #281 and #282, plan P2 of the technical-debt sweep. #281: `PlayLifecycleContext<TConfig, TEngine, TResults>` already existed in `app/src/lib/game/types.ts`, and all nine `*PlayContext` types restated its 17 members by hand — nine independent copies free to drift. Each is now `PlayLifecycleContext<…> & { per-game members }` — 45 insertions against 141 deletions in one file across two commits, one hunk per type. `init` and `uploadAndCompleteSession` deliberately stay in the per-game half, because the shared type declares them without a `this` parameter and the concrete contexts bind `this` so Alpine's directive-driven calls typecheck — the intersection of the two signatures is what every current call site already relies on. `TuodPlayContext`, `FiveOhOnePlayContext` and `OneTwentyOnePlayContext` likewise keep their own `$store`, which carries `checkoutHints?: CheckoutHintsStoreContext`; TypeScript intersects it with the shared one and the narrower type wins. The three union config arguments (`OneTwentyOneSnapshot | OneTwentyOneV2Snapshot`, `SinglesSnapshot | SinglesV2Snapshot`, `ShanghaiSnapshot | ShanghaiV2Snapshot`) were preserved arm-for-arm. No context had to stay hand-written. #282: a function-by-function comparison of `score-training-play.data.ts` against `tuod-play.data.ts` — not a percentage — decided what was extractable. Three helpers were byte-identical (`findBotSeat`, `botDartIndex`, `formatRemaining`), three differed only in type annotations (`startCountdown`, `maybeResumeCountdown`, `resumeEngine`), and three differ behaviourally (`throwOneDart`, `throwBotDart`, `statsFor` — Score Training's bot always fires treble 20 via `chooseTarget()`, TUOD's aims at the seat's own `currentTarget` and routes stats through `tuodCheckoutVisits`). The first six moved into new `app/src/lib/game/play-bot-seat.ts` and `app/src/lib/game/play-countdown.ts` plus `resumeGameEngine` in `app/src/lib/game/play-lifecycle.ts`; the last three stay duplicated on purpose, and D309 records why. Every moved body was verified raw-byte-identical against **both** origins, brace-matched, not whitespace-normalised. Two structural changes execution forced, neither in the plan: `scripts/check-type-barrels.sh` rejects an implementation file declaring an exported type, so `DartbotSeat`, `CountdownGame`, `ExpirableEngine` and `CountdownConfig` were relocated into `app/src/lib/game/types.ts` (+23/−0, purely additive); and `CountdownGame`'s four fields are optional rather than required, because `PlayStoreContext.game` declares them optional and required fields would make the structural constraint unsatisfiable by the real store — `!= null` already handled `undefined`, so no behaviour moved. Deleting the six helpers orphaned the `SeatFact` import and the local `ScoreTrainingConfig`/`TuodConfig` aliases, whose `ts(6196)` hints fail the repo's 0-hint bar; all three removed. The generic `resumeGameEngine` needed no cast — `as any`, `as unknown as`, `@ts-expect-error` and `@ts-ignore` are all absent from the branch, the registry's `create` parameter being `unknown` already. Measured rather than assumed, which was the point: `npx fallow` went 5,170 duplicated lines (13.4%, 63 files) → 5,139 (13.4%, 66 files), while the clone family spanning `one-twenty-one-play.data.ts`, `score-training-play.data.ts` and `tuod-play.data.ts` fell from 6 groups / 190 lines to 4 groups / 102 lines. The percentage is unchanged to one decimal because the extracted helpers are small against a 38k-line corpus; the plan named that outcome in advance and declined to chase the metric by fusing two rulesets that genuinely differ. New **D309** (`decisions/frontend/architecture.md`), no renumbering needed — 308 was the maximum on both `origin/main` and the branch at derivation time. `one-twenty-one-play.data.ts` was held out of scope throughout despite carrying its own `maybeResumeCountdown`, and the extraction gave `npx fallow` a new family to report — 2 groups / 28 lines between that file and the new `app/src/lib/game/play-countdown.ts`. Holding it out was deliberate (the repo forbids fixing discovered work in the same pass) but the residue is real and is named here rather than left for the next reader to rediscover; captured as `discovered-work` issue #422 rather than fixed in the same pass. Verified: `astro check` 608 files 0 errors/0 warnings/0 hints, Vitest 228 files / 4047 tests, `npx fallow` exit 0, and the doc/structure gates green. No `app/` runtime behaviour, database schema or migration changed, so `validate:app`'s `db:status`/`db:migrate`/`db:introspect` steps do not apply — there is no `DATABASE_URL` in this environment (D193). Work done in a worktree on branch `refactor/p2-play-context-dedup`.)

> **Version:** 1.87.0 (2026-09-18 — p4-dartbot-bias-flatspots: closes issue #286, plan P4 of the technical-debt sweep, task 3 of 3 (docs, decision and context registration; tasks 1 and 2 shipped the derivation script and the table edit). `LEVEL_SKILL_TABLE` levels 7/8, 9/10 and 12/13 each carried an exact, shared `biasXMm`/`biasYMm` pair inherited from the pre-D-L table — both D-L's power-law rescale and D-N's log-space interpolation operate on pre-edit per-level ratios, so a pair identical before an edit stayed identical after it, and two refits carried the duplication forward unnoticed because nothing asserted anything about bias across levels. Task 1 (`8686458f`) added `app/scripts/dartbot-bias-flatspot-fix.ts`, exporting `interiorPoints()` (geometric interpolation between two bracket values), `SPOTS` (the six flat spots and their nearest distinct bracket levels — `biasXMm`'s 7/8 pair brackets against level 5 rather than level 6, since level 6's measured `-5.0` is a sign anomaly, not a curve point) and `deriveFlatSpotValues()`, with `main()` behind an `import.meta.url` guard mirroring `dartbot-level-curve-recalibration.ts`, plus `app/tests/scripts/dartbot-bias-flatspot-fix.test.ts` (8 tests, computed from `SPOTS` itself rather than hand-typed). Task 2 (`461d72db`) applied the derived values (level 7 `biasXMm`/`biasYMm` 2.22/2.54, 8 1.63/2.08, 9 1.34/1.57, 10 1.1/1.19, 12 0.62/0.62, 13 0.43/0.43) to `LEVEL_SKILL_TABLE`, appended two guard tests to `skill-profile.module.test.ts` ("gives every adjacent level pair a distinct bias pair" across all 15 levels, and "shrinks bias magnitude from level 7 to level 15"), regenerated the level-8 `throw-engine.determinism.test.ts` snapshot (only `landing.x`/`landing.y` shift, by a constant `-0.87`/`-1.22` matching level 8's bias delta exactly — `aim`, `hit` and the entry count are untouched), and rewrote `LEVEL_SKILL_TABLE`'s TSDoc paragraph. Anchors 1, 6 and 15, and every non-bias field, are untouched throughout. This task adds a new row to `08-DartBot-Anchor-Log.md` recording levels 1/8/15 re-verified at `tier-bands.test.ts`'s own seed (`BASE_SEED = 700000 + level`, 5000 visits) — level 1 average 32.15/checkout 0.0214/treble 0.0565/miss 0.138, level 8 average 60.54/checkout 0.152/treble 0.1673/miss 0.00053, level 15 average 136.11/checkout 0.63/treble 0.6343/miss 0 — noting that level 8's treble rate sits only ~11% above its 0.15 band floor, a margin inherited from the D-N recalibration rather than created here (the pre-change value at the same seed measured 0.1670, so this change moved treble slightly further from the floor, not toward it); new **D313** (`decisions/game-engine.md`, 62 decisions now). `08-DartBot.md`'s version header gains a `0.8.9` entry pointing at the anchor log rather than restating its detail — no new `## Resolved:` section, since none of the document's four existing ones (D-E, D-L, D-M, D-N) are the lettered design deferral this closes; this is a follow-on data fix to an already-closed D-N, not a new closure. `skill-profile.module.ts`'s TSDoc gains one clause naming the second guard test (bias magnitude strictly decreasing level 7 to 15) — a comment-only edit, the sole non-documentation file this task touches. `00-File-Inventory.md`'s `decisions/game-engine.md` row recounts 61→62 with D313 named, and its `08-DartBot.md` row names the new script and the flat-spot removal alongside D-N's existing citation. > **Version:** 1.88.0 (2026-09-18 — p5-decision-id-derivation: closes issue #386, plan P5 of the technical-debt sweep, task 3 of 3 (docs, decision and context registration; tasks 1 and 2 shipped the two scripts). #386 reported PRs #381 and #382 each declaring a decision id that `main` had since issued to a different decision, and asked whether the uniqueness check belongs pre-merge. It already did: `scripts/check-decision-ids.sh` runs from `quality.yml` on every `pull_request` to `main`, and `actions/checkout` resolves that event to the *merge* ref, so the uniqueness check already sees branch and `main` combined — which is exactly how both #386 collisions were caught. No new CI job is added here; the gap was authoring ergonomics and the cost of a late fix, not a missing gate. Task 1 (`2f57632b`) added `scripts/next-decision-id.sh`, which prints the next free id derived against fetched `origin/main` union the working tree, searching both the migrated table-row form (`| D<n> |`) and the block-heading form (`### D<n>`) since both share one id space. Task 2 (`4227382e`, hardened `16b6b797`) added `scripts/renumber-decision.sh <old> <new>`, which rewrites one id across every tracked file and reports what it touched. Review of Task 2's first commit found the script, as the plan specified it, carried two defects that violated the plan's own Global Constraints: no guard refused renumbering an id already live on `main`, and no guard refused filling one of the 20 never-issued ids (`D18 D19 D29 D38 D39 D42 D43 D44 D45 D46 D47 D48 D49 D53 D54 D55 D56 D57 D58 D59`). The second was demonstrated, not asserted: `renumber-decision.sh D293 D18` exited 0 and all six checks of `check-decision-ids.sh` then printed `OK:`; undoing it rewrote 18 files (57 lines), including `app/src/modules/game/checkout-path.module.ts`, whose double-out table became `["T20","T20","D293"]` — precisely the darts-shorthand hazard the ledger's position-anchoring rule exists for. Commit `16b6b797` closed both — the second correctly, the first by overshooting, which the fix wave below repairs: an on-main refusal (fetching `origin/main` fresh and refusing `OLD` if it is present there at all) and a `NEVER_ISSUED_IDS` refusal for `NEW`, alongside four lower-severity fixes — the dirty-tree guard now reads the whole working tree (`git status --porcelain`) rather than only the staged index, id validation rejects zero-padded forms like `D0293`, the heading-anchor regex now also accepts a title joined without a space (such as an em dash) or the id alone at end of line, and the per-file occurrence count switched from a line-count (`grep -c`) to a true match-count (`grep -o | wc -l`). This task wires both scripts in: `.claude/skills/context-maintenance/SKILL.md` step 3 gains the re-derive-before-PR instruction and names the renumber helper as the fix for a stale id; `00-File-Inventory.md` gains two Helper rows under Cross-cutting mechanical guards and its `decisions/context-system.md` row recounts 43→45, naming D315, D316 and D318; both scripts' header comments gain one comment-only clarifying clause each — `next-decision-id.sh` notes that "the working tree" means whichever repository the caller's cwd resolves to, not the script's own worktree, and `renumber-decision.sh`'s SAFETY paragraph corrects "dirty index" to "dirty working tree", matching what the hardened guard actually checks. A whole-branch review then found the on-main refusal was the wrong test: an id `main` has *since issued* is by definition on `main`, so that guard fired on every real collision and the tool could not fix the one problem it exists for. One fix wave replaced it with the test that does distinguish the two states, reading `OLD`'s declaration lines under `decisions/**` (row form or `### D<n>` heading) — more than one is the unresolved post-merge tree, refused; exactly one that is byte-identical to a line on `origin/main` is main's own block, refused; anything else is the branch's own block and proceeds — and a citation with no block anywhere is refused too, distinctly from an id that appears in no tracked file at all. The same wave taught `next-decision-id.sh` to union in the ids that plans under `docs/superpowers/plans/` reserve in prose, against both refs, matching both historical spellings (`- Decision id: **D123**` and `**Decision id:** D317`), since a plan merges to `main` tasks ahead of the decision it reserves; rewrote `00-File-Inventory.md`'s renumber row to name all five refusals instead of a "dirty index"; pointed `DECISIONS.md`'s "How to add a decision" at `bash scripts/next-decision-id.sh` while keeping the raw `git grep` visible as what it runs, closing the contradiction this branch created by telling authors to follow that section verbatim and then overriding its command; aligned the OLD-side heading anchor with the NEW-side's `([^0-9]|$)`; and added a side-effect clause to both script headers, since an advisory helper that writes the local `origin/main` ref is not read-only. Branch context: rebased twice as `main` moved under it, finally onto `71cb9838`, and the decision id moved from D314 to D318 because `main`'s PR #443 took D314 mid-flight while D315, D316 and D317 sat reserved by three in-flight plans on `main` (#445 has since issued D315 for real, and #449 D316) — the exact miss that made reservation-reading a requirement. New **D318** (`decisions/context-system.md`, 45 decisions now). Validation: `check-context-map.sh`, `check-doc-links.sh`, `check-context-budget.sh`, `check-agent-mirrors.sh`, `check-file-locations.sh`, `check-test-coverage.sh`, `check-skill-pointers.sh` and `check-decision-ids.sh` (297 distinct ids, none duplicated) all pass; `format:check` clean. `db:status`/`db:migrate`/`db:introspect` not run — no `DATABASE_URL` in this environment (D193), the established precedent; no schema or migration file touched. Work done in the `p5-decision-id` worktree on branch `chore/p5-decision-id-derivation`.)

> **Version:** 1.66.0 (2026-09-16 — issue-336-335-training-activity-guards: closes issues #336 (critical, security) and #335 (high, bug) on one branch. #336: `POST /api/training-sessions/:activityId/steps/:n` never checked ownership — `findActivityConfiguration` (`training-session.repository.ts`) filtered on `activity_id` only, so any authenticated player could read another player's activity configuration snapshot and insert `exercise_sessions` rows into their activity. The query now takes `playerId` and inner-joins `activities`; `startTrainingStep` returns `SESSION_OWNERSHIP_MISMATCH` (403) before any write when the snapshot is not the caller's, so unknown and foreign activity ids are indistinguishable. #335: `transitionTrainingActivity` (`training-session.service.ts`) rewrote `status_id` + `completed_at` with no status guard, so `complete` followed by `abandon` destroyed a completed routine's original `completed_at` — against root `CLAUDE.md`'s first hard invariant. `updateActivityStatusRecord` now takes `expectedStatusId` and carries `activities.status_id = <ACTIVE>` in its own WHERE (atomic, not read-then-write); a zero-row update is classified by the new player-scoped `findActivityStatus` into `NOT_FOUND` or `SESSION_ALREADY_COMPLETED` (409), mirroring `session.service.ts`'s `updateSessionStatus`. Tests written red-first: 9 failing cases across `training-session.repository.test.ts` (player-scoped predicate on both reads, `status_id` in the UPDATE predicate, `findActivityStatus`) and `training-session.service.test.ts` (403 for a foreign activity with no insert attempted, `expectedStatusId` passed, 409 on a terminal activity), plus two envelope cases in `pages/api/training-sessions/training-sessions.test.ts` (403 and 409). Work done on branch `claude/issue-336-335-training-activity-guards`.)

> **Version:** 1.67.0 (2026-09-16 — issue-283-dartbot-atc-doubles: Around the Clock and Doubles Training gain a working DartBot opponent, closing issue #283 (migrated `FINDINGS.md` F61). Both rulesets were already admitted in `RULESET_DARTBOT` and both setup forms already passed `allowDartbot={supportsDartbot(...)}`, so seating a bot created a session whose bot seat took the turn and never threw — a silently stuck session the human could only escape by abandoning. The fix is play-page only and mechanical: `around-the-clock-play.data.ts` and `doubles-training-play.data.ts` each gain `botThrowing`, `findBotSeat`, `botDartIndex`, `throwBotDart`, `maybeRunBotVisit` (fired from `init` and `commitDart`'s tail) and `undoVisit`'s `undoToActiveSeat` branch, copied from `shanghai-play.data.ts`/`bobs27-play.data.ts` — both new games are `PER_SEAT` + `DictatedStrategy`, the same shape as the two references, so no strategy-layer, engine, `.astro` or capability-map change was needed and none was made. The single per-game adaptation is the target path: Around the Clock reads the fixed `targetAt(numbersPath(), seatState.targetIndex)`, Doubles Training reads `targetAt(doublesPath(config.targetOrder), seatState.targetIndex)` since its order mode is configurable per session — `singles-training-play.data.ts`'s own `config.targetOrder` adaptation is the precedent. `chooseTarget` (`dictated.strategy.module.ts`) already handles Around the Clock's terminal `BULL`-kind target (aims `INNER_BULL`), so the strategy layer was untouched. `AroundTheClockPlayContext`/`DoublesTrainingPlayContext` (`types.ts`) each gain `botThrowing: boolean` and `maybeRunBotVisit(): Promise<void>`, and each `undoVisit` widens `void` → `Promise<void>` to match the new async body; existing unawaited `play.undoVisit.call(play)` test call sites keep working because `playUndoVisit`/`undoToActiveSeat` still run synchronously before the body's first `await`. No persistence-shape change: both rulesets stay `DETAILED_DARTS`·`VISUAL_BOARD` on an `EXERCISE_BLOCK` stage, and a bot dart is recorded through the same `playCommitDart` → `engine.record(DartObservation)` path a human dart takes, landing in the bot seat's own `TurnFact.darts` with its own `participantRef` — the existing batch upload carries it unchanged. TDD: 4 tests added (2 per game — "throws automatically once it becomes the bot's turn, without any UI action" and "undoVisit returns control to the human across the bot's own turn"), mirroring `shanghai-play.data.test.ts`'s own DartBot block; all 4 failed first (`expected 0 to be greater than 0`, `expected 'bot-1' to be 'participant-1'`) and pass after the wiring. `08-DartBot.md` bumped to 0.8.7 and its play-loop dependency row rewritten from "shipped on Bob's 27, Shanghai, Singles Training, 121, TUOD and Score Training … Around the Clock and Doubles Training are admitted … but their play pages never received this wiring — `FINDINGS.md` F61" to "shipped on all eight `PER_SEAT` rulesets", with both new play-data files added to its file column. `00-File-Inventory.md`'s `08-DartBot.md`, `doubles-training-play.data.ts` and `play-lifecycle.ts` rows refreshed with the two new consumers and an ISO date. No `DECISIONS.md` entry — every part mirrors an already-established sibling pattern (Shanghai's and Bob's 27's own play-loop shapes), no new alternative weighed. Two discovered-work issues filed, neither fixed here: **#360** (`00-File-Inventory.md` has no row at all for `around-the-clock-play.data.ts`, unlike every sibling play-data file) and **#361** (that inventory's `doubles-training-play.data.ts` row claims `DOUBLES_TRAINING_V1` never enters `VISUAL_BOARD`, contradicted by `capabilities.ts:47` and by the file's own `VISUAL_BOARD` tests). Validation: `cd app && npm test` 3895/3895 pass across 215 files (up 4); `astro check --minimumFailingSeverity hint` 0 errors/0 warnings/0 hints across 586 files; `npx fallow` exit 0, 0 above threshold, maintainability 91.9 (good), 7029 analyzed; `npm run format:check` clean; `check-context-map.sh`/`check-doc-links.sh`/`check-context-budget.sh`/`check-agent-mirrors.sh`/`check-file-locations.sh`/`check-test-coverage.sh`/`check-astro-class-composition.sh`/`check-astro-conventions.sh`/`check-game-engines.sh`/`check-refinement-coverage.sh`/`check-type-barrels.sh`/`check-alias-sync.sh`/`check-constraint-mirror.sh`/`check-no-inline-comments.sh`/`check-style-tokens.sh`/`check-game-wiring.sh` all pass. `db:status`/`db:migrate`/`db:introspect` not run — no `app/.env`/`DATABASE_URL` in this session, the established D193 precedent; no migration or schema file touched. `refresh-graph.sh` warned "graphify CLI not installed"; graph freshness is CI-owned.)

> **Version:** 1.68.0 (2026-09-16 — issue-343-views-include-exercise-sessions: fixes issue #343. Migration `0029` relaxed `exercise_sessions.game_type_id` (and `ruleset_version_id`/`capture_mode_id`/`input_mode_id`), `0028` did the same for `exercise_templates.game_type_id`, but no read-model view was generalised with them — every one still INNER JOINed those lookups, so the training gameplay D277/D281 had just made persistent was written to `turns`/`darts` and readable by nothing. The repo owner decided training sessions ARE career statistics, so new migration `0033_read_model_non_game_sessions.sql` rebuilds `v_active_sessions`, `v_session_overview`, `v_dart_analytics`, `v_dart_locations`, `v_player_visit_facts`, `v_player_leg_facts` and `v_routine_execution` with `LEFT JOIN`s. `v_configuration_presets` and `v_double_out_checkout_darts` are deliberately untouched — the audit table in #343 listed the latter, but its own `gt.implementation_key = '501'` filter already excludes every non-game session, so a LEFT JOIN there is a no-op; verified against the files rather than trusted. Read layer made null-safe: `SessionActive` (five nullable keys), `PlayerSessionSummaryRow`/`PlayerVisitFactRow`/`PlayerLegFactRow`, `favoriteGameTypeKey` (skips NULL-keyed rows), `gamesIndex` (drops a session with no ruleset version), and `setSessionModes` now takes a new `SessionModePair` instead of `ModePair`. TDD: `app/tests/db/read-model-non-game-sessions.test.ts` parses the chain's effective view definitions and failed on 12 INNER JOINs before the migration — the shape `migration-numeric-typing.test.ts` established, since no live database exists in this session. `db:status`/`db:migrate`/`db:introspect` were NOT run: no dev `.env`, no `neon` CLI, and production reads denied by the sandbox — the D193 precedent — so `app/src/db/schema.ts`'s view bodies were hand-edited to match `0033`. > **Version:** 1.69.0 (2026-09-16 — tuod-step-advance-conflict: root-causes issue #355 (the persisting form of #332) — Balanced Training could not advance from the Double Pattern ("Doubles") step into the Finishing (TUOD) step, showing `Could not continue to the next step (INTERNAL_ERROR, <requestId>). Try again.` Production row state for the failing run settled which call failed: the Double Pattern step session had 1 stage / 9 turns / 27 darts persisted and `completed_at` set (so `appendBatch` and `completeSession` both succeeded) and no step-4 session row existed at all, so the failure was `startTrainingStep` for the GAME step. Its insert collided with `uq_sessions_single_active` against a TUOD session left open since 2026-09-14 — resumable-session state by design — which should have returned `SESSION_ALREADY_ACTIVE`. It did not, because drizzle-orm 0.45.2 wraps every query error in a `DrizzleQueryError` (`pg-core/session.cjs`) whose message is `Failed query: …` and whose `code`/`constraint` are undefined; the Postgres `23505` and constraint name survive only on `cause`, and `isActiveSessionConflict` (`session.service.ts`) read the top-level error only, so the conflict was rethrown and classified `INTERNAL_ERROR` at the middleware boundary. Its existing unit test passed throughout because the fixture was a flat object carrying `code`/`constraint` directly — a shape production never throws. `isActiveSessionConflict` now walks the `cause` chain (bounded to 8 links, self-reference guarded), which also restores the setup screens' active-session reconciliation modal for wrapped conflicts; the routine's step-advance message moves into `stepAdvanceErrorMessage` (`lib/training/step-advance-error.ts`), which maps `SESSION_ALREADY_ACTIVE` to a message naming the unfinished Ten Up One Down game instead of a bare code, since only the player can decide the fate of a session that may hold real darts — extracted rather than inlined because the inline branch pushed `balanced-training-play.data.ts` to CRAP risk 31.6, over `npx fallow`'s threshold of 30. New **D285** (`decisions/api.md`). `00-File-Inventory.md`'s `decisions/api.md` row updated (38→39 decisions, ~6.5k→~7.2k). Tests: `session.service.test.ts` gains four `isActiveSessionConflict` cases (singly wrapped, doubly wrapped, unrelated wrapped, self-referencing `cause`); `balanced-training-play.data.test.ts` gains one case asserting the new message on the Double Pattern → Finishing transition, and `step-advance-error.test.ts` is new (four cases). Validation: `npx fallow` 0 above threshold, maintainability 92.0 (good, up from 91.9). Validation: `cd app && npx vitest run` 3896/3896 pass across 215 files. Evidence came from read-only SELECTs against the production Neon branch (`main`), run by the user — the first task in this repo to root-cause from live row state rather than code reading alone. `db:status`/`db:migrate`/`db:introspect` not run (no schema change); `scripts/refresh-graph.sh` real refresh not run (no `graphify` CLI in this session, D193 precedent). The stale TUOD row itself is a production data fix the user applies; recurrence prevention (a routine-level Continue/Abandon modal) is filed as discovered work, not fixed here. Work done on branch `claude/issue-355-tuod-step-advance`.)

> **Version:** 1.74.0 (2026-09-17 — b1-single-active-invariant: closes issue #310 and the enforceable half of #358, bundled because both defend the same rule at two layers. #310: `uq_sessions_single_active` keyed on `(player_id, game_type_id)`, and migration `0029` had made `game_type_id` nullable, so from `0029` onward every WARM_UP/SWITCHING/DOUBLE_PATTERN session carried a NULL that Postgres reads as distinct — the index constrained nothing on the training path. New migration `0034_single_active_exercise_sessions.sql` rewrites it over `(player_id, COALESCE(game_type_id, exercise_type_id)) WHERE completed_at IS NULL`, after a backfill that keeps the newest `started_at` per key and marks the rest ABANDONED with `completed_at = now()`; the backfill is deliberately not reversed by `migrate:down`. `NULLS NOT DISTINCT` was rejected because it collapses all non-game sessions into one key. New `database/verification/0034_single_active_session_checks.sql` (4 checks: second open SWITCHING rejected, a different exercise type still startable, closing the first frees the key), registered in `database/README.md`. New **D291** (`decisions/database.md`). #358: `startTraining` now abandons every training activity the player still has open — identified by carrying an `activity_configurations` snapshot, which is what separates it from the activity a standalone game creates — plus every step session still open under one, inside the transaction that inserts the new activity; `insertTrainingActivity` takes the caller's tx instead of opening its own, matching `insertExerciseSessionRecord`. Closing those sessions is not optional: leaving them open would hand the new routine a `SESSION_ALREADY_ACTIVE` dead-end on its first step, now that the index actually fires. `startNonGameStep` gains the conflict handling its game sibling already had, via new `findActiveSessionForExerciseType` (`session.repository.ts`) and `resolveActiveExerciseConflict`; `npx fallow` pushed `startNonGameStep` to CRAP 37.1 once the catch landed, resolved by extracting `resolveNonGameReferences` and the conflict helper (0 above threshold after). New **D292** (`decisions/api.md`). The activities-level partial unique index issue #358 proposes is deliberately NOT built: `insertSessionRecords` creates an `activities` row for every game and nothing ever writes `activities.completed_at` for it, so every game ever played leaves a permanently-open activity and no `WHERE completed_at IS NULL` index over `activities` could be created — filed as discovered work (issue #380), with #358 left open for it. TDD: 6 cases added red-first across `training-session.repository.test.ts` (abandon closes activities and their open sessions; no-op when nothing is open) and `training-session.service.test.ts` (abandon precedes insert; INTERNAL_ERROR when ABANDONED status is missing; SESSION_ALREADY_ACTIVE on a non-game conflict; non-conflict errors rethrow); the existing `insertTrainingActivity` repo case was re-pointed at the new tx signature, same guarantee. `app/src/db/schema.ts`'s `uq_sessions_single_active` carries the COALESCE as raw `sql`. Docs: `05-Database/04-Indexes.md` (both copies of the index), `10-Database-Agent-Guide.md`, `06-Spec/04-Runtime-Layer.md`; migration ranges bumped `0001`–`0033` → `0001`–`0034` across root `CLAUDE.md`, `database/CLAUDE.md`, `database/README.md`, `00-Context-Map.md`, `00-File-Inventory.md`, `docs/architecture/README.md`, `05-Database/10-Database-Agent-Guide.md`, `05-Database/11-Neon-Integration.md`, `07-Frontend/09-Adding-A-Game.md`. `00-File-Inventory.md`'s `decisions/database.md` (17→18, ~4.5k→~5.7k) and `decisions/api.md` (39→40, ~7.2k→~8.4k) rows updated. Validation: `npx vitest run` 3960/3960 across 219 files; `astro check --minimumFailingSeverity hint` 0 errors/0 warnings/0 hints across 593 files; `npx fallow` 0 above threshold, maintainability 92.0 (good); `npm run format` clean; `check-decision-ids`/`check-context-map`/`check-doc-links`/`check-context-budget`/`check-file-locations`/`check-no-inline-comments`/`check-type-barrels`/`check-alias-sync`/`check-constraint-mirror`/`check-astro-conventions`/`check-game-wiring`/`check-agent-mirrors`/`check-test-coverage` all pass. `db:status`/`db:migrate`/`db:introspect` not run — no `DATABASE_URL` in this session, the D193 precedent — so migration `0034` and its verification script are unapplied and unproven against a live database; `0034` must be applied before the service change ships, since the non-game conflict path depends on the index existing. Work done in a git worktree on branch `fix/b1-single-active-invariant`, the first task after D287 re-permitted worktrees.)
