> **Version:** 1.76.0 (2026-09-17 — b5-exercise-engine-config-bounds: closes issues #300 and #298, bundle B5 of the medium-severity grouping — two latent exercise-config defects, each one file plus its tests. #300: `DoublePatternEngine.record()` reused the open turn whenever `last.darts.length < patternLength`, comparing the already-open (or already-completed) turn's dart count against the *incoming* dart's own pattern length. For `patterns: [[20,10],[16,8,4]]` the first dart of the 3-long second pattern found a completed 2-dart turn, read `2 < 3` as true, and extended a turn already stamped `completedAt` to 3 darts — grouping observed as `[3, 2]` instead of `[2, 3]` by mutation-checking the new test against the old predicate. The predicate is now `(last) => last.completedAt === null`, which is already stamped exactly when a turn fills its own pattern's length and needs no new state; `undoLastDart` clears `completedAt` whenever darts remain and pops the turn when none do, so undo stays consistent with it. Latent today — every seeded Double Pattern template uses three length-3 patterns. #298: `SwitchingV1Config.targets` admitted `25` per element, but `pointsFor` has no bull zone, so a bull target scored 0 on every visit, indistinguishable from a miss. Resolved by tightening the bound to `.max(20)` rather than adding a bull zone: every Switching dart is stamped `TREBLE` as its intended zone (D276) and the bull has no treble, so target 25 is incoherent at the intent level, not merely unscored — the bound had been copied from `WarmUpPhaseConfig`, whose targets carry no ring intent and rightly keep `.max(25)`. `pages/api/sessions/types.ts`'s `TargetNumber` also keeps `.max(25)`: a *hit* on the bull is real data whatever was aimed at. New **D297** (`decisions/game-engine.md`, 47→48, ~24.5k→~27.9k). `09-training-routines.md` §17 gains both notes. Not verifiable here: no `DATABASE_URL` in this environment (D193), so whether a live `exercise_templates.default_configuration` row names 25 in a Switching target list is unchecked — every in-repo seed is clean (`seeds/0017` configures `[20, 19, 18]`), but a production row carrying 25 would begin failing validation.)

> **Version:** 1.79.0 (2026-09-17 — b8-frontend-structure-and-rendering: closes issues #347 and #350, bundle B8 of the medium-severity grouping — two canonical frontend documents describing a smaller app than the one in the repo. #347: `07-Frontend/02-Folder-Structure.md` calls itself the authoritative `app/src/` layout, but its tree omitted nine directories that exist — `lib/{exercise,training,stats,trivia,ui}` and `modules/{exercise,training,stats,trivia}` — and its Path Aliases table carried 15 of `app/tsconfig.json`'s 18, missing `@auth`, `@server` and `@assets`. All nine trees and all three aliases added. `04-Modules-And-OOP.md`'s OOP Boundary table gains three rows for the undocumented module families; both were written against the code rather than the issue body, which corrected two claims mid-flight — `modules/exercise/*.engine.module.ts` implements `ExerciseEngine<TState>` reached through an `ExerciseEngineFactory`, not `GameEngine`, and `modules/training/` holds no classes at all (pure functions plus a `trainingEngine` object literal), so it is recorded as prefer-functions rather than OOP. #350: `01-Rendering-Strategy.md` states that every new HTML route must be classified in it, then omitted `/training` — four shipped `prerender = true` routes including Balanced Training's play page — and still described `/statistics` as a "post-v1 placeholder shell — no stats API calls until view-backed endpoints ship (D63)", although D258 landed that endpoint on 2026-09-06 and `stats.store.ts` reads it. Both rows corrected; the § Server Islands list's third instance of the same stale claim ("statistics (deferred post-v1)") corrected with them. New **D302** (`decisions/frontend/architecture.md`, 22→23): `scripts/check-alias-sync.sh` gains the doc's alias table as a third source beside `tsconfig.json` and `vitest.config.ts`, failing in both directions, so this class of drift is mechanical rather than audit-found — D113's gate read only the two machine sources, which is why #347 surfaced through a manual repo audit. Mutation-checked both ways: deleting the `@assets` row fails it, adding a `@bogus` row the build cannot resolve fails it. The vitest `@styles` allowlist is now scoped to the vitest comparison only; no alias is exempt from being documented. The tree half of the same document stays unenforced — no gate would have caught the nine missing directories either, and D302 records that. Docs: `07-Frontend/01-Rendering-Strategy.md` (0.2.0→0.3.0), `07-Frontend/02-Folder-Structure.md` (0.2.4→0.3.0), `07-Frontend/04-Modules-And-OOP.md` (0.2.2→0.3.0). `00-File-Inventory.md`'s `decisions/frontend/architecture.md` row also read "19 decisions" against an actual 22 before this change; corrected to 23 rather than carried forward as 20. No `app/` source changed and no migration was added, so `validate:app`'s `db:status`/`db:migrate`/`db:introspect` steps do not apply — there is no `DATABASE_URL` in this environment (D193) — and neither does the migration-range cross-check.)

> **Version:** 1.80.0 (2026-09-17 — b9-finishing-step-seam: closes issue #370 and resolves issue #306 by verification, bundle B9 of the medium-severity grouping. #370: `app/tests/lib/training/finishing-step.data.test.ts` was the Finishing step's only coverage and it `vi.mock`s `tuodPlay` wholesale, so the path the step actually takes to completion — `recordDart` → `wouldComplete()` → `showFinishConfirm` → `confirmFinish()` → `uploadAndCompleteSession()` → the routine's `onStepComplete` — executed in no test. That seam is where every Finishing-step dead-end has lived (#216, #357, #370): the suite could not distinguish "the routine advances" from "the routine can be reached at all". New `app/tests/lib/training/finishing-step-seam.test.ts` drives the real `tuodPlay()` under `finishingStep()` with only `@client/api/sessions` and `SegmentTimer` mocked, over a `$store` stub carrying `timerExpired: true` on a solo MINUTES config — under `durationSeatComplete` that makes the next visit-resolving dart the session's last, which is the state the step hangs in. Five cases: the finish confirm is raised without committing or advancing; confirming commits the dart, uploads and advances exactly once; a rejected `appendBatch` leaves `completionStatus = "failed"` with the routine held in place; cancelling leaves the step playable; and `resultsSnapshot.seats[0]` is populated, which is the read `captureStepSummary` depends on. The tests were proved non-vacuous by mutation rather than assertion alone: deleting the `return` after `showFinishConfirm = true` in `tuod-play.data.ts` fails three of the five, and removing the `completionStatus !== "succeeded"` guard in `finishing-step.data.ts` fails the fourth. The mocked file is deliberately kept — it pins the wrapper's own contract independently of TUOD's rules, and the module-scope `vi.mock` is why the two cannot share a file — and gains a header comment naming the seam file. New **D304** (`decisions/testing.md`), generalising past this file: wherever one store wraps another and overrides part of its behaviour, the seam is covered against the real inner store, not only against a double. `07-Frontend/06-Test-Strategy.md` gains a section stating the two-file split and the `timerExpired` fixture; `00-File-Inventory.md`'s `decisions/testing.md` (8→9 decisions, ~2.3k→~3.2k — the old figure was already understated before this task) and `07-Frontend/06-Test-Strategy.md` (~1.6k→~1.9k) rows updated. #306 required no code: two of the three blocks it asks for already render (`CheckoutConfirm` and the finish-confirm `ConfirmDialog`, added by PR #372), and the third, `TenUpOneDownResults`, is deliberately NOT added — its `ResultsModalShell` hardcodes a "Back to games" `back()` and a "Play again" `playAgain()` button row that `finishingStep()` does not override, so dropping it into the routine gives the player two buttons that escape the routine mid-flow. The results themselves already surface through `summariseFinishing` (`routine-summary.module.ts`) into `RoutineSummaryModal`, emitting the same two stat rows (`Target reached`, `Double accuracy`) that modal shows; the new seam test's fifth case pins the snapshot that path reads. Test-only change to `app/`, so no source file moved and `check-test-coverage` has nothing to trip on. Validation recorded in the PR. Work done in a git worktree on branch `test/b9-finishing-step-seam`.)

> **Version:** 1.78.0 (2026-09-17 — b7-request-id-header-and-stale-contracts: closes issues #340, #341 and #339, bundle B7 of the medium-severity grouping — one unbuilt header contract and two canonical API documents describing a system that is not running. #340: `03-Shared-Conventions.md` has specified `X-Request-Id` as a response header since 2026-07-10, but `envelope.ts` held one shared frozen `JSON_HEADERS` carrying `Content-Type` alone, so the request id existed only inside the JSON body — D282 surfaced `requestId` in client errors precisely so a failure could be correlated to its Worker log line, and the half of that contract a proxy, the devtools network pane or a log shipper can read was never built. `ok()` and `fail()` now build headers per response via `jsonHeaders(requestId)`. The header contract is narrowed from "all responses" to "all envelope responses": the `/api/auth/*` pass-through proxy returns the upstream response untouched and is never wrapped in the envelope, so it emits no `X-Request-Id`; every other response the API produces comes from one of the two builders. New **D300** (`decisions/api.md`, 42→43). New `app/tests/lib/server/envelope.test.ts` (5 cases) asserts the header against the body's id on both builders; mutation-checked by dropping the header from `jsonHeaders` (3 of 5 fail). #341: `04-Endpoint-Contracts.md` documented `inputModeKey` as `QUICK_SCORE | DETAILED_DARTS`, omitting the `VISUAL_BOARD` seeded in `0005` that every dart-exercise step session, the Finishing step, `v_dart_locations` and `v_double_out_checkout_darts` actually use. The same section named `ANALYTICS` / `DETAILED_DARTS` as the ruleset-required pair — a pair seed `0007` declares for no ruleset at all: all 15 ruleset versions pair `ANALYTICS` with `VISUAL_BOARD`, and `RECREATIONAL` with `QUICK_SCORE` or `DETAILED_DARTS` per game. Both corrected, with the legal pairs restated as what they are — rows in `ruleset_version_capabilities` (migration `0019`, seeded in `0007`), not a rule the contract states. Doc-only; no decision. #339: `06-API/00-Overview.md` § Frozen Decisions and `04-Endpoint-Contracts.md` both froze "one activity per session; multi-session activities and routine-run writes deferred post-v1", which shipped Balanced Training contradicts — `startTraining` writes one `activities` row plus its snapshot and each `startTrainingStep` adds another `exercise_sessions` row against that same activity, so one activity to four sessions is the shipped shape. Both restated as shipped under new **D301** (`decisions/api.md`, 43→44, Supersedes D64). This is a semantics correction to a frozen bullet, not a doc-only bump, so both documents take a minor version bump and D301 is the authority for it; nothing in `app/` changes. Docs: `06-API/00-Overview.md` (1.8.0→1.9.0), `06-API/03-Shared-Conventions.md` (1.7.0→1.8.0), `06-API/04-Endpoint-Contracts.md` (1.5.0→1.6.0). Discovered work filed, not fixed: the Frozen Decisions bullet immediately above the activity one still freezes "guest/DartBot play deferred post-v1", contradicted by the guest seats that shipped 2026-08-21 and are documented in `04-Endpoint-Contracts.md` — same defect class as #339, different bullet. Validation: `npm test` 4008/4008 across 221 files; `astro check --minimumFailingSeverity hint` 0 errors/0 warnings/0 hints across 594 files; `npx fallow` 0 above threshold, maintainability 92.0; `npm run format:check` clean; all 17 `scripts/check-*.sh` gates pass. No migration and no schema change, so the `db:status`/`db:migrate`/`db:introspect` steps of `validate:app` were not run — there is no `DATABASE_URL` in this environment (D193) — and nothing in this change needs them.)

> **Version:** 1.77.0 (2026-09-17 — b6-read-model-view-contracts: closes issues #342 and #344, bundle B6 of the medium-severity grouping — two read models documented as the source for a read that did not use them. #342: `findStartingScores` (`statistics.repository.ts`) selected `exercise_configurations.configuration` directly, because `v_double_out_checkout_darts` exposed no `starting_score` — the one raw-table dependency in a path `06-API/00-Overview.md` calls view-backed, and a coupling of career statistics to a JSONB snapshot's shape with no view boundary between them. Migration `0024` had justified the omission ("a plain arithmetic projection, never a JSONB-parsing one"); `0025` overtook that one migration later by projecting `(configuration ->> 'max_darts_per_turn')` on `v_player_visit_facts`, so `0036` follows `0025`. The LEFT JOIN is non-fanning only because `uq_exercise_configuration_session` exists, which the verification script asserts. `findDoubleOutVisits` now issues one query where it issued two; a NULL `starting_score` reads as 0, the same fallback the two-query version applied to a missing config row. #344: `v_routine_execution` had zero consumers — `findRoutineTemplateSteps` re-implemented the same read against `routine_templates`/`routine_steps`/`exercise_templates`/`duration_types`, because the view exposed none of `is_system_template`, `exercise_type_key`, `exercise_ruleset_version_key`, `default_configuration` or `step_configuration`. `0033` had already fixed the view's `game_types` join (issue #343, closed) but nothing consumed the result. `0036` adds all five columns and the repository reads the view in one query filtered on `routine_name` + `is_system_template`, importing no template table at all. Behaviour change: a stepless system routine now reads as no routine (the view is built from `routine_steps`), so `startTraining` answers `VALIDATION_FAILED` instead of opening an activity with an empty step list. The three `GET /api/routines*` rows stay in the Read Contract, marked unbuilt, with `POST /api/training-sessions` named as the view's live consumer — dropping them was rejected because they are frozen contract in the same sense as the `GET /api/sessions` list. New **D298** (`decisions/database.md`, 22→23) and **D299** (`decisions/api.md`, 41→42). New `database/verification/0036_read_model_view_consumers_checks.sql` (10 checks), registered in `database/README.md` and `00-File-Inventory.md`. Tests: `statistics.repository.test.ts` takes `starting_score` off the view row and gains a NULL case; `training-session.repository.test.ts`'s D295 pin-join assertion is re-pointed at the view column that now carries it — same guarantee — plus new cases for the single-query read and the system-routine filter. The filter case was mutation-checked (dropping `is_system_template` fails it); the dropped-select-column case is caught by `astro check`, not by the mock-based tests, and was verified that way. Docs: `05-Views/00-Overview.md` (version 1.3.0→1.4.0), `06-Spec/05-Read-Model-Layer.md`, `06-API/00-Overview.md`, `06-API/04-Endpoint-Contracts.md`; migration ranges bumped `0001`–`0035` → `0001`–`0036` across root `CLAUDE.md`, `database/CLAUDE.md`, `database/README.md`, `00-Context-Map.md`, `00-File-Inventory.md`, `docs/architecture/README.md`, `05-Database/10-Database-Agent-Guide.md`, `05-Database/11-Neon-Integration.md`, `07-Frontend/09-Adding-A-Game.md`. Validation: `npm test` 4003/4003 across 220 files; `astro check --minimumFailingSeverity hint` 0 errors/0 warnings/0 hints across 593 files; `npx fallow` 0 above threshold, maintainability 92.0; `npm run format:check` clean; all 17 `scripts/check-*.sh` gates pass. `db:status`/`db:migrate`/`db:introspect` not run — no `DATABASE_URL` in this environment (D193) — so migration `0036` and its verification script are unapplied and unproven against a live database, and `schema.ts` was hand-edited to match, as `0034` and `0035` were.)

> **Version:** 1.75.0 (2026-09-17 — b4-exercise-ruleset-validation: closes issues #337 and #338, bundle B4 of the medium-severity grouping. #338: `findRoutineTemplateSteps` resolved a step's exercise ruleset version by joining `exercise_ruleset_versions` on `exercise_type_id` with no version predicate — unambiguous only because exactly one version exists per type today, and fanning the `leftJoin` out the moment a second is seeded. Migration `0035` adds `exercise_templates.exercise_ruleset_version_id` (nullable) under a *composite* FK on `(exercise_type_id, exercise_ruleset_version_id)` → `exercise_ruleset_versions (exercise_type_id, id)`, backed by the new `uq_exercise_ruleset_versions_type_id`, so a template pinned to another exercise type's ruleset is unrepresentable; seed `0019` backfills the three non-game system templates and the join is repointed. #337: `warmUpValidator`/`switchingValidator`/`doublePatternValidator` had no importer outside their own tests, so the training path snapshotted step configuration unvalidated while the game path validated every create. `startTraining` now validates each step's merged configuration and fails the whole call `VALIDATION_FAILED` — including when a non-game step resolves no validator, which is what makes `0035`'s nullable column safe. Validation runs on the merge *before* `resolveStep` injects `stepDurationSeconds`, since `WarmUpV1Config` is `.strict()`; both orderings were mutation-checked. New **D295** (`decisions/database.md`, 21→22) and **D296** (`decisions/api.md`, 40→41). `database/verification/0035_exercise_template_ruleset_version_checks.sql` (11 checks) written but NOT executed — no `DATABASE_URL` in this environment, per D193 — and `schema.ts` hand-edited to match the migration for the same reason, as `0034` was. Discovered work filed, not fixed: the `06-Spec/06-Relationships-and-Evolution.md` matrix has no rows for `exercise_types`/`exercise_ruleset_versions` at all, and a GAME routine step's configuration still reaches `exercise_configurations` unvalidated against its game ruleset.)

> **Version:** 1.70.0 (2026-09-16 — issue-357-balanced-training-session-conflict: fixes issue #357, the recurrence/UX half D285 deliberately left open. A Balanced Training run dead-ended at the Finishing step whenever an unfinished TUOD game already existed: `startTrainingStep`'s `SESSION_ALREADY_ACTIVE` reached the play page as a bare `ErrorAlert` with no action, so the player had to leave the routine, resolve the game under Games, and start over — losing the Warm-Up, Switching and Double Pattern work already persisted for that run, and leaving the routine activity ACTIVE forever. `startCurrentStep()` (`balanced-training-play.data.ts`) now catches that one code, reads the blocking game from the failure's `details` through the new `lib/training/step-session-conflict.ts` (`activeSessionConflict`), and holds it in `blockingSession` instead of rethrowing; the new `BlockedStepModal.astro` (`components/layout/training/`, page-owned `x-if`) offers `Abandon & continue` (`resolveBlockingSession()` — `completeSession(sessionId, "ABANDONED")` then `startCurrentStep()` again, resuming at step 4) and `Leave routine` (the existing `abandonAndExit()`). `reconcileActiveSession` is deliberately not reused: its D88 mismatch rule auto-abandons silently, and only the player may end a game that may hold real darts. The server already returns `details.sessionId`/`startedAt` (`resolveActiveSessionConflict`), so no `fetchActiveSessions` round-trip was added. `stepAdvanceErrorMessage()` loses its now-unreachable `SESSION_ALREADY_ACTIVE` branch, and its test case with it; the step-start branching moves into a new `openStep(result, durationSeconds)` because the added `try`/`catch` pushed `startCurrentStep` to CRAP 31.6, over `npx fallow`'s threshold of 30 — the same split D285 needed. New **D286** (`decisions/frontend/alpine.md`); `08-Component-Inventory.md` gains the modal's row; `00-File-Inventory.md`'s `decisions/frontend/alpine.md` (~8.8k→~9.3k) and `08-Component-Inventory.md` (~2.2k→~2.7k) rows refreshed. Tests: `step-session-conflict.test.ts` is new (four cases); `balanced-training-play.data.test.ts`'s D285 dead-end case is repointed to the modal — same guarantee, resolvable ending — plus five new cases (resolve-and-resume, abandon failure keeps the choice open, a repeat conflict re-arms on the newly named session, an id-less conflict stays a plain step error, `blockingStartedLabel()`). Validation: `cd app && npm test` 3947/3947 pass across 218 files; `astro check --minimumFailingSeverity hint` 0 errors/0 warnings/0 hints across 592 files; `npx fallow` exit 0, 0 above threshold, maintainability 92.0, and `fallow audit --base origin/main` reports no issues in the 9 changed files; `npm run format:check` clean; `check-context-map`/`check-doc-links`/`check-context-budget`/`check-agent-mirrors`/`check-file-locations`/`check-test-coverage`/`check-astro-class-composition`/`check-astro-conventions`/`check-game-engines`/`check-refinement-coverage`/`check-type-barrels`/`check-alias-sync`/`check-constraint-mirror`/`check-no-inline-comments`/`check-style-tokens`/`check-game-wiring`/`check-decision-ids` all pass. `db:status`/`db:migrate`/`db:introspect` not run — no dev `.env`/Neon credentials in this session (D193 precedent); no migration or schema file touched. `scripts/refresh-graph.sh` not run locally; graph freshness is CI-owned. Work done on branch `claude/issue-357-balanced-training-session-conflict`.)

> **Version:** 1.73.0 (2026-09-17 — graph-refresh-manual-merge: corrects D287's auto-merge claim, measured on PR #379. `enablePullRequestAutoMerge` succeeded and the PR still reported "Merging is blocked — Commits must have verified signatures": the `Main branch protection` ruleset has carried `required_signatures` since 2026-07-10, `GET /pulls/379/commits` returns `verified: false, reason: "unsigned"` for the `github-actions[bot]` commit, and the ruleset's only bypass actor is the repository owner. Approval was never the blocker. `graph.yml` therefore stops arming auto-merge, writes a notice naming the PR, and states in the PR body that landing it is a manual merge and why; root `CLAUDE.md`'s freshness bullet is corrected from "needs one approval" to "is one human merge". D289 records it, refining D287. Rejected: an SSH signing key in Actions secrets, `createCommitOnBranch` (GitHub-signed, but 7.2 MB of `graph.json` base64-encoded through GraphQL), and adding the bot as a bypass actor, which is per-ruleset and would also exempt it from the pull-request and linear-history rules. The D287 403 fallback is untouched.)
>
> **Version:** 1.72.0 (2026-09-17 — issue-354-migrate-on-deploy: closes issues #293 (high, infrastructure) and #354. `deploy.yml` shipped the Worker on every merge and never touched the database, so a merged migration produced a Worker expecting a schema production did not have — measured when `0024`-`0026` sat unapplied and `GET /api/statistics/overview` returned `INTERNAL_ERROR` for `relation "v_..." does not exist`. The pipeline is now `quality -> rehearse -> migrate -> deploy` inside the existing `deploy-production` concurrency group. New `.github/workflows/db-rehearsal.yml` cuts a throwaway Neon branch from `main`, applies migrations + seeds, runs `db:verify` and deletes the branch in an `always()` step; it is reusable (`workflow_call`) and also runs on any PR touching `database/migrations/**`, `database/seeds/**`, `database/verification/**`, the seed/verify runners or `app/package.json`. `migrate` then applies the same chain to production and re-runs `db:status` to prove nothing is pending. Four `:ci` script variants read `DATABASE_URL` from the environment instead of an `.env` file and pass `--no-dump-schema` (CI has no `pg_dump`); the `:prod` variants are untouched. Two credentials are required and are set in GitHub's UI, never in the repo: `DATABASE_URL` on the `production` environment and a repository-level `NEON_API_KEY`; the Neon project id is read from committed `app/.neon` and is not a secret. Both jobs fail closed with an explicit message when their credential is missing. D288 records it; `11-Neon-Integration.md` gains an Applying Migrations in CI section and `app/DEPLOYMENT.md`'s manual post-merge step becomes a fresh-environment/recovery path. The first real rehearsal run earned its keep immediately: it failed on seed `0002`, unappliable since `0032` promoted `exercise_templates.exercise_type_id` to NOT NULL (NOT NULL is checked before `ON CONFLICT`, so even an already-seeded database could not re-run it) — fixed here by joining both of that file's inserts to the rows they depend on, using the two-pass execution `seed.ts` already guarantees. It then failed on `db:verify`, which is now excluded from the rehearsal: six fixtures predate `0031`'s NOT NULL (#383), 53 of 404 production sessions are undeclared against the capability table (#384), and `0007`'s VALUES lists are two ruleset versions stale (#304, confirmed against production data in this run). Not proven end to end at merge time: the rehearsal needs `NEON_API_KEY` to exist, so its first real run is its first test — the scripts were smoke-tested locally (dbmate resolves the flags and reads `DATABASE_URL` from the environment) and both workflow files parse.)
> **Version:** 1.71.0 (2026-09-17 — issue-345-graph-freshness: closes issue #345 (high, infrastructure). The committed knowledge graph was 692 commits / six weeks stale while every `graph.yml` run reported success: the rebuild, the normalised delta gate, the commit and the force-push to `chore/graph-refresh` all worked, and only `pulls.create` 403'd because *Allow GitHub Actions to create and approve pull requests* was off — a state D186 deliberately degraded to a `core.warning` plus a run summary that nobody reads. Four tasks: (1) the pending `chore/graph-refresh` head landed by hand as PR #368, then PR #374 once CI could open it; (2) `graph.yml`'s blocked-PR branch now opens/updates a tracking issue (`graph: refresh PR could not be opened`, `discovered-work`/`type:infrastructure`/`severity:high`, keyed on a `<!-- graph-refresh-blocked -->` marker and matched on the bot author) and closes it on the next successful open — it fired for real as issue #373 and self-closed on #374; (3) both repo settings (Actions-may-open-PRs, auto-merge) flipped by the owner, after which auto-merge arming failed with "merge commits are not allowed on this repository" because `main`'s ruleset requires linear history, fixed by pinning the `enablePullRequestAutoMerge` call to `SQUASH` (PR #375); (4) this task — the docs rewritten to the measured model, D287 appended (`Supersedes: D186 · Refines: D185`), and the two `D185` citations in `app/CLAUDE.md` and `.claude/skills/validate-app/SKILL.md` re-pointed to `D185, D287`. No refresh has produced a graph delta since the `SQUASH` pin, so D287 records the auto-merge success path as reasoned rather than observed and names the next real delta as its first test. One human approval per refresh is retained deliberately: direct-commit-to-`main` was rejected on least privilege.)
>
> **Version:** 1.65.0 (2026-09-15 — training-routine-error-diagnostics: investigates issue #332 — Balanced Training broke advancing out of the Double Pattern ("Doubles") step into the Finishing (TUOD) step with `Could not continue to the next step (INTERNAL_ERROR). Try again.` Full trace of the client and server code on this path — `advanceAfterStepCompletion`/`uploadCurrentStepFacts` (`balanced-training-play.data.ts`), `appendBatch`/`validateBatchAgainstRuleset` (`session.service.ts`), `startTrainingStep`/`startGameStep` and its `uq_sessions_single_active` conflict-resolution (`training-session.service.ts`), the `DoublePatternEngine`'s fact shape, and the Neon serverless driver's own Postgres error fields — found no reproducible defect in the code as committed; a client-only reproduction of the same Double Pattern → GAME transition (mocked APIs resolving) completed without error. Root cause is therefore still open. What the trace did surface: `middleware.ts`'s uncaught-error boundary already logs the real cause server-side (`console.error([api] uncaught error requestId=..., error)`) but the registry message for `INTERNAL_ERROR` is the fixed string "Internal error" for every cause (`errors.ts`), and `SessionApiError` (`client/api/sessions.ts`) was dropping the one thing the envelope does safely return — `requestId` — before it ever reached the UI, so a generic `INTERNAL_ERROR` on screen could not be correlated to its matching Worker log line. `SessionApiError` now carries `requestId`/`details` from every throw site in `client/api/sessions.ts` and `client/api/training-sessions.ts`; `balancedTrainingPlay()`'s `completeCurrentStep` appends the request id to its on-screen message when present. New **D282** (`decisions/api.md`). `00-File-Inventory.md`'s `decisions/api.md` row updated (36→37 decisions, ~5.7k→~6.5k). Tests: `sessions.test.ts` and `training-sessions.test.ts` each gain a case asserting the envelope's `requestId`/`details` land on the thrown `SessionApiError`; `balanced-training-play.data.test.ts` gains a case asserting the displayed error includes the request id when the underlying API error carries one. Validation: `cd app && npx vitest run` 3891/3891 pass across 215 files; `astro check --minimumFailingSeverity hint` 0 errors/0 warnings/0 hints; `npx fallow` 0 above threshold, maintainability 91.9 (good, unchanged); `npm run format`/`format:check` clean; `check-test-coverage`/`check-no-inline-comments`/`check-type-barrels`/`check-alias-sync`/`check-context-map`/`check-doc-links`/`check-context-budget`/`check-decision-ids` all pass. `db:status`/`db:migrate`/`db:introspect`, `scripts/refresh-graph.sh`'s real refresh, and Cloudflare Worker log access were not available in this session (no `DATABASE_URL`/Neon credentials, no `graphify` CLI, no `wrangler` auth — the established D193 precedent extended to Worker logs); the actual server-side cause of issue #332 remains to be found by reading the `[api] uncaught error requestId=...` log line for the failing request once the user reproduces it again, now that the on-screen message names that request id. Issue #332 stays open pending that log read — this task is diagnostics only, not a located fix. Work done on branch `claude/training-routine-bug-debug-ih64to`.)

> **Version:** 1.64.0 (2026-09-15 — warmup-facts-upload: fixes issue #309 — Warm-Up's `EXERCISE_SECTION` stages were computed every phase (`WarmUpEngine.advance()`) but never reached the server, since `uploadCurrentStepFacts` (`balanced-training-play.data.ts`) only ever read `activeDartEngine()` (Switching/Double Pattern), so its unconditional call in `advanceAfterStepCompletion`/`abandonAndExit` was always a no-op for a `WARM_UP` step — the session was marked `COMPLETED`/`ABANDONED` with zero persisted stages, contradicting root `CLAUDE.md`'s "store what happened." `uploadCurrentStepFacts` now uploads `this.activeDartEngine() ?? this.warmUpEngine`; `WarmUpEngine.facts()`'s `turns: []` degrades correctly through `resolveSoloParticipantRef` (no-op on an empty array) and `buildEventsBatch` (each stage gets an empty `turns` array). Server-side, `validateBatchAgainstRuleset` (`session.service.ts`) rejected exactly this batch shape: D277 (2026-09-14, one day prior) had just re-affirmed "a capture pair remains mandatory for any batch, so a Warm-Up session... still returns `INTERNAL_ERROR`" — true for a batch that writes a dart, but stricter than the real constraint. The check now looks at whether the batch carries any turn at all (`batch.stages.some((stage) => stage.turns.length > 0)`) before requiring a capture pair; a turn-less batch is admitted on `appendBatch`'s structural checks alone, and a batch carrying a turn still requires `captureModeKey`/`inputModeKey` exactly as before. New **D281** (`decisions/api.md`, supersedes D277's capture-pair clause). Issue #313 — the sibling finding that `appendBatch` rejected Switching/Double Pattern's dart-carrying batches outright — was found already fixed on `main` (D277/commit `f893a49`, 2026-09-14) before this task started; no code change was needed for it, and it is closed as a duplicate of already-shipped work rather than left open. `docs/architecture/09-training-routines.md` §16's "Implemented" paragraph gains a sentence naming the new upload path. `00-File-Inventory.md`'s `decisions/api.md` row updated (32→36 decisions, ~4.5k→~5.7k) to keep `check-context-budget.sh` passing after the file grew. Tests: `session.service.test.ts` gains one case (a turn-less `EXERCISE_SECTION` batch against a no-capture-pair Warm-Up session now inserts); the pre-existing "returns INTERNAL_ERROR for a Warm-Up session" case is unchanged and still passes, since its fixture batch carries a turn. `balanced-training-play.data.test.ts` gains one case asserting `appendBatch` is called with the Warm-Up step's `EXERCISE_SECTION` stage on step completion. > **Version:** 1.63.0 (2026-09-14 — game-timer-switch-error: root-causes the "Could not continue to the next step" failure Balanced Training hit the moment the Switching countdown expired, which D276's intended-zone fix did not reach. `appendBatch` rejected every dart-exercise batch before reading a single dart: a `SWITCHING`/`DOUBLE_PATTERN` step session is created with `ruleset_version_id`, `capture_mode_id` and `input_mode_id` all NULL — D264 puts the exercise ruleset in its own column — and the service hard-required all three plus a game `RulesetValidator`, which `SWITCHING_V1` has none of and cannot have, since exercise rulesets validate configuration only. Reproduced as a unit test against `appendBatch` first. `startTrainingStep` now creates dart-exercise step sessions under the `ANALYTICS` + `VISUAL_BOARD` capture pair the playable-Balanced-Training design already specified; `findSessionRow` returns the exercise ruleset key alongside the game one; and `appendBatch`'s new `validateBatchAgainstRuleset` helper validates a game session exactly as before while admitting a dart-exercise batch (`SWITCHING_V1`/`DOUBLE_PATTERN_V1`) on the structural checks alone, still requiring a capture pair so a Warm-Up batch stays an `INTERNAL_ERROR`. The store's swallowed catch now names the API error code in the on-screen message so the next such failure is diagnosable on-device. Records D277; `04-Endpoint-Contracts.md` and `09-training-routines.md` updated; the pre-existing "non-GAME exercise session" test re-pointed at Warm-Up, the session it actually describes)

> **Version:** 1.62.0 (2026-09-13 — ios-wpa-wake-lock: fourth and final pass on the iOS Home Screen wake lock (#278), after PRs #316/#317/#318 all failed on the reporting device. Research established two facts the prior three passes were missing: the silent-video technique has never worked in an iOS Home Screen web app at all (`richtr/NoSleep.js` issue #27 — works in a Safari tab, fails once launched from the Home Screen), so #316's `canvas.captureStream` and #317's embedded MP4 were both dead on arrival there; and the native API's own failure in Home Screen apps was WebKit bug 254545, fixed in iOS 18.4 (Safari 18.4 release notes), while the reporting device runs iOS 26 with Low Power Mode off. `modules/ui/wake-lock.module.ts` is therefore rewritten native-only: the base64 MP4 asset, `createFallbackVideo`, `isStandaloneDisplayMode` and #318's one-shot gesture gate are all deleted, replaced by one idempotent `ensureHeld()` fed by six re-entry points — the sentinel's own `release` event (the signal iOS uses to revoke a lock with no `visibilitychange`, which nothing previously listened for), `visibilitychange`, `pageshow`, `focus`, `pointerdown`/`touchend`/`click`/`keyup` in capture phase, and a 15s watchdog sweep — each no-opping when the lock is held, a request is in flight, or the document is hidden. Gesture events are retry opportunities, not a precondition: neither the spec (w3c/screen-wake-lock issue 350, open) nor WebKit requires transient activation, and #318's gate also disarmed after one event. New `WakeLockStatus`/`WakeLockEvent` (raised into `modules/ui/types.ts` per the type-barrel gate) carry `idle`/`unsupported`/`requesting`/`held`/`released`/`blocked` plus the `DOMException` name, deduped against the previous event. Because a wake lock has no observable effect in jsdom or any browser this repo can drive — the reason four passes shipped on inference — `gameLayoutData()` now keeps the last 8 events and `GameLayout.astro` renders them in a `<template x-if="wakeLockDebug">` fixed overlay behind `?wakelock=debug` (persisted in `sessionStorage`, cleared by `?wakelock=off`); that overlay, not another mechanism swap, is what the next iteration starts from. New **D274** (`decisions/frontend/architecture.md`) records the decision, the research, and the boundary: if the overlay reads `held` while the screen still sleeps, the cause is outside the page and no page-level code can address it. Spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-13-wake-lock-self-healing-design.md`, plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-13-wake-lock-self-healing.md`. `app/tests/modules/ui/wake-lock.module.test.ts` rewritten against the new contract (17 cases: immediate acquisition, retry after a blocked request, re-acquire on silent revocation / visibility / pageshow / watchdog, no request while hidden, error name on `blocked`, retries stopped by release and destroy, a sentinel resolving after `release()` released rather than leaked, no media element ever created); `app/tests/lib/ui/game-layout.data.test.ts` gained the debug-flag and capped-log cases. Validation: `cd app && npx vitest run` 3833/3833 pass across 211 files; `astro check --minimumFailingSeverity hint` 0 errors/0 warnings/0 hints; `npx fallow` 0 above threshold, maintainability 92.0 (good); `npm run format`/`format:check` clean; all 17 structural/context/decision gate scripts pass. `db:status`/`db:migrate`/`db:introspect` and `scripts/refresh-graph.sh`'s real refresh not run — no `DATABASE_URL`/Neon credentials and no `graphify` CLI in this sandboxed session, the established D193 precedent; no migration or schema file touched. On-device verification is explicitly not claimed: no iOS hardware or BrowserStack access in this session, so the fix is unverified against the platform it targets until the user reads the overlay on the device. Work done on branch `claude/ios-wpa-wake-lock-6gzp1b`.)
>

No app/database code touched.)

> **Version:** 1.60.0 (2026-09-13 — issue-274-eyep7v: Balanced Training's play page hides the bottom nav during play and gains a Leave button, matching every game mode (issue #274). `app/src/pages/training/balanced-training/play/index.astro` swaps `AppLayout` for `GameLayout` (`gameTitle="Balanced Training"`, no per-match title to compute) and adds `@confirm-exit.window="abandonAndExit()"` on its root `x-data` element — `GameLayout` already owns the header exit button and `ExitModal`, so no new component was needed (**D272**, `decisions/frontend/astro.md`). `balancedTrainingPlay()` (`lib/training/balanced-training-play.data.ts`) gains `abandonAndExit()`: for the three non-`GAME` steps it abandons the current step's `exercise_sessions` row (`PATCH /api/sessions/:sessionId`, existing endpoint) then the training `activities` row itself; for the Finishing (`GAME`) step it delegates to `finishing-step.data.ts`'s wrapped `abandonAndExit` instead, since that step's facts live on the real `$store.game` via `tuodPlay()`. New `PATCH /api/training-sessions/:activityId/abandon` mirrors the existing `.../complete` route — both now share one `transitionTrainingActivity(playerId, activityId, statusKey)` helper in `training-session.service.ts` — and reuses the `ABANDONED` `game_statuses` row `exercise_sessions` already accepted, now also valid for `activities` (**D271**, `decisions/api.md`; no schema/migration change). `playAbandonAndExit` (`play-lifecycle.ts`) gained an optional `redirectTo` parameter (default `/games`, every existing caller unaffected) and now `await`s its `onAbandoned` callback (previously fire-and-forget) instead of firing it synchronously, so a caller's own async cleanup (here, the activity-abandon call) is guaranteed to finish before the store resets and the page navigates. `finishing-step.data.ts`'s `finishingStep()` gained a second `onAbandon` parameter and now also overrides `abandonAndExit` (stops the timer, then calls `playAbandonAndExit(this, onAbandon, "/training")`) alongside its existing `uploadAndCompleteSession` override — both overrides exist because `tuodPlay()`'s own inferred return type never has an own `$store` field (Alpine injects it once mounted as `x-data`), so calling into the wrapped object from a differently-typed caller needs the override's own explicit `this: TuodPlayContext` annotation to type-check; `balanced-training-play.data.ts`'s own call site casts `this.finishing as unknown as TuodPlayContext` for the same reason, safe only because Alpine has already mounted `finishing` as `x-data` by the time a user can click Leave. New **F92**: the whole `/api/training-sessions/*` route family (`start`/`steps`/`complete`, all pre-existing, plus this task's own `abandon`) is missing from `06-API/00-Overview.md`'s Route Surface — a pre-existing documentation gap widened by one route, logged rather than backfilled since three of the four routes predate this task. `00-File-Inventory.md`'s `decisions/api.md` row updated (31→32 decisions, ~3.6k→~4.5k) to keep `check-context-budget.sh` passing after the file grew. Tests added/extended: `balanced-training-play.data.test.ts` (4 new `abandonAndExit` cases: non-`GAME` success, session-abandon failure, re-entrancy no-op, `GAME`-step delegation), `play-lifecycle.test.ts` (2 new `playAbandonAndExit` cases: custom `redirectTo`, awaited async `onAbandoned`), `finishing-step.data.test.ts` (1 new `abandonAndExit` case), `training-session.service.test.ts`/`training-sessions.test.ts` (route)/`training-sessions.test.ts` (client)/`types.test.ts` — new `abandonTraining` coverage mirroring each file's existing `completeTraining` case. Validation: `cd app && npx vitest run` 3799/3799 pass across 208 files; `astro check --minimumFailingSeverity hint` 0 errors/0 warnings/0 hints; `npx fallow` 0 above threshold, maintainability 92.0 (good); `npm run format`/`format:check` clean; all 18 structural/context/decision/findings gate scripts pass (`check-file-locations`, `check-astro-class-composition`, `check-astro-conventions`, `check-game-engines`, `check-refinement-coverage`, `check-type-barrels`, `check-alias-sync`, `check-constraint-mirror`, `check-no-inline-comments`, `check-style-tokens`, `check-findings-log`, `check-game-wiring`, `check-test-coverage`, `check-doc-links`, `check-context-budget`, `check-context-map`, `check-decision-ids`, `check-agent-mirrors`). `db:status`/`db:migrate`/`db:introspect` and `bash ../scripts/refresh-graph.sh`'s real refresh not run — no `DATABASE_URL`/Neon credentials and no `graphify` CLI in this sandboxed session, the established D193 precedent; no migration or schema file touched. No interactive browser walkthrough either, for the same reason (no reachable Neon/auth backend) — the change was verified by unit test plus a manual trace of the Alpine event wiring (`ExitModal`'s `$dispatch('confirm-exit')` → `@confirm-exit.window` → `abandonAndExit()`) against the identical, already-shipped pattern every `games/*/play` page uses. Work done on branch `claude/issue-274-eyep7v`, off `main`.)

> **Version:** 1.59.0 (2026-09-13 — warmup-sound-timer-bug: fixes the reported bug that Balanced Training's Warm-Up phase-change ping never played. Root cause: `SegmentTimer`'s `AudioContext` was created and `resume()`-d lazily inside a `setInterval` tick, on a timer started automatically by Alpine's `init()` lifecycle hook with no click anywhere in the chain — browser autoplay policy only lifts a suspended `AudioContext` synchronously inside a real user-gesture handler, so commit `63e0b2a`'s earlier "resume before beep" fix (a state check, not a timing fix) never actually unlocked audio. Since the Warm-Up screen was deliberately built hands-free (`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-12-warmup-play-interface-design.md`, "no button, no text") and this app has no `ClientRouter`/view-transitions carrying a gesture over from the previous page's Start button, the user chose to add one deliberate tap on the Warm-Up screen itself (option 1 of three presented) — a "Are you ready?" title with a "Let's play!" `Button` that unlocks audio and starts the timer. `balanced-training-play.data.ts`'s `startCurrentStep()` no longer auto-starts the Warm-Up timer; it stores `warmUpConfiguration` and sets `warmUpReady = false`. New `confirmWarmUpReady()` method flips `warmUpReady` and calls the (unchanged) `startWarmUpTimer()`, which now also calls the timer's new `unlockAudio()` before `.start()`. `SegmentTimer` gains public `unlockAudio()`, factored from `playBeep()`'s existing suspended-context check via a shared private `resumeIfSuspended(ctx)`. `WarmUpPanel.astro` gained the ready-screen `x-if` branch (reuses `components/forms/Button.astro`, no new component). New **D270** records the decision and the two rejected alternatives (moving the unlock onto the previous page's Start button; relying on browsers' Media Engagement Index heuristics) in `decisions/frontend/alpine.md`; `00-File-Inventory.md`'s row for that file updated (~5k → ~6.8k, description gains the D270 citation) to keep `check-context-budget.sh` passing after the file grew. `app/tests/modules/ui/segment-timer.module.test.ts` gained a direct `unlockAudio()` test; `app/tests/lib/training/balanced-training-play.data.test.ts`'s three existing Warm-Up cases that asserted the timer already running right after `init()` now call `confirmWarmUpReady()` first (same guarantee, now reached through the new gesture), plus two new cases cover the gating itself and the `SegmentTimer.prototype.unlockAudio` call. Validation: `cd app && npx vitest run` 3784/3784 pass across 208 files; `astro check --minimumFailingSeverity hint` 0 errors/0 warnings/0 hints; `npx fallow` 0 above threshold, maintainability 92.0 (good); `npm run format`/`format:check` clean; `check-context-map.sh`/`check-doc-links.sh`/`check-context-budget.sh`/`check-agent-mirrors.sh`/`check-file-locations.sh`/`check-findings-log.sh`/`check-test-coverage.sh`/`check-decision-ids.sh` all pass. `db:status`/`db:migrate`/`db:introspect` not run and `bash ../scripts/refresh-graph.sh` only warned (no `graphify` CLI) — no `DATABASE_URL`/Neon credentials in this sandboxed session, the established D193 precedent; no migration or schema file touched, and no interactive browser walkthrough for the same reason (no reachable Neon/auth backend), so the actual click → audible beep path is unverified beyond the unit tests and manual code trace. Work done on the pre-existing branch `claude/warmup-sound-timer-bug-v96xgf`, off `main` at `732ffee`.)
>
> **Version:** 1.58.0 (2026-09-12 — balanced-training-playable: makes Balanced Training's Start button work end to end, the orchestration/API/UI layer `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-12-balanced-training-playable-design.md` scoped and `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-12-balanced-training-playable-implementation.md`'s 16 tasks executed in full, inline (no subagents), on `claude/finalize-balanced-training-playable`. `app/src/repositories/session.repository.ts`'s `insertSessionRecords` split into independently callable `insertActivityRecord`/`insertExerciseSessionRecord` (both tx-scoped) plus a new `findExerciseRulesetVersionId`; `CreateSessionRecordsInput` widened so the same insert path covers both a GAME session and a non-game exercise session. New `app/src/repositories/training-session.repository.ts` (`findRoutineTemplateSteps`, `insertTrainingActivity`, `findActivityConfiguration`, `updateActivityStatusRecord`) and `app/src/services/training-session.service.ts` (`startTraining`/`startTrainingStep`/`completeTraining`) — the latter's `TrainingStepResolved`/`StartTrainingResult`/`StartTrainingStepResult` types joined the existing shared `services/types.ts` rather than a new per-service file, reusing the existing `ServiceResult<T>` the plan's own draft had duplicated. `session.service.ts`'s private `isActiveSessionConflict` exported (one line) so the new service's Finishing-step insert can reuse the same `uq_sessions_single_active` conflict detection without copying it. New `/api/training-sessions` (POST), `/api/training-sessions/[activityId]/steps/[sequenceNumber]` (POST), `/api/training-sessions/[activityId]/complete` (PATCH) routes plus Zod schemas and a `lib/client/api/training-sessions.ts` wrapper, wired into both barrels. `lib/game/board-input.data.ts`'s `boardInputData(onCommit)` widened to `boardInputData(onCommit, getTurns)`, dropping its hardcoded `this.$store.game.turns` read from `visitMarkers()` — all 9 existing `*-play.data.ts` callers updated to pass `() => self.$store.game.turns` in the same commit, full regression suite unchanged. New `components/layout/training/ExerciseBoardInputPanel.astro` (mirrors `BoardInputPanel.astro` minus the game-store gate) plus `WarmUpPanel`/`SwitchingPanel`/`DoublePatternPanel.astro`. New `lib/training/balanced-training-play.data.ts` drives all 4 steps — it keeps the routine's resolved step list (`sequenceNumber`/`durationSeconds`/`exerciseTypeKey`) from `startTraining()`'s own response rather than reading it back off `trainingEngine`'s `RoutineStepSnapshot`, which structurally carries neither field (it exists only for step-advancement bookkeeping); `switchingEngine`/`doublePatternEngine` are typed as the concrete engine classes, not the `DartExerciseEngine` interface, so `expireTimer()` is callable, mirroring how `tuod-play.data.ts` types its own `engine` field. New `lib/training/finishing-step.data.ts` wraps `tuodPlay()` unchanged, overriding only `uploadAndCompleteSession()` to advance the routine after TUOD's own completion — new **D269** records why the Finishing step populates the real global `$store.game` (`reset()`+`startSession()`) rather than forking `TenUpOneDown.astro`/`BoardInputPanel.astro`, which read it as Alpine's registered global store, not a prop. New `play/index.astro` and `lib/training/routine-start.data.ts`; `RoutineDetail.astro` drops its hardcoded `disabled` Start button for a `playPath` prop and `routineStart()` wiring. Neither the page's own `x-data="balancedTrainingPlay()"` nor the nested `x-data="finishing"` carries an `x-init` directive — Alpine calls a factory's own `init()` automatically once mounted (confirmed against `tuodPlay()`'s own standalone page, which has none either), which is what lets the nested Finishing scope's reconciliation run with `$store` correctly injected once `$store.game` has already been populated. `npx fallow`'s complexity gate flagged two new functions once the work was fully assembled — `startTrainingStep` (cyclomatic 16, cognitive 17) and `startCurrentStep` (cyclomatic 10, CRAP 31.6) — both split into single-purpose helpers (`startGameStep`/`startNonGameStep`/`resolveActiveSessionConflict`; `buildWarmUpEngine`/`buildSwitchingEngine`/`buildDoublePatternEngine`/`startFinishingStep`) with no behavior change. Several of the plan's own draft signatures didn't match the real codebase once implemented against it — `insertExerciseSessionRecord` needed a caller-supplied `withTransaction` wrapper the plan's draft called bare; `RoutineStepSnapshot` lacks `durationSeconds`/`exerciseTypeKey` entirely (resolved by keeping the API response's own step list as the source of truth instead); `@modules/training/interfaces`/`@modules/exercise/interfaces` needed the raised `@modules/interfaces` barrel, not the deep path; `EngineFacts` needed the raised `@modules/types` barrel the plan's own draft had actually gotten right before a spurious "fix" briefly broke it — each caught by `astro check`/`npx fallow`'s own gates during execution, not discovered later. New **F90**: the Finishing step's page markup carries no `CheckoutConfirm`/finish-confirm `ConfirmDialog`/`TenUpOneDownResults` — the underlying `finishingStep()` store still exposes all three's state unchanged from `tuodPlay()`, only the page's own v1 markup omits the panels that read it (matches the spec's own §7 "no completion summary screen for v1," but worth naming explicitly since a player finishing on a double or wanting to confirm early exit today sees nothing happen). Validation: `cd app && npx vitest run` 3764/3764 pass across 207 files; `astro check --minimumFailingSeverity hint` 0 errors/0 warnings/0 hints; `npx fallow` 0 above threshold, maintainability 92.0 (good), exit 0 (confirmed the pre-existing ~80 duplicate-clone groups and the `visit-stats.module.ts` refactoring-target suggestion, neither touched by this task, don't actually gate the build — `.fallowrc.jsonc`'s `duplicates.threshold: 0` means no limit, matching F72's own finding); `npm run format`/`format:check` clean; all 14 pre-commit structural gates plus `check-decision-ids.sh` pass; a full `astro build` (server/Cloudflare adapter) succeeds and prerenders the new play page alongside every existing game page. `db:status`/`db:migrate`/`db:introspect` and `bash ../scripts/refresh-graph.sh`'s real refresh not run — no `DATABASE_URL`/Neon credentials and no `graphify` CLI in this sandboxed session, the established D193 precedent; no migration or schema file touched, matching the plan's own Global Constraints. No interactive browser walkthrough of the golden path either, for the same reason (no reachable Neon/auth backend) — `astro build`'s full prerender is the nearest substitute available in this sandbox. Design: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-12-balanced-training-playable-design.md`; plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-12-balanced-training-playable-implementation.md`.)

> **Version:** 1.57.0 (2026-09-10 — exercise-template-type-backfill: fixes a migration bug the user hit running `db:migrate`/`db:seed` against production after 1.55.0 landed: `0028`'s comment claimed `exercise_templates` was "confirmed empty in production," which was false — `seeds/0002_default_templates.sql` already ships four rows (501 Match, Ten Up One Down, Singles Accuracy, Score Training Block) predating the `exercise_type_id` column, so `ADD COLUMN exercise_type_id UUID NOT NULL` failed with `23502` on every real database, `0028` rolled back every time (it never once committed anywhere), and `db:seed` then failed separately on `seeds/0015` inserting into a column that consequently never existed. Fix: edited `0028` in place — justified only because it had never successfully applied to any real database, so no deployed schema reflected its old text — to add `exercise_type_id` nullable instead of `NOT NULL`; `seeds/0014_exercise_types.sql` gained a second backfill statement (`UPDATE exercise_templates ... WHERE exercise_type_id IS NULL`, alongside its existing `exercise_sessions` backfill) setting the four legacy rows to `GAME`; new migration `0032_exercise_template_type_not_null.sql` promotes the column to `NOT NULL` after the seed runs — the identical `0029`/`0031` nullable→backfill→NOT NULL shape already used for `exercise_sessions.exercise_type_id`, just applied to the sibling column D263 missed. New **D266** (supersedes D263 on this one point only; the rest of D263 stands; numbered D266 rather than D264/D265 — both already claimed in parallel by other in-flight branches off the same 1.55.0 base, D264 by the already-merged training-engines-warmup task below and D265 by a separate unmerged frontend branch). Every stale `0001`–`0031` migration-range mention this task touched bumped to `0001`–`0032`: root `CLAUDE.md`, `database/CLAUDE.md`, `00-Context-Map.md` (two mentions), `00-File-Inventory.md`'s `migrations/` row. `06-Spec/02-Template-Layer.md` needed no change — its Key Columns list already treats `exercise_type_id` as NOT NULL with no per-migration citation, which matches the final state after `0032`. Work done on a dedicated branch (`claude/fix-exercise-template-type-backfill`) off `main`, per the root invariant; not run against the user's live production database by this session — the user's own terminal output was the only evidence available, no `DATABASE_URL`/Neon credentials in this sandboxed session, the established D193 precedent. `db:migrate`/`db:seed`/`db:introspect`/`astro check`/`fallow` not run for the same reason; no application code touched. Version bumped to 1.57.0, not 1.56.0, on merge with `main` — 1.56.0 was independently claimed by the already-merged training-engines-warmup task below; both branched off 1.55.0 in parallel.)

> **Version:** 1.56.0 (2026-09-10 — training-engines-warmup (Part B of the phase-1 training plan): the domain-engine layer Part A's schema (D263) was built for, on a fresh branch off `main` since PR #260 had already merged Part A — `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-10-training-session-phase-1-warmup.md`'s own Part B branching instruction (off Part A's branch) was superseded by that merge, same as the harness's merged-PR restart rule intends. `app/src/modules/game/types.ts`'s `StageTypeKey` gains `EXERCISE_SECTION`. New `app/src/lib/exercise/rulesets/types.ts`: `ExerciseRulesetVersionKey` (`"WARM_UP_V1"` only), `WarmUpPhaseConfig`/`WarmUpV1Config` (Zod, `.strict()`), `EXERCISE_RULESET_CONFIGS`. New `app/src/modules/exercise/`: `interfaces.ts` (`ExerciseEngine<TState>`/`ExerciseEngineFactory<TConfig, TState>`, parallel to `GameEngine` and never built on it), `engine.registry.ts` (its own `Map`-backed registry, deliberately not the game engine registry), `warm-up.engine.module.ts` (`WarmUpEngine` — appends one `EXERCISE_SECTION` stage per phase entered, holds no clock, rehydrates via `create(config, prior)`). New `app/src/services/exercise-rulesets/`: `interfaces.ts`/`types.ts` (split by the repo's type-barrel convention, unlike the plan's draft which mixed both into one file), `warm-up/warm-up.validator.ts`, `registry.ts` — kept out of `services/rulesets/registry.ts`/`RulesetVersionKey`/`RULESET_CAPABILITIES` entirely, since `scripts/check-game-wiring.sh` requires every key there to declare a capture/input mode pair and a warm-up has neither; `check-game-engines.sh`/`check-game-wiring.sh`/`check-file-locations.sh` stayed green throughout, confirming the split held. New `app/src/modules/training/`: `types.ts`/`interfaces.ts` (`TrainingEngine`, split out of `training.module.ts` for the same type-barrel reason — the plan's own draft declared the interface inline in the implementation file, which `check-type-barrels.sh` rejects), `training.module.ts` (`OrderedTraining` — ordered step progression, no clock, no knowledge of any `ExerciseEngine`'s internals), `routine-duration.module.ts` (`MAX_ROUTINE_MINUTES = 60`, `validateRoutineDuration()` — a `ROUNDS` step counts as zero minutes, `09-training-routines.md` §7). New **D264** records the parallel-contract-not-inheritance decision and the separate-registry rationale, both stated in the plan's own "Deviation from the spec" section but not yet recorded in `decisions/**`. Every barrel-raising edit needed by the type-barrel convention (`lib/exercise/types.ts`, `lib/types.ts`, `modules/types.ts`/`interfaces.ts`, `services/types.ts`/`interfaces.ts`) landed alongside its source file in the same commit as each task, not deferred. Two plan-execution gaps fixed during validation, neither anticipated by the plan: the validator and registry imported the `ExerciseRulesetValidator` interface through the wrong area-root barrel (`@services/types` instead of `@services/interfaces`) — `npx vitest`'s type-erased transform never caught it since a type-only import compiles away before the check that would have failed ever runs, only `npx fallow`'s reachability scan surfaced it as an "unused export" once the real declaration site had zero legitimate importers; and `WarmUpPhase` (`modules/exercise/types.ts`), part of the plan's own listed "Produces" interface for Task 8, had no consumer anywhere in Tasks 8–11 and was removed rather than kept speculatively. New **F76**: `09-training-routines.md`'s frontmatter says `status: canonical` while its body's own line 10 says "Status: Proposed architectural design" — noticed while citing the document for D264, logged rather than fixed. `00-File-Inventory.md`'s `09-training-routines.md` row updated to name the four new source trees now implementing its §8–§10. `app/CLAUDE.md` gains an "Exercise engines" bullet mirroring the existing "Game engines" one, so a future agent extending either the game or exercise engine family finds the right contract without re-deriving the split from source. Validation: `cd app && npx vitest run` 3624/3624 pass across 192 files (up from 3563 at the end of the prior quick-subtract-trivia task, reflecting Part A's own test growth plus 40 new tests this task added — 10 for `WarmUpEngine`+registry, 13 for the ruleset config Zod schemas the plan didn't originally have a direct-test task for (`check-test-coverage.sh` required one once fallow's dead-code scan forced the barrel-import fix), 5 for the validator, 2 for its registry, 7 for `TrainingEngine`, 9 for `routine-duration.module.ts`); `astro check --minimumFailingSeverity hint` 0 errors/0 warnings/0 hints; `npx fallow` 0 dead files, 0 dead exports (2 found and fixed mid-task, see above), 75 pre-existing duplicate-clone groups across the 9 dartboard games (none newly introduced — the plan's own predicted registry-duplication complaint did not materialize, both registries stayed small enough to stay under fallow's clone-detection floor), maintainability 92.0 (good); `npm run format`/`format:check` clean; all 14 structural gates named in the plan's own Task 12 Step 2 pass, plus every pre-commit gate on every commit. `db:status`/`db:migrate`/`db:introspect` and `bash ../scripts/refresh-graph.sh`'s real refresh not run — no `DATABASE_URL`/Neon credentials and no `graphify` CLI in this sandboxed session, the established D193 precedent; no migration, schema, or `app/src/db` file touched by this task, so nothing was left unintrospected that this task itself created. Design: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-10-training-session-phase-1-warmup-design.md`; plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-10-training-session-phase-1-warmup.md`. No API route or UI yet — both remain future sub-phases per the plan's own "Out of scope" section.)
> **Version:** 1.55.0 (2026-09-10 — training-schema-warmup (Part A of the phase-1 training plan): generalises the template and runtime layers so a non-game exercise (Warm-Up) can exist without being forced into the game abstraction (`09-training-routines.md` §12). Migration `0027` adds `exercise_types` (UUID catalog, mirrors `game_types`) and `exercise_ruleset_versions` (mirrors `ruleset_versions`, kept as a separate table because a game-backed exercise holds both an exercise ruleset and a game ruleset at once, §11/§24). `0028` gives `exercise_templates` an `exercise_type_id` and relaxes `game_type_id` to nullable (both tables confirmed empty, so no backfill), and gives `routine_steps` a `configuration` JSONB column (the §3.5 Routine Exercise Configuration; duration stays structural). `0029` relaxes `exercise_sessions.game_type_id`/`ruleset_version_id`/`capture_mode_id`/`input_mode_id` to nullable under two independent CHECKs (`chk_exercise_sessions_game_pair`, `chk_exercise_sessions_capture_pair`) and adds `exercise_type_id`/`exercise_ruleset_version_id`/`routine_step_sequence_number`; migration `0020`'s composite `fk_sessions_capability` is left untouched since it is `MATCH SIMPLE` and a fully-NULL warm-up row satisfies it trivially. `0030` adds `activity_configurations`, mirroring `exercise_configurations` one layer up — the Resolved Training Configuration snapshot (§18), no foreign key to a template. `0031` promotes `exercise_sessions.exercise_type_id` to NOT NULL after seeds `0014`/`0015` backfill it, the same `db:migrate → db:seed → db:migrate` order `0019`/`0020` established. Seed `0014` inserts `GAME`/`WARM_UP` exercise types, `WARM_UP_V1`, and `stage_types` id `6` `EXERCISE_SECTION`; seed `0015` inserts the one system Warm-Up routine (five 60-second phases in `exercise_templates.default_configuration`, per `09-training-routines.md` §16). New **D263** records the model (D258 was already taken by `decisions/api.md`'s statistics-overview decision — checked immediately before appending, per `DECISIONS.md`'s own next-id instruction). `06-Spec/01-Reference-Layer.md`, `02-Template-Layer.md` and `04-Runtime-Layer.md` updated before the migrations were written (documentation-first). `00-File-Inventory.md`, `database/README.md`, `06-Database-Specification.md`'s migration-range banner, and every stale `0001`–`0026` migration-range mention repo-wide (`CLAUDE.md`, `database/CLAUDE.md`, `00-Context-Map.md`, `05-Database/10-Database-Agent-Guide.md`, `05-Database/11-Neon-Integration.md`, `07-Frontend/09-Adding-A-Game.md`, `architecture/README.md`) bumped to `0001`–`0031` as a consistency propagation, not a scope change. Four `database/verification/*.sql` scripts ship alongside (`0027`, `0029`, `0030`, `0015` — 18 checks total) but could not be run in this sandboxed session — no `DATABASE_URL`/live PostgreSQL server here, the established D193 precedent; likewise `db:migrate`/`db:seed`/`db:introspect` were not run, so `app/src/db` was not touched by this task and its Drizzle introspection remains the repo owner's to run before merge. > **Version:** 1.54.0 (2026-09-09 — quick-subtract-trivia: ships "Quick Subtract", an ephemeral client-only checkout-arithmetic drill (`start − subtraction = ?`), as a new top-level **Trivia** section outside the 26-file dartboard-game pipeline — no `game_types` row, no `GameEngine` contract, no persistence. New `app/src/modules/trivia/dart-scores.module.ts` (`ONE_DART_SCORES`, `getRandomDartScore(maxScore)`) and `quick-subtract.module.ts` (`QuickSubtractGame`, the family's only class — Checkout Trivia has none). Independent rounds: `start` (2–501) and `subtraction` (any one-dart score ≤ `start`) are generated fresh every round, never chained off the previous result — this replaces the user's original draft, which chained off a running score and had a genuine dead end once the score dropped below 2. `modules/ui/segment-timer.module.ts` gains an additive `direction?: 'countdown' | 'countup'` option (default `'countdown'`) and `getElapsed()`; the 3 existing countdown consumers (Score Training, TUOD, 121) pass no `direction` and their own tests are untouched. New `app/src/lib/trivia/quick-subtract-play.data.ts` (`quickSubtractPlay()`, plain Alpine factory, no `$store`/`$persist`) registered in the existing shared `register-route-data.ts`; reuses `ScoreInputBuffer` (`@modules/game/score-input.module`) rather than a hand-rolled digit buffer, for the same ghost-tap debounce every other `ScoreInput.astro` consumer gets. New `app/src/components/layout/trivia/QuickSubtract.astro` (setup/running/finished `x-show` swap) and `app/src/pages/trivia/quick-subtract/index.astro`; new `app/src/pages/trivia/index.astro` (the shared family landing page `10-trivia.md` already anticipated — lists Quick Subtract now, room for Checkout Trivia later) and a new "Trivia" entry in `BottomNav.astro` (target.svg icon, between Games and Stats). Design: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-09-quick-subtract-trivia-design.md`; plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-09-quick-subtract-trivia.md`. New **D261**: trivia tools are plain OOP modules outside the `GameEngine` contract, not a new game type — extends `10-trivia.md`'s already-established precedent (Checkout Trivia) to a second, class-based tool, recording the one carve-out it needed. `07-Frontend/04-Modules-And-OOP.md` (0.2.1 → 0.2.2) gains a `modules/trivia/*.module.ts` OOP-boundary row and a new "Non-Game Client Tools" section; `07-Frontend/02-Folder-Structure.md` (0.2.3 → 0.2.4) gains a matching Colocation-vs-Promotion row (a single-route class still warrants `modules/`, unlike single-route plain functions). `docs/game-rules/trivia/README.md` rewritten to point at the shipped spec/decision instead of describing the shape as still-open. New Context Map pack row, "New non-game client tool (Trivia)" (~18.5k, computed from `10-trivia.md` + `04-Modules-And-OOP.md` + `02-Folder-Structure.md` + `07-Frontend/00-Overview.md` + `03-Alpine-Patterns.md` + `app/CLAUDE.md` + `docs/game-rules/trivia/README.md`). Two plan-execution gaps fixed during Task 8's own validation, neither anticipated by the plan: `dart-scores.module.ts`'s `ONE_DART_SCORES` ignore-comment (`fallow-ignore-next-line unused-export`) turned out stale — `npx fallow`'s dead-export scan already counts the test's import as real usage — removed; `QuickSubtractGame.answer()` (10 cyclomatic/11 cognitive/41 lines) and `SegmentTimer.start()`'s interval callback (11 cyclomatic/6 cognitive/28 lines) both breached fallow's complexity thresholds once the trivia code was added — both extracted into small private helpers (`parseAnswerValue`/`invalidAnswerResult`/`recordAttempt`; `tick`/`tickCountup`/`tickCountdown`/`completeTimer`) with no behavior change, verified by diffing `npx fallow`'s output against a baseline run at the pre-task commit (`d8f8389`) in a temporary detached checkout — confirms the pre-existing 74 duplicate-clone groups (~12.7–12.9%, unrelated to this task, entirely across the 9 existing dartboard games) and the `visit-stats.module.ts` refactoring-target suggestion both already existed before this task and are not this task's to fix; only the 2 new complexity breaches and the 1 new stale suppression were real regressions, and both are now fixed. Two small edge-case tests added alongside that refactor (answering before `start()`; stopping a countup timer) — not required by the plan, but the cheapest honest way to confirm the extracted helpers' behavior per `check-test-coverage.sh`'s own stated intent. New **F74**: `destroy()` methods on `*-play.data.ts` Alpine factories (`tuodPlay`, `scoreTrainingPlay`, `oneTwentyOnePlay`, and this task's own `quickSubtractPlay`) are never invoked by any Astro lifecycle hook — `07-Frontend/04-Modules-And-OOP.md`'s "Alpine teardown must call `destroy()`" rule describes aspirational, not wired-up, behavior; pre-existing gap this task's own code perpetuates rather than introduces, logged rather than fixed. Manual interactive browser verification of the golden path (setup → play → summary, count/timer modes) could not be completed in this sandboxed session: `/trivia` and `/trivia/quick-subtract` are `protected-page`-classified routes whose client-side `auth.store.init()` calls Neon Auth's `getSession()` and redirects to `/login` on failure — confirmed via a headless-Chromium run (no `PUBLIC_NEON_AUTH_BASE_URL`/Neon credentials in this sandboxed session, the established D193 precedent). Verified instead: both routes render server-side at 200 with correct titles; a JS-disabled screenshot of `/trivia` shows the landing page and its `GameCard` correctly, and the 5-item `BottomNav` (Home/Games/Trivia/Stats/Profile) rendering without overflow on a 390px viewport with "Trivia" correctly highlighted active; the `/trivia/quick-subtract` setup view stays correctly hidden pre-hydration behind its `x-cloak`, confirming no flash of unstyled content.
