> **Version:** 1.50.0 (2026-09-04 — dartbot-level-curve-recalibration: closes D-N, a user-reported bug in D-L's own level-curve refit ("level 15 suggests an average of 180... does not make any sense") — D-L's single-anchor-plus-power-law search only checked level 1, leaving level 15 to whatever the power law extrapolated to: `sigmaAlongMm`/`sigmaAcrossMm` so tight the bot landed treble 20 almost every dart, clipping D-M's three-dart-average band at the 180 ceiling and a ~96% checkout rate. The user supplied replacement guardrails at both ends: level 1 three-dart average 20–30 / checkout% 5–20%, level 15 average 90+ / checkout% 45–80%. Before writing any code, the two level-1 guardrails were probed directly against the live throw engine (`throwDart`/`skillProfileForLevel`, varying `sigmaAlongMm`/`sigmaAcrossMm`/bias independently and combined) and found mutually unsatisfiable in this scatter model — checkout 5%+ never appears before average is already past ~40, since a double and a treble are geometrically similar-sized rings and the same precision that raises one raises the other. Put to the user via `AskUserQuestion`, who chose a compromise anchor (~32–37 average, ~3–4% checkout) over forcing either literal band; level 15's pair had no such conflict and was searched directly. New `app/scripts/dartbot-level-curve-recalibration.ts`: `scaleCurve(base, scale)` uniformly scales `sigmaAlongMm`/`sigmaAcrossMm`/`biasXMm`/`biasYMm`/`outlierRate` (bias direction is preserved for free — scaling x and y by the same factor never rotates the vector); `interpolateCurve(currentTable, lowLevel, lowValue, midLevel, midValue, highLevel, highValue, level)` positions an intermediate level by where its *pre-edit* value sits, in log space, within whichever segment (1→6 or 6→15) brackets it, generalizing D-L's single power-law rescale to two segments anchored at three points instead of one. `main()` binary-searches a single uniform scale per anchor: level 1 against its three-dart-average compromise band (`[32, 37]`), level 15 against its checkout-rate band (`[0.45, 0.8]`, average checked only as a `>= 90` floor) — converged to scale `0.64375` (level 1, average 32.64) and scale `2.3125` (level 15, checkout 63.6%, average 136.4). Level 6 stays fixed at the exact D-E anchor throughout, per the anchor log's own stated rule. `LEVEL_SKILL_TABLE` (`skill-profile.module.ts`): all 15 rows' `sigmaAlongMm`/`sigmaAcrossMm`/`biasXMm`/`biasYMm`/`outlierRate` replaced with the script's real output, rounded to the file's existing precision; `decisionQuality`/`bedOffsetMm`/`bounceOutRate`/`deflectionRadiusMm`/`covarianceRotationDegrees` untouched, as in D-L. `tier-bands.test.ts`'s level 1/15 sanity bands re-set from real `vitest` output at the file's own seed (`700000 + level`)/5000 visits: level 1 average 26–40 (measured 32.15), checkout 0.008–0.04 (measured 0.0214), miss 0.08–0.2 (measured 0.138), treble band left unchanged (measured 0.0565 already fit inside it); level 15 average 115–160 (measured 136.11), checkout/treble 0.5–0.75 (measured 0.63/0.634), miss 0–0.01 (measured 0) — the 5-test monotonicity suite needed no change, confirmed still green across all 15 levels. `throw-engine.determinism.test.ts.snap` regenerated — numeric-only diff (landing coordinates for level 8, whose interpolated position shifted slightly under the new level-15 anchor), no structural change. `dartbot-level-select-stats.ts` re-run against the recalibrated table; `LEVEL_SELECT_STATS_TABLE` (`level-select-stats.module.ts`) replaced with its real output — level 15's average band is `100–180`, no longer clipped at a degenerate `180–180`. `08-DartBot.md` gains a new "Resolved: D-N level-curve recalibration" section and a 0.8.5 version-header entry (closing part of **F64** for its own future entries — the header itself, not the two entries F64 named as already missing); the Calibration section's level-picker paragraph widens its `08-DartBot-Anchor-Log.md` pointer from "(D-L)" to "(D-L, D-N)". New `08-DartBot-Anchor-Log.md` row recording both guardrail sets, the found scales, and the measured values — `00-File-Inventory.md`'s row for that file refreshed (~0.3k → ~0.7k, `check-context-budget.sh` had drifted 56%) alongside `08-DartBot.md`'s own row (~25.6k → ~26.6k, D-N closure named). No `DECISIONS.md` entry — D-N is resolved within `08-DartBot.md`'s own local lettered ledger (D-A…D-N), the same pattern D-E/D-L/D-M's own closures used. New **F65**: the original hand-set table's levels 7/8, 9/10 and 12/13 shared exact bias pairs before D-L, and both D-L's and D-N's ratio/log-position-preserving transforms carry that duplication forward unchanged — cosmetic, nothing reads bias equality as meaningful, logged rather than fixed since neither refit's job is to invent new hand-set values. Validation: `cd app && npx vitest run` 3390/3390 pass across 166 files; `astro check --minimumFailingSeverity hint` 0 errors/0 warnings/0 hints; `npx fallow` 0 above threshold (maintainability 92.0); `npm run format`/`format:check` clean; `check-context-map.sh`/`check-doc-links.sh`/`check-context-budget.sh`/`check-findings-log.sh`/`check-test-coverage.sh` and the other `app/`-scoped structural gates pass. `db:status`/`db:migrate`/`db:introspect` not run — no `DATABASE_URL`/Neon credentials in this sandboxed session, the established D193 precedent; no migration or schema file touched. Branch `claude/dartbot-config-insights-g8on7o` was restarted from `origin/main` at the start of this task per the harness's merged-PR rule — PR #238 (this branch's own prior task) had already merged, and `main` had already picked up its content under a different commit hash via a rebase-style merge, confirmed identical by `git diff` before restarting.)

> **Version:** 1.49.0 (2026-09-04 — dartbot-level-select-stats: closes D-M, `08-DartBot.md`'s deferred level-picker average/checkout UI (deferred at D-E's 1.47.0 closure, unblocked by D-L's 1.48.0 refit). `OpponentChooserModal.astro`'s level step gains a three-dart-average band and a checkout-% band above the slider — each a label, a numeric range, and a range bar (`bg-accent` fill on a `bg-surface-overlay` track, positioned/sized as a percentage of a fixed domain: 0–180 for the average, the real ceiling of three darts at treble 20; 0–100 for checkout %) — plus a persistent `Badge` pill (`Lv. {n}`) beside the retitled "Difficulty" heading, replacing the old drag-only floating tooltip and its `peer-active` CSS entirely. New `app/src/modules/dartbot/level-select-stats.module.ts`: `LEVEL_SELECT_STATS_TABLE` (15 rows, `{ averageLow, averageHigh, checkoutLow, checkoutHigh }`), `levelSelectStatsForLevel()` (clamped lookup, mirrors `skillProfileForLevel`), and `allLevelSelectStats()` — the single access point `OpponentChooserModal.astro`'s frontmatter calls once per render, serializing the result into the modal's Alpine scope via `JSON.stringify` (the same pattern `Toggle.astro` already uses to hand Astro-computed config to Alpine). This is the deliberate swap seam the design spec asked for: a later move to a live DB lookup or on-the-fly compute changes only `allLevelSelectStats()`'s body — sync today, `await`-able later since Astro frontmatter already supports top-level `await` — with no change to the caller or the per-level shape. New `LevelSelectStats` type in `app/src/modules/dartbot/types.ts`. Table values generated by new `app/scripts/dartbot-level-select-stats.ts`: per level 1–15 (seed `800000 + level`, `N = 5000`), the average band is the 25th/75th percentile (linear-interpolation, numpy-default style) of simulated three-dart visit totals at T20 treble; the checkout band is the 25th/75th percentile of per-batch checkout rate across 20 batches of 250 simulated D20 attempts each — a single pass/fail attempt stream has no spread of its own, so batching is what turns it into a distribution. `app/tests/modules/dartbot/harness/simulate-tier.ts` (test-only, exempt from D224) gained `simulateTierStatsDetailed()`, returning `simulateTierStats`'s existing aggregate plus the raw `visitTotals`/`checkoutOutcomes` arrays it already computed internally; both functions now share a new private `aggregateTierStats()` rather than duplicating the aggregation math, and `simulateCheckoutAttempts` (returned a hit count) became `simulateCheckoutOutcomes` (returns the per-attempt boolean array) — a pure refactor, verified behavior-identical by re-running `tier-bands.test.ts` and `throw-engine.determinism.test.ts` before and after. The design deliberately shows only two stats, not the three a reference screenshot suggested: no "scoring average" distinct from three-dart average has a defined meaning, so it wasn't invented. The module's own test (`level-select-stats.module.test.ts`) asserts structural sanity (`low <= high` per band, checkout bounds within `0..100`, all 15 levels present) rather than cross-level monotonicity — unlike `LEVEL_SKILL_TABLE`'s `threeDartAverage` (a mean over 5000 visits, verified monotonic in `tier-bands.test.ts`), a 25th/75th-percentile order statistic between two similarly-parameterized adjacent levels carries no such mathematical guarantee under finite sampling, and asserting it would have risked a flaky test on genuinely correct data — a deliberate narrowing of the design spec's own testing note, recorded in the plan's self-review rather than left silent. Design: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-04-dartbot-level-select-stats-design.md`; plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-04-dartbot-level-select-stats.md`. This same pass corrected a labeling bug the plan's own drafting introduced: the design/plan/shipped code and `08-DartBot.md`'s new section all initially reused the letter **D-L** for this decision, colliding with D-L's already-closed level-curve-refit meaning (two `## Resolved: D-L …` sections would have existed in `08-DartBot.md`). Corrected to **D-M** (the next unused letter after D-A…D-L) in every living artifact — `08-DartBot.md`'s new section heading and body, `level-select-stats.module.ts`, `types.ts`, `simulate-tier.ts`'s doc comment, and this entry — but *not* in the spec or plan under `docs/superpowers/**`, which stay as historical record of what was actually planned and run (`docs/CLAUDE.md`: "Historical records stay historical … status notes only, never rewrites"). `08-DartBot.md`'s D-E "Then done" line (added at 1.47.0/1.48.0) is also corrected — it previously said the level-picker UI "stays blocked" on `fitProfile()`, which stopped being true the moment D-M shipped in this same pass; it now points at D-M instead. Version bumped 0.8.3 → 0.8.4. `00-File-Inventory.md`'s `08-DartBot.md` row refreshed (~25.3k → ~25.6k) to name D-M's closure. No `DECISIONS.md` entry — D-M follows D-E/D-L's own precedent of resolving within `08-DartBot.md`'s local lettered ledger (D-A…D-M), not the global `decisions/**` ledger. No new `FINDINGS.md` entries from this pass's own work, but one pre-existing gap was noticed and logged rather than fixed in place, per the findings-not-fixed rule: `08-DartBot.md`'s version header was never bumped for D-E's 1.47.0 or D-L's 1.48.0 closures (it still read 0.8.3 dated 2026-09-03 immediately before this pass's own 0.8.4 edit, even though both those context-maintenance passes updated `00-Context-Map-History.md`) — see `FINDINGS.md`. > **Version:** 1.48.0 (2026-09-04 — dartbot-level-curve-refit: closes D-L, `08-DartBot.md`'s open decision on refitting `LEVEL_SKILL_TABLE` against the D-E population prior (1.47.0). New `app/scripts/dartbot-level-curve-refit.ts` exports `rescaleLevel(currentLevel, currentAnchor, anchorValues, p)`: for `sigmaAlongMm`/`sigmaAcrossMm`/`outlierRate`, `anchorValues.field × (currentLevel.field / currentAnchor.field) ** p`; for bias, the same magnitude rescale applied along `currentLevel`'s own bias direction, except when `currentLevel` and `currentAnchor` are bias-identical (the level being computed *is* the anchor level), where the result is `anchorValues`' bias directly — the spec's literal reference implementation always used `currentLevel`'s own direction and failed its own first test (`rescaleLevel(anchor, anchor, measured, p)` did not equal `measured` for either `p` tested), so this identity special-case was added during Task 1's red→green step; it has no effect on the real search since `main()` already bypasses `rescaleLevel` for the anchor level itself. `main()` binary-searches `p` (0.1–6, 40 iterations max) against `simulateTierStats(1, 900000, 20000)` until level 1's three-dart average lands in [26, 31]; converged to `p = 2.3125`, level 1 average 27.10. `LEVEL_SKILL_TABLE` (`skill-profile.module.ts`): level 6 set to the D-E anchor exactly (27.5/20.1/-5.0/3.1/0.003); the other 14 levels' `sigmaAlongMm`/`sigmaAcrossMm`/`biasXMm`/`biasYMm`/`outlierRate` replaced with the script's real output, rounded to the file's existing precision (1 decimal for mm fields, 4 decimals for the now much smaller `outlierRate` values); `decisionQuality`/`bedOffsetMm`/`bounceOutRate`/`deflectionRadiusMm`/`covarianceRotationDegrees` untouched — D-E measured spatial scatter only. Header comment rewritten to state the table's current calibrated-vs-hand-set contract only (D255), not the refit's history. `skill-profile.module.test.ts` gains one assertion locking level 6 to the D-E values exactly, satisfying D224 alongside the data-only edit. `tier-bands.test.ts`'s levels 1/8/15 sanity bands re-set from real `vitest` output at the file's own seed (`700000 + level`)/5000 visits, not the search's seed: level 1's spread widened enough under `p = 2.3125` that its old checkout/treble/miss bounds no longer held (measured 26.68/0.0092/0.04393/0.275); level 8's `missRate` fell to near zero (0.000333, under the old 0.004 floor); level 15's average/checkout/treble rates pushed toward their ceilings (174.82/0.9584/0.9569, over the old upper bounds) — the 5-test monotonicity suite needed no change, a power-law rescale of positive ratios preserves ordering. `throw-engine.determinism.test.ts.snap` regenerated — numeric-only diff (landing coordinates, one score/target pair that crossed a zone boundary), no structural change. `08-DartBot.md` gains a new "Resolved: D-L level-curve refit" section; D-E's "Not yet done" line becomes "Then done", pointing at D-L; the Calibration section's level-picker paragraph gains a sentence pointing at the new anchor log; a new Related Documents row added. New `08-DartBot-Anchor-Log.md`: append-only anchor history (date, anchor level, data source, measured values, `p`, verified level-1 band, task branch), first row filled with this task's real values — `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-04-dartbot-level-curve-refit.md`'s Task 6 explicitly required no placeholder text before committing. Design: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-04-dartbot-level-curve-refit-design.md`; plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-04-dartbot-level-curve-refit.md`. `00-File-Inventory.md` gains a row for `08-DartBot-Anchor-Log.md` and refreshes `08-DartBot.md`'s row (~25.1k → ~25.3k) to name D-L's closure. No `DECISIONS.md` entry — D-L is resolved within `08-DartBot.md`'s own local Open-Decisions ledger (D-A…D-L), the same pattern D-E's own closure used, not the global `decisions/**` ledger. No new `FINDINGS.md` entries. > **Version:** 1.47.0 (2026-09-04 — d-e-population-prior: closes D-E, `08-DartBot.md`'s open decision on the population prior `fitProfile()` needs to shrink toward at `n = 0`. A human ran the doc's specified extract against production data (`D-E-extract.md`, 328 `PLAYER`-only rows, `participant_type_id = 1`, joined to `dart_zones` for a readable `intended_zone_key` rather than the doc's literal raw `intended_zone_id`, which `missMargin()`/`zoneCentroid()` cannot consume). New `app/src/modules/dartbot/population-prior.module.ts`: `foldPopulationPrior(rows)` pools every row into one population-level scatter around each row's own declared-target centroid, reusing `zoneCentroid()` for the centre and the same row-exclusion rule `missMargin()` already applies (no single centre, or an unset landing point). Three points the doc's fold recipe left open, resolved and documented rather than left implicit: along/across stays unrotated board y/x, matching `throw-engine.module.ts`'s `covarianceRotationDegrees: 0` on every hand-set level today; "tail beyond 3σ" reads as one scalar threshold, `3 × sqrt(sigmaAlong² + sigmaAcross²)`, since a two-axis scatter has no single σ; sigma is sample stddev (n−1). New `PopulationPrior` type (`app/src/modules/dartbot/types.ts`) deliberately omits `outlierSigmaMm` — the doc's recipe never asks for it, so no formula was invented for one. New `app/scripts/dartbot-population-prior.ts`: reads `D-E-extract.md`, takes the **last** fenced ```` ```json ```` block (the file holds two query runs; the first uses the doc's literal `intended_zone_id` and is skipped), camel-cases fields, folds, prints the result — run against the real extract: `sigmaAlongMm 27.5, sigmaAcrossMm 20.1, biasXMm -5.0, biasYMm 3.1, outlierRate 0.3%, sampleSize 328, excludedCount 0`. `08-DartBot.md`'s "Still open" section is trimmed to D-K alone; D-E's paragraph and stale SQL snippet move into a new "Resolved: D-E population prior" section recording the measured values, a caveat that the sample is thin and almost entirely `DOUBLE`/`INNER_BULL` intents, and what's explicitly *not* yet done — `LEVEL_SKILL_TABLE` is not refit against this prior, and `fitProfile()` itself (phase 10) is not built, so the number is stored but unconsumed; D-K's own text corrected to name `fitProfile()`, not the now-measured prior, as its actual remaining blocker. The original task that surfaced this gap — a level-picker modal showing DartBot average/checkout-accuracy stats, styled like a reference screenshot — stays explicitly out of scope per the design spec's own scope check: D-D (`08-DartBot.md`) forbids a stated average until a level curve is actually fit, which this task's measurement feeds but does not itself complete. `astro check` caught a real type bug during Task 1's own validation, not simulated: `zoneCentroid()`'s `zoneKey` parameter isn't nullable, unlike `MissMarginInput.intendedZoneKey` — `missMargin()` already guards this with an early null check, and `foldPopulationPrior()` needed the same guard before its `excludedCount` bookkeeping could be trusted; fixed with an explicit null check ahead of the `zoneCentroid()` call, plus a new test asserting a null `intendedZoneKey` row is excluded without reaching `zoneCentroid()`. Design: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-04-d-e-population-prior-design.md`; plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-04-d-e-population-prior.md`. `00-File-Inventory.md`'s `08-DartBot.md` row refreshed (~24.6k → ~25.1k) to name D-E's closure and the two new files. No `DECISIONS.md` entry — the fold's method was already fully dictated by `08-DartBot.md`'s own D-E text; the three points resolved here were left-open specifics, not a new architectural alternative weighed. No new `FINDINGS.md` entries — the doc's own stale SQL snippet (raw `intended_zone_id`, unusable by `missMargin()`) was corrected in place as part of properly closing D-E, not left stale for a separate pass. Validation: `cd app && npx vitest run` 3365/3365 pass across 162 files (6 new/changed tests in the two new test files); `astro check --minimumFailingSeverity hint` 0 errors/0 warnings/0 hints (after the `zoneCentroid` null-guard fix above); `npx fallow` 0 above threshold (maintainability 91.9, good; the one flagged refactoring target, `score-input.module.ts`, predates this task); `npm run format:check` clean; `check-context-map.sh`/`check-doc-links.sh`/`check-context-budget.sh`/`check-decision-ids.sh` and all `app/`-scoped structural gates pass via the pre-commit hook on every commit in this task. `db:status`/`db:migrate`/`db:introspect` not run — no `DATABASE_URL`/Neon credentials in this sandboxed session, the established D193 precedent; no migration or schema file touched. > **Version:** 1.46.0 (2026-09-03 — 121/tuod/score-training-dartbot-wiring: the last three of the nine rulesets gain a DartBot opponent, closing the opponent-mode gap this branch started from ("I can't select DartBot still on the 121, tuod and score training"). `RULESET_DARTBOT` (`capabilities.ts`) gains `121_V1: true`, `TUOD_V1: true`, `SCORE_TRAINING_V1: true` — its doc comment rewritten to explain each ruleset's strategy assignment rather than just list keys. New `app/src/modules/dartbot/strategy/scoring.strategy.module.ts` — `chooseTarget(): ThrowIntent` unconditionally returns treble 20, since Score Training has no checkout or decision axis to route on (D-G). 121 and TUOD reuse `x01.strategy.module.ts` **unchanged**: both are checkout-ladder games sharing 501's `{ remaining, checkoutPath }` decision shape, so no new strategy module was needed for either — narrower than D-G's original "501 uses it plus a checkout branch" framing, and 501 itself was explicitly not touched. `OneTwentyOneSetupForm.astro`/`TuodSetupForm.astro`/`ScoreTrainingSetupForm.astro` now pass `allowDartbot={supportsDartbot(...)}` to `UserSection`, and each screen's duration-mode `x-if` guard widens from `guests.length === 0`/`> 0` to also key off `bot` — a real gap the design spec itself missed: without it, a bot-seated session showed the wrong duration controls even though `forceTargetIfGuested`/`forceRoundsIfGuested` had already locked the underlying value correctly. `one-twenty-one-setup.data.ts`/`tuod-setup.data.ts`/`score-training-setup.data.ts` each gain a module-level `guested(ctx)` helper (`guests.length > 0 || bot !== null`), `addBot`/`removeBot`, and `pendingBotLevel`/`showBotLevelPicker`, mirroring `five-oh-one-setup.data.ts`'s own shape; 121's `start()` renames its local `guested` to `isGuested` to avoid shadowing the new module-level function. `one-twenty-one-play.data.ts`/`tuod-play.data.ts`/`score-training-play.data.ts` each gain `botThrowing`, `findBotSeat`, `botDartIndex`, `throwOneDart`/`throwBotDart`/`throwBotQuickScoreDart`, `maybeRunBotVisit`, and `undoVisit`'s `undoToActiveSeat` branch, copying `five-oh-one-play.data.ts`'s phase-7 mechanism. TUOD's own adaptation: its per-attempt "remaining" is `seat.currentTarget`, not a visit-scoped carry — an attempt always restarts at the ladder target, unlike 121/501's visit-to-visit remainder — so `throwBotDart` reads `context.state()?.seats.find(...).currentTarget` directly rather than a formatted display helper. Score Training's own adaptation: `throwOneDart` takes no `remaining` parameter at all (Task 1's `chooseTarget()` reads no state), and `recordDart` widens from synchronous `void` to `async`, firing `maybeRunBotVisit()` at its tail — a `Promise<void>`-returning function assigned into `boardInputData`'s `void`-typed `onCommit` slot type-checks under TypeScript's own void-returning-function-type rule, so no call-site change was needed. `OneTwentyOnePlayContext`/`TuodPlayContext`/`ScoreTrainingPlayContext` (`types.ts`) each gain `botThrowing: boolean` and `maybeRunBotVisit(): Promise<void>`. Two pre-existing tests that used `SCORE_TRAINING_V1`/`121_V1`-adjacent rulesets as their "DartBot not admitted" fixture broke once those rulesets started admitting one — both retargeted to a ruleset that still refuses (D255/`app/CLAUDE.md`: re-pointed at the same guarantee, never a different one): `session-seats.service.test.ts`'s inverse-guarantee case moved from `SCORE_TRAINING_V1` to `121_V2` (verified `session-seats.service.ts`'s DartBot-support check runs before its `SEAT_CAPS` check, so `121_V2`'s own solo-only cap never masks the intended rejection reason), and `session.service.test.ts`'s integration-level case moved from the `SCORE_TRAINING_V1`-based `inlineRequest` fixture to a new `oneTwentyOneV2Request` fixture (`121_V2`, `{ duration_type: "TARGET" }`). `capabilities.test.ts`'s own `RULESET_DARTBOT`/`supportsDartbot` describe blocks gained matching new-ruleset acceptance tests and a `121_V2`-based rejection test, replacing the now-stale `SCORE_TRAINING_V1`-rejects-case. Design: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-03-dartbot-121-tuod-score-training-design.md`; plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-03-dartbot-121-tuod-score-training.md`. `08-DartBot.md` bumped to 0.8.3: the Module Boundary tree's `scoring.strategy.module.ts` annotation corrected from "shared by 501 and Score Training (D-G)" (stale — 501 was never actually refactored onto it) to "Score Training only (D-G) — 501/121/TUOD stay on x01.strategy.module.ts"; a sentence added under the Strategy Layer table explaining how "X01 ladder"/"Score Training" resolved; the play-loop dependency-table row widened to name all six now-wired rulesets and list all six play-data files. `00-File-Inventory.md`'s `08-DartBot.md` row (~24.4k → ~24.6k), `capabilities.ts` row ("six" → "nine" admitted rulesets), and `play-lifecycle.ts` row (naming Shanghai/Singles Training/121/TUOD/Score Training as consumers, not just Bob's 27/501) all refreshed. No `DECISIONS.md` entry — every part of the fix mirrors an already-established sibling pattern (five-oh-one-play.data.ts's own play-loop shape, five-oh-one-setup.data.ts's own bot-wiring shape), no new alternative weighed; D-G itself already recorded in `08-DartBot.md`'s own Open-Decisions table, not the append-only ledger. No new `FINDINGS.md` entries — F61 (Around the Clock/Doubles Training still unwired) and F63 (unrelated `setup-controller.ts` doc mismatch) both remain open, out of this task's scope. > **Version:** 1.44.0 (2026-09-03 — shanghai-dartbot-wiring: Shanghai gains a DartBot opponent, closing the same three-part gap `08-DartBot.md`'s own capabilities-map comment already claimed was closed for it. `RULESET_DARTBOT` (`capabilities.ts`) gains `SHANGHAI_V1: true`; `ShanghaiSetupForm.astro` now passes `allowDartbot={supportsDartbot("SHANGHAI_V1")}` to `UserSection`, mirroring `Bobs27SetupForm.astro`/`AroundTheClockSetupForm.astro`/`DoublesTrainingSetupForm.astro` exactly — it never had before, so the DartBot chooser never rendered on Shanghai's setup screen regardless of what the capability map said. `shanghai-play.data.ts` gains the play-loop wiring (`botThrowing`, `findBotSeat`, `throwBotDart`, `maybeRunBotVisit`, `undoVisit`'s `undoToActiveSeat` branch) by copying `bobs27-play.data.ts`'s already-shipped phase-6 mechanism verbatim — same `PER_SEAT` stage ownership, same `DictatedStrategy`, only `doublesPath()` swapped for `numbersPath()`. `ShanghaiPlayContext` (`types.ts`) gains `botThrowing: boolean` and `maybeRunBotVisit()` to match. A fourth, deeper defect surfaced investigating why the wiring alone would not have been reachable: `shanghai-setup.data.ts` hardcoded `rulesetVersionKey: "SHANGHAI_V2"` unconditionally, and `SHANGHAI_V2`'s own `SEAT_CAPS` default (no entry → 1) structurally blocks any second seat — the same F45 failure mode already fixed for 121 and Singles Training, just never applied to Shanghai. `rulesetVersionKey` widens to `singlesTrainingSetup()`'s own resolver-function precedent (`(ctx) => guested(ctx) ? "SHANGHAI_V1" : "SHANGHAI_V2"`, `guested` true for a guest or a bot), and `configOverrides` returns `{}` rather than `{ difficulty }` once guested — `ShanghaiConfig` (V1) is a `.strict()` empty schema with no `difficulty` field to receive it, unlike `SinglesConfig`'s locked-to-`"EASY"` field, so Shanghai's fix cannot reuse Singles' own "force the value back" shape as-is. New `addGuest`/`addBot` overrides in `shanghai-setup.data.ts` reset `difficulty` to `"NORMAL"` on success, matching `singlesTrainingSetup()`'s own `addGuest` precedent, so a HARD selection made before adding an opponent doesn't linger invisibly. Investigating the capabilities-map comment's claim also surfaced that it was never true for two of the five rulesets it named: Around the Clock and Doubles Training are admitted in `RULESET_DARTBOT` (since the dartbot-setup-wiring-fixes plan, 2026-09-02) with their setup forms already offering the chooser, but neither `around-the-clock-play.data.ts` nor `doubles-training-play.data.ts` ever received the play-loop wiring — logged as **F61**, not fixed here (out of this task's scope). Singles Training has none of the three parts at all — logged as **F62**. `08-DartBot.md`'s dependency table row for "The play loop" corrected from "wired it onto Bob's 27, the one `VISUAL_BOARD`-only ruleset `RULESET_DARTBOT` already admits" (stale on two counts: `RULESET_DARTBOT` now admits four such rulesets, and only two of the four are actually wired) to name both Bob's 27 and Shanghai and point at F61 for the other two; new 0.8.1 Version entry added. `00-File-Inventory.md`'s `08-DartBot.md` and `FINDINGS.md` rows updated (`FINDINGS.md` ~2.2k → ~3k after two additions, `check-context-budget.sh` had drifted 26%). No `DECISIONS.md` entry — every part of the fix mirrors an already-established sibling pattern (Bob's 27's own play-loop shape, Singles Training's own guested-resolver shape), no new alternative weighed. > **Version:** 1.43.0 (2026-09-03 — dartbot-levels-implementation: closes the gap between the DartBot skill model's already-shipped architecture (`08-DartBot.md`'s D-D: a plain 1–15 level, no tier names) and the client, which had no way to choose one — every DartBot seat was hardcoded to `DEFAULT_BOT_LEVEL` (8). `addBotOpponent` (`guest-list.ts`) now reads `context.pendingBotLevel ?? DEFAULT_BOT_LEVEL` instead of the constant directly, and resets `showBotLevelPicker`/`pendingBotLevel` to their defaults on success. `GuestListContext` gains two new optional fields, `pendingBotLevel?: number` and `showBotLevelPicker?: boolean`; `PresetSetupContext` and `FiveOhOneSetupContext` widen the same two fields non-optional, both initialized to `DEFAULT_BOT_LEVEL`/`false` in `setup-controller.ts` and `five-oh-one-setup.data.ts`. `OpponentChooserModal.astro` gains a second body/footer step: choosing "DartBot" no longer seats immediately — it reveals a native `<input type="range">` (1–15, bound to `pendingBotLevel` via `x-model.number`) with `<datalist>` ticks at 1/5/10/15 and a drag tooltip (`Level ${pendingBotLevel}`) styled like `BoardMagnifier.astro`'s glass readout bubble, positioned via a `:style` percentage binding and shown only while dragging through a CSS-only `peer`/`peer-active:opacity-100` mechanism (no third Alpine state field needed); a new "Add DartBot" button calls `addBot()` to seat at the chosen level, "Cancel" resets and closes. One `x-text`-bound `<h2>` covers both steps to avoid a duplicate `titleId`. No post-seat editing — changing a seated bot's level means removing it and re-adding, matching the existing guest pattern. Design and planning also hardened the architecture itself: `app/CLAUDE.md`'s comment rule (§Comments, scope widened from `app/src/**/*.ts` to also cover `.astro` frontmatter) now bars restating decision history inside a doc comment — no decision id, phase number, prior version, or architecture-doc paragraph narrated in prose, only a short pointer — recorded as **D255**, prompted by the plan's own first draft doing exactly that (`guest-list.ts`/`OpponentChooserModal.astro`/`BoardMagnifier.astro` already showed the same pattern). Spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-03-dartbot-level-picker-design.md`; plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-03-dartbot-level-picker.md`. One plan-execution gap fixed during Task 3: the plan's own markup left the chooser's default (Guest/DartBot) footer `x-show="!showBotLevelPicker"` without a paired `x-cloak`, tripping `check-astro-conventions.sh`'s x-show/x-cloak pairing gate — fixed by adding `x-cloak` there too, matching the other three conditional blocks in the same file. `00-File-Inventory.md`'s `decisions/context-system.md` row refreshed from "31 decisions, ~5.1k" to "35 decisions, ~6.6k" (`check-context-budget.sh` had drifted 23% after D255's addition last session). Live browser verification of the picker (drag, tick markers, Cancel/Add DartBot, remove-and-re-add reset) could not be performed in this sandboxed session — the app requires Neon Postgres credentials and its own auth (`/games/*/setup` redirects to `/login`), and neither `DATABASE_URL` nor the `neon` CLI's auth are available here; verified instead by static review of `Modal.astro`/`Button.astro`'s prop contracts against the new markup's usage and `GuestSection.astro`'s existing `x-if="showOpponentChooser"` wiring, both consistent with what Tasks 1–2 already expose on the shared Alpine root. Validation: `cd app && npm test` 3313/3313 pass across 159 files; `astro check --minimumFailingSeverity hint` 0 errors/0 warnings/0 hints; `npx fallow` 0 above threshold (maintainability 91.9); `npm run format:check` clean; `check-context-map.sh`/`check-doc-links.sh`/`check-context-budget.sh`/`check-agent-mirrors.sh`/`check-file-locations.sh`/`check-findings-log.sh`/`check-test-coverage.sh` and all ten `app/`-scoped structural gates pass. `db:status`/`db:migrate`/`db:introspect` not run — no `DATABASE_URL`/Neon credentials in this sandboxed session, no migration or schema file touched. F59: new `scripts/check-plan-findings-closure.sh` mechanically checks every `Closes FINDINGS.md F<id>` claim in a plan file against that same file's own text for a matching deletion step (exit 1 + stderr message on mismatch); wired as a `PostToolUse` hook (`scripts/check-plan-findings-closure-hook.sh`, added via `.claude/settings.json`, matcher `Write|Edit`) that filters to `docs/superpowers/plans/*.md`, blocks (exit 2) on a mismatch, and prints a non-blocking reminder when a plan's text names a `scripts/*.sh` path (not mechanically verifiable, since that would require executing the plan's own proposed diff). The gate script's own Step 2 acceptance fixture (a plan claiming `Closes FINDINGS.md F999` with no deletion step, expected to fail) surfaced a genuine bug during this task's own execution, not deferred as a finding since it blocked the task's own verification: the deletion-detection regex's `clos\w*` alternative matched the word "Closes" inside the claim sentence itself ("Closes FINDINGS.md F999"), so the claim doubled as its own deletion evidence and the gate silently passed malformed input — fixed by masking out the claim's own matched span before scanning for deletion evidence. Plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-03-open-findings-cleanup.md`. `00-File-Inventory.md` rows added for the two new scripts; `FINDINGS.md` row refreshed from ~2.7k to ~2.2k (`check-context-budget.sh` had drifted 23% after F58/F59's deletions) and its closure/addition history extended with F60 (play-data-lifecycle-dedup, recorded but not yet reflected in this row before this task) and this plan's own F15(partial)/F58/F59 closures. > **Version:** 1.41.0 (2026-09-03 — play-data-lifecycle-dedup: closes F27's play-data half. `five-oh-one-play.data.ts`/`one-twenty-one-play.data.ts`/`score-training-play.data.ts`/`tuod-play.data.ts` each reimplemented `abandonAndExit`/`back`/`uploadAndCompleteSession`/`playAgain`/`currentFacts` near-identically instead of adopting `play-lifecycle.ts`'s already-shared generic functions (the other 5 rulesets — Bob's 27, Singles/Doubles Training, Shanghai, Around the Clock — already had). `currentFacts` was exported then reverted to module-private once Tasks 2-4 removed every external call site (fallow flagged the now-dead export). `playAbandonAndExit` gains an optional `onAbandoned?: () => void`, called on the success path right before store reset/redirect, so 121/Score Training/TUOD's `timer.stop()` has somewhere to attach. `playUploadAndCompleteSession` gains an optional `resolveFinalState?: () => ReturnType<TEngine["state"]> | null` (default: the exact prior `context.engine?.state() ?? null`), since all 4 rulesets read final state through their own store-fold-based `this.state()` instead — preserving each one's completion-retry-with-no-live-engine path, the exact regression the plan's own investigation flagged as the trap in a naive pass-through. `runPlayAgain` gains two optional callbacks, `resetLocalState?: () => void` and `afterEngineReady?: (engine: TEngine) => void` — both deliberately parameterless-of-context (the plan's own draft typed them `(context) => void`, which does not type-check: `PlayLifecycleContext<TConfig,TEngine,TResults>` erases each ruleset's own extra fields like `pendingCheckoutScore`/`timer`/`scoreInput`, so a callback receiving it as a parameter cannot read or write them without a cast — callers close over their own `this` instead, the same pattern Task 2's `onAbandoned` already used). All four extensions are additive and optional; the 5 existing callers pass none of the new arguments and needed no edit. `five-oh-one-play.data.ts`'s own `buildResultsSnapshot` reads `winningSideKey`/`legsWonFor` off the page context directly rather than off a `finalState` value (unlike the other 3 rulesets' local snapshot builders) — a second plan-draft mismatch, corrected by wrapping it (`() => buildResultsSnapshot(this)`) rather than passing it straight through. `one-twenty-one-play.data.ts`'s `resetForReplay` and Score Training's/TUOD's `finalScoreTrainingState`/`finalTuodState` are deleted, each provably equivalent to the shared function plus a callback or `this.state()` alone. TUOD's own pre-existing asymmetry (`playAgain` never called `scoreInput.clear()`, unlike 501/121/Score Training) is preserved exactly, not normalized. Plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-03-play-data-lifecycle-dedup.md`; design: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-03-play-data-lifecycle-dedup-design.md`. `npx fallow`'s total duplication percentage rose rather than fell against the plan's own 11.4% pre-task baseline (12.2% after) — every clone group the plan targeted (3-/4-way spans across the 4 files centred on `uploadAndCompleteSession`/`abandonAndExit`/`playAgain`) is gone, but removing that literal duplication let fallow's near-miss detector bridge a much longer already-similar span between `score-training-play.data.ts` and `tuod-play.data.ts` specifically (105 to 531 shared lines) — the same "whole-file near-twin, not extractable blocks" pattern F27 already left out of scope for those two rulesets' engine modules, now visibly present in their play-data files too; logged as **F60** rather than acted on. `dead-code` issues: 0 (fallow's own scan; the `currentFacts` dead-export flag it raised mid-task is the reason for the export/revert above). Validation: `cd app && npm test` 3308/3308 pass across 159 files (run after every task, not only at the end, per the plan's own fragility warning about the Play Again path); `astro check --minimumFailingSeverity hint` 0 errors/0 warnings/0 hints; `npx fallow` 0 above threshold (maintainability 92.0, no numeric duplication ceiling configured in `.fallowrc.jsonc`); `check-findings-log.sh` passes (F27 deleted, F60 added). No `DECISIONS.md` entry — every extension mirrors an already-established generic-function pattern (Task 2's `onAbandoned` shape reused for Task 4's two new callbacks), no new alternative weighed. `db:status`/`db:migrate`/`db:introspect` not run — no migration or schema file touched.)

> **Version:** 1.40.0 (2026-09-02 — process-gate-improvements: five independent governance/gate findings closed (F5, F38, F42, F43, F50), plus one new finding raised (F59) and one doc-only investigation recorded. `scripts/check-context-map.sh`'s migration-range check (lines 51-68) flips from an exclusion list (`grep -iv 'seed'`, skip any line naming "seed") to a label-adjacency positive requirement: `grep -hoiE 'migrations?[^0-9A-Za-z]{0,20}0001.?[–-].?.?[0-9]{4}'` only checks a range sitting directly after the literal word "migration(s)" (F5). The plan's own given regex (a simpler line-level "does this line contain 'migration'" check) broke on two real lines in `docs/architecture/README.md` the plan's own baseline note never accounted for — a Version History note pairing "migration/seed ranges" under one shared label, and a sentence stating a migration range and a seed range together ("Migrations `0001`–`0021` and seeds `0001`–`0007` implement it."); the label-adjacency form correctly checks only the genuine migration claim on each line and skips the seed half, and also still fixes F5's original bug (a seed range phrased without the literal word "seed" no longer misreads as a migration range). That same sentence's now-exposed stale claim was corrected to `0001`–`0023`/`0001`–`0013`, since it is a live current-state claim, not a historical record. `scripts/check-game-engines.sh` gains a new "Resumable ruleset version wiring" section (after its existing per-module loop): for any engine module naming more than one `rulesetVersionKey`, its paired `*-play.data.ts` file must reference every one of those keys literally, or the gate fails — the exact structural gap that let SHANGHAI_V2 ship without its play page's resume logic ever being updated (F43); proven by reintroducing that exact bug on a throwaway local edit (gate correctly failed) before reverting it. `09-Adding-A-Game.md`'s touch list gains a note on the `<code-slug>-play.data.ts` row naming this new enforcement. F38 (historical spec discrepancy — nothing to fix, by its own text) and F50 (`session.service.ts`'s `buildSeatPlan` already has the early-return-per-branch shape F50 said it needed; `npx fallow` confirmed clean) closed doc-only, both already-resolved findings the plan named as pure FINDINGS.md deletions. F42 (why `fallow`'s duplication gate never flagged the pre-cleanup bust/checkout and `otherSeatsComplete` duplication) investigated on a throwaway branch (`throwaway/f42-fallow-investigation`, discarded after): hand-reconstructed the pre-fix duplication (5 bust/checkout sites across `five-oh-one`/`one-twenty-one`/`tuod` engine modules; 3 `otherSeatsComplete`-shaped inline folds across `tuod`/`score-training`) and ran `npx fallow dupes` in both default (`mild`) and `--near` mode. Not a threshold/size gap — several reconstructed clones were smaller than duplicates the gate already reports elsewhere in this codebase; it did catch an exact same-file repeat and one close cross-file pair, but never unified the whole family, because each hand-copied site's own local variable/field names and destructuring shape were enough to defeat token-based matching across files even under `--near`. Recorded as a new "Fallow Duplication Detection — Known Limitation" section in `06-Test-Strategy.md` (no prior fallow section existed there to append to, contrary to the plan's assumption). Plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-02-process-gate-improvements.md`. Global Constraints named this plan as closing all five findings, but only Tasks 2/3/6 (F38/F50/F42) carried an actual FINDINGS.md deletion step; Tasks 1 and 4 fixed F5's and F43's underlying defects with no corresponding deletion step named anywhere in the plan — both deleted directly in Task 6 once the gap was noticed running the full gate suite, and the gap itself logged as **F59**, the same failure category F50 already named for the DartBot phase-plan series recurring in a different plan series. `00-File-Inventory.md` rows refreshed: `07-Frontend/06-Test-Strategy.md` (~0.7k → ~1.3k, new fallow section) and `FINDINGS.md` (~4.1k → ~2.7k after five closures and one addition; closure/addition history extended). No `DECISIONS.md` entry — both fixes are mechanical (a script regex, a new structural-gate section mirroring an already-established per-module-loop pattern), no new alternative weighed. > **Version:** 1.39.0 (2026-09-02 — scoring-stats-correctness: four independent stats/results correctness fixes in the Score Training / Shanghai results path, closing F20, F34, F35, F37. `foldShanghaiState` (`shanghai.engine.module.ts`) gains `seats.length === 1 ? null : raceWinner(...)`, matching `foldOneTwentyOneState`'s already-shipped guard — a solo session that ends on a Shanghai now reports `winningSideKey: null` instead of leaking its own `sideKey` (F20). `visitScoreBandCounts` (`play-visit-stats.ts`)'s top-band check changes from `score === 180` to `score >= 180`, matching the other three bands' own "highest threshold" contract (F34). New exported `completedVisitsTotal(turns)` in `play-visit-stats.ts`, alongside `highestVisitScore`; `score-training-play.data.ts`'s `statsFor` swaps `total: seat.totalScore` for `total: completedVisitsTotal(seatTurns)`, so an open visit's running score no longer contradicts the other seven per-seat stats when a results snapshot is taken mid-visit (F35). Two new 1v1 regression tests (decided finish, tie) confirm the reshaped `ScoreTrainingResultsSnapshot`'s top-level `winningSideKey`/`status` — pure test coverage, no source change, both passed immediately as expected (F37). Plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-02-scoring-stats-correctness.md`. Two plan-authoring gaps found and fixed in the same task, both direct consequences of the plan's own specified fixes rather than out-of-scope changes: (1) the plan's Task 1 Step 5 named three stale `winningSideKey: "A"` assertions in `shanghai-play.data.test.ts` (lines 309, 394, 514) as all solo-session fixtures needing the null update; the third (514) is actually a 1v1 fixture ("ends the whole match instantly on either seat's Shanghai, naming that seat the winner") where seat A legitimately still wins under the new 2-seat-only guard, so it was left unchanged — updating only 309 and 394. (2) Task 2's `>= 180` fix, run against the full suite, broke an unrelated pre-existing test in `five-oh-one-play.data.test.ts` ("uploads the batch, completes the session, and snapshots per-seat results") whose 461-point visit fixture asserted `oneFortyPlus: 1`/`oneEighties: 0` under the old equality-check bug; `visitScoreBandCounts` is shared across every engine, and 501 (unlike Score Training) has no `max_visit_score` ceiling stopping a >180 visit total, so the plan's own Task 2 Impact note ("inert today... the engine cannot produce one") held only for Score Training, not 501 — the fixture's expected band counts were swapped to `oneFortyPlus: 0`/`oneEighties: 1` to match the corrected contract. `ShanghaiResults.astro`'s solo short-circuit, `one-twenty-one.engine.module.ts`, every seeded template's `max_visit_score`, and Score Training's persisted-mirror retry path are all unchanged, per the plan's own Non-goals. `00-File-Inventory.md`'s `FINDINGS.md` row refreshed from ~5.9k to ~4.1k (`check-context-budget.sh` had drifted 42% from this plan's four closures) and its closure history extended with F32/F33 (preview-seat-scoping-fixes), F31 (alpine-reactivity-fold-fixes), and F20/F34/F35/F37 (this plan) — F32/F33/F31 were never recorded when those three earlier plans actually landed (see F58, logged this task). No `DECISIONS.md` entry — each fix mirrors an already-established pattern (121's own guard, the other three bands' own `>=`, the other seven stats' own `completedVisits` filter), no new alternative weighed. > **Version:** 1.38.0 (2026-09-02 — tuod-hardening: three independent TUOD fixes closing F10, F18, F21. `applyTuodAttempt` (`tuod.engine.module.ts`) gains `MAX_FINISHABLE_TARGET = 170` (the highest three-dart double-out total), clamping the success branch symmetrically with the existing `MIN_FINISHABLE_TARGET` floor on the failure branch, so the checkout ladder can no longer climb onto a target no double can ever finish (F10). `FINDINGS.md`'s F18 closed doc-only: its claimed live-stats banner in `TenUpOneDownResults.astro` was already replaced by `SinglePlayerSummary`/`ComparisonSummary` in a prior commit, confirmed by grep before deletion. New private `TuodEngine.isMatchDecided()` mirrors `ScoreTrainingEngine.isMatchDecided()` (D229): `rejectionReason`, `recordDart`, and `wouldCompleteDart` now gate on it instead of the public `isComplete()`, narrowing engine-level record()-blocking to 2-seat (1v1) sessions whose score-compare outcome is settled. A solo session is exempt entirely — not just around MINUTES timer-expiry, the plan's stated F21 scenario — because `isComplete()`'s MINUTES reading (`timerExpired && attempts >= 1`) can turn true mid-session before the one finishing attempt is recorded, and the plan's own design deliberately relocates solo completion enforcement to `tuod-play.data.ts`'s existing `finished`/`showFinishConfirm` gate rather than duplicating it in the engine. This is a wider exemption than the plan's Task 3 text anticipated: it broke three pre-existing engine-level tests asserting a solo ROUNDS-complete session refuses further `record()`/reads `wouldComplete() === false` — both engine and dart-input paths. Rather than weaken those assertions to keep them green (CLAUDE.md's test-integrity invariant forbids re-pointing a test at a different input just to pass), they were updated to assert the new, intentional engine contract (record() no longer throws; wouldComplete() recomputes rather than short-circuiting), and a new `tuod-play.data.test.ts` case was added confirming `recordAttempt` still no-ops once `finished` is true — the guarantee's new, sole enforcement point. `isComplete()` itself, `ScoreTrainingEngine`, `checkout-path.module.ts`, and TUOD's solo-only MINUTES capture restriction are all unchanged. Plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-02-tuod-hardening.md`. Two plan-authoring gaps beyond the plan's own text, both fixed in the same task since they were either a literal input error the task's own test needed corrected or the direct, foreseeable consequence of the task's own specified design change: Task 1's own test fixture recorded a 168-checkout with `dartsUsed: 1`, which `checkoutDartsRejection` rejects outright (168 needs at least 3 darts) — corrected to `dartsUsed: 3`. Task 3's `isMatchDecided()` as specified exempts every solo session unconditionally (not narrowly scoped to the MINUTES-expiry race), the three broken pre-existing tests described above. `00-File-Inventory.md`'s `FINDINGS.md` row refreshed from ~7.9k to ~5.9k (`check-context-budget.sh` had drifted 33%, itself triggered by this and the immediately preceding plan's finding closures shrinking the file) and its closure history extended with F44 (prior plan) and F10/F18/F21 (this one). No new `FINDINGS.md` entries. No `DECISIONS.md` entry — mirrors D229's already-established pattern, no new alternative weighed. > **Version:** 1.35.0 (2026-09-02 — DartBot phase 7 (X01Strategy): opponent mode ships on `501_V1`, the first ruleset whose bot needs a real decision — checkout routing — rather than a dictated target. `RULESET_DARTBOT.501_V1` flips to `true` (`capabilities.ts`), so a bot seat is now offered on the 501 setup screen (`FiveOhOneSetupForm.astro` gains the same two-line `allowDartbot={supportsDartbot("501_V1")}` pattern `Bobs27SetupForm.astro` established at phase 4). `SkillProfile` (`modules/dartbot/types.ts`) gains `decisionQuality: number` (D-D's decision axis), populated 7→100 across the fifteen `LEVEL_SKILL_TABLE` levels. New `app/src/modules/dartbot/strategy/x01.strategy.module.ts` exports `chooseTarget(view: X01View, decisionQuality)`: below a 30-point threshold it always fires at treble 20 (`08-DartBot.md` §Decision degrades too's "low" tier); at or above it, it aims at the first step of `view.checkoutPath` — the caller's own `checkoutPathFor(remaining)` result, re-derived fresh before every dart so a miss that changes the remaining score self-corrects on the very next call with no visit-scoped state anywhere, never falls back to treble 20 only when no route exists (a bogey number, or above 170). `playFoldBotQuickScoreVisit`'s `throwDart` parameter widens from `() => DartObservation` to a new `BotQuickScoreThrower<TState> = (state: TState) => DartObservation` (`lib/game/types.ts`), the QUICK_SCORE fold's first real change since phase 6 built it ahead of a live consumer — recorded as D253 (`decisions/game-engine.md`), since D252 had predicted no such change. `five-oh-one-play.data.ts` gains `botThrowing: boolean` and `maybeRunBotVisit()`, wired from `init`, the VISUAL_BOARD `commitDart` tail, and `recordVisit`'s tail; it branches on `this.$store.game.inputModeKey === "QUICK_SCORE"` — VISUAL_BOARD reuses `playRunBotVisualBoardVisit` unchanged (mirrors Bob's 27), QUICK_SCORE calls the widened `playFoldBotQuickScoreVisit` with the concrete `fiveOhOneEngineFactory` (not the type-erased `getEngineFactory` registry, so `TState` infers as `FiveOhOneState` with no cast) and then `recordVisit(fold.totalScore, fold.totalScore === remainingBefore)` — a checkout being provably the only path to `totalScore === remainingBefore` under this engine's own bust matrix. `undoVisit` branches to `undoToActiveSeat` when a bot seat exists, otherwise the prior inline undo logic, mirroring Bob's 27's phase-6 shape. New engine-level contract test `app/tests/modules/dartbot/harness/five-oh-one-dartbot-seat.contract.test.ts` drives a full 1v1 match to `WON` with fixed human keypad visits and real bot darts, proving both `FiveOhOneInput` variants coexist inside one `SHARED` leg — the phase's own gate. Design: `08-DartBot.md` (already written, phase 5); plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-02-dartbot-7-x01-strategy.md`. Seven plan-authoring gaps found and fixed in the same task, none logged as findings since each was an adjacent edit the task's own acceptance tests genuinely required, not an incidental discovery of a pre-existing unrelated defect: (1) Task 1's own code snippet wrote `501_V1: true,` as a bare object key, invalid JS since the key starts with a digit and isn't a valid identifier or numeric literal — quoted to `"501_V1": true,`, matching the file's own existing `RULESET_CAPABILITIES` convention. (2) Two pre-existing test fixtures (`aim-resolver.module.test.ts`, `throw-engine.module.test.ts`) broke once `decisionQuality` became a required `SkillProfile` field, a fallout Task 2's own file list never named — fixed by adding `decisionQuality: 100` to each's local `SkillProfile` literal. (3) Task 3's own "parses a plain-number single step" test fixture set `checkoutPath: ["T20", "20", "D20"]`, but `chooseTarget` only ever reads `checkoutPath[0]` — the fixture as written would have exercised treble parsing, contradicting its own name — corrected to `["20", "T20", "D20"]`. (4) Task 5's own `maybeRunBotVisit` code checked `this.$store.game.captureModeKey === "QUICK_SCORE"`, but `captureModeKey` only ever holds `"RECREATIONAL"`/`"ANALYTICS"` (`capabilities.ts`'s own `ModePair` shape) — the QUICK_SCORE/VISUAL_BOARD value lives on the sibling `inputModeKey` field, exactly as this same file's own `statsFor` already reads it — the plan's condition as written would never have taken the QUICK_SCORE branch; fixed to check `inputModeKey`. (5) Two pre-existing service tests (`session-seats.service.test.ts`, `session.service.test.ts`) asserted `501_V1` as their example of a ruleset `RULESET_DARTBOT` does not admit, broken once Task 1 flipped the flag — repointed to `SCORE_TRAINING_V1`, still excluded. (6) Task 7's own contract-test config snippet omitted `checkIn`/`checkOut`, both required fields on `FiveOhOneSnapshot` — added `STRAIGHT_IN`/`DOUBLE_OUT`. (7) Task 5's own test sketch asserted `turns.at(-1)!.participantRef === HUMAN_REF` immediately after the bot's own visit with no subsequent human turn recorded, which is false by construction regardless of correctness — the plan's own "control returned" comment matches Bob's 27's actual phase-6 precedent test, which checks `activeParticipantRef` instead — corrected to match. `08-DartBot.md` (0.7.0 → 0.8.0; `updated:` 2026-09-02): the play-loop dependency-table row's "no live consumer yet" clause removed (now false) and a new phase-7 row added; the speculative `GameView contracts` `X01View` example (`{ remaining, dartsLeftInTurn, opponentRemaining }`) replaced with the shipped shape (`{ remaining, checkoutPath }`) — phase 7's own gate needed neither field, both deferred to a later phase if a strategy ever needs them. `00-File-Inventory.md` rows updated: `08-DartBot.md` ("phases 1–6" → "phases 1–7", "phase 7+" → "phase 8+", ~23.1k → ~23.9k), `capabilities.ts` ("three ruleset versions" → "four"), `play-lifecycle.ts` (D253 named alongside D252). No new `FINDINGS.md` entries. > **Version:** 1.34.0 (2026-09-01 — DartBot phase 6 (The Play Loop): builds the mechanism `08-DartBot.md` §The Play Loop specifies as one thing seen from four angles, generically in `play-lifecycle.ts`, and wires it onto Bob's 27 — its first live consumer. `undoToActiveSeat(context, participantRef)` pops visits until the named seat is active again (always at least once, so a solo session's undo button never goes dead), replacing a bare `undo()` for any session carrying a bot seat. `playRunBotVisualBoardVisit(context, botParticipantRef, throwDart, wait?)` drives the bot's whole visit — one real `playCommitDart` per dart, looping while the engine's own active seat stays the bot's — behind two required re-entrancy guards: `context.botThrowing` (a second concurrent call for the same trigger is a no-op) and a post-`wait(pacing.preThrowMs)` active-seat re-check before recording (abandons the throw if `undoToActiveSeat` moved the active seat away during the delay — this is the load-bearing guard; `botThrowing` only stops two *overlapping* loops from both reaching it at once). `playFoldBotQuickScoreVisit(factory, config, facts, throwDart, dartsPerVisit)` feeds a bot's darts into a throwaway scratch instance of the same ruleset and returns only `{ totalScore, dartsThrown }` — proven against `scoreTrainingEngineFactory` at the engine level, with no live `QUICK_SCORE` page consumer yet (no ruleset `RULESET_DARTBOT` admits is `QUICK_SCORE`-only until phase 7 adds the flag to `501_V1`). `Bobs27PlayContext` gains `botThrowing: boolean` and `maybeRunBotVisit()`; `bobs27-play.data.ts` wires the real phase 1–3 throw pipeline (`skillProfileForLevel`, `createDartRng`, `chooseTarget`, `throwDart`) into a `throwBotDart()` adapter whose `dartIndex` is re-derived from the fact log on every call (never held on a closure, so an undone bot visit re-throws identically from the same seed), and calls `maybeRunBotVisit()` from `init`/`commitDart`/`undoVisit`; `undoVisit` itself now branches to `undoToActiveSeat` when a `DARTBOT` seat is present and falls through to the unchanged `playUndoVisit` otherwise, so a guest 1v1 session is untouched. No `DartBot` class exists or was needed — the shipped phase 1–3 functions compose directly, exactly as `play-dictated-session.ts`'s test harness already showed for a solo bot. Design: `08-DartBot.md` §The Play Loop (already written, phase 5); plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-01-dartbot-6-play-loop.md`. Decision: D252 (`decisions/game-engine.md`) records the two reusability choices — an injected `BotDartThrower` rather than a `DartBot`-class or `modules/dartbot/*` dependency in `play-lifecycle.ts`, and the fold's ruleset-agnostic `{ totalScore, dartsThrown }` return shape. Two plan-authoring gaps beyond the plan's own text, both fixed in the same task rather than left to trip CI later: `BotQuickScoreFold` was written as an inline `export type` inside `play-lifecycle.ts` (Task 3's own code block), which `scripts/check-type-barrels.sh` rejects — moved to `types.ts` and imported back via a relative `./types` import, matching every other exported type in the file; and the plan's own D248-numbering note ("re-check ... in case a concurrently-landed phase already claimed D248") undersold the risk — the correct next id, derived from a repo-wide (not single-file) grep across all of `decisions/**.md`, was D252, not D248 or D249 or D250, three ids having landed on `main` from concurrent PRs (#205, #213, and one more) since this branch's checkout; caught by `scripts/check-decision-ids.sh` and corrected before commit. `00-File-Inventory.md` rows updated: `08-DartBot.md` ("phases 1–5" → "phases 1–6", "phase 6+" → "phase 7+", ~22.9k → ~23.1k), `decisions/game-engine.md` (45 → 46 decisions, D252 named, ~17.4k → ~21.9k — this was the token-budget gate's actual failure, `check-context-budget.sh` having flagged 20% drift before this edit), `bobs27-play.data.ts` and `play-lifecycle.ts` (both description rows extended to name the new bot-play-loop exports). > **Version:** 1.33.0 (2026-09-01 — DartBot phase 5 (Participant Write Path): closes `08-DartBot.md`'s write-path deferral by proof rather than by new code. New contract test `app/tests/modules/dartbot/harness/bobs27-dartbot-seat.contract.test.ts` drives a real `DARTBOT`-typed 1v1 seat through `Bobs27Engine` using the already-shipped throw pipeline (phases 1–3: `throw-engine.module.ts`, `skill-profile.module.ts`, `dictated.strategy.module.ts`) and confirms `buildEventsBatch` carries the bot's own `participantRef` through unchanged for every bot turn. `session.service.test.ts` gains a second test proving `appendBatch` persists a `DETAILED_DARTS` turn attributed to a `DARTBOT` participant exactly like any other seat's, reaching `insertBatchRecords` via the same generic `validateBatchReferences`/`resolveBatchStructure` path every participant type already used. `database/verification/0023_owner_scoped_dart_view_checks.sql` gains a third fixture participant (`DARTBOT`, `participant_type_id = 3`, `player_id IS NULL`) with its own turn and dart, extending the existing PLAYER/GUEST checks to prove a bot's dart is likewise excluded from both `v_dart_analytics` and `v_dart_locations` while `v_game_replay` deliberately still returns all three participants' turns; the anti-vacuity guard's expected count rises from 6 to 8. No source file under `app/src/` changed — direct reading of the write path (`events.payload.module.ts`, `session.service.ts`, `session-seats.service.ts`, `three-dart.validator.ts`, `quick-score.validator.ts`) confirmed none of it branches on participant type, so phase 4's `DARTBOT`-typed `SeatFact` (D220) and migration `0023`'s owner-scoped view join (D222) already covered a bot seat without change. `08-DartBot.md`'s dependency table row for "The DartBot write path" flips from "still deferred"/"`DARTBOT` is explicitly refused at the create contract" (both now false since phase 4 shipped) to "shipped"; version bumped 0.5.0 → 0.6.0, `updated:` front-matter to 2026-09-01. `DECISIONS.md` §Deferred no longer lists "DartBot participants (2026-07-12)" — the second and last half of that deferral, the guest half having been struck earlier. Design: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-01-dartbot-v1-delivery-design.md`; plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-01-dartbot-5-participant-write-path.md`. Context maintenance found `database/verification/0023_owner_scoped_dart_view_checks.sql` itself had never been registered in `00-File-Inventory.md`, a pre-existing gap dating to the script's original creation alongside migration `0023` (2026-08-21) — not this phase's doing, but this phase's own Task 5 step named updating that file's entry, so the missing row was added rather than logged as a separate finding (adjacent edit the named step required, not incidental discovery). `08-DartBot.md`'s own inventory row updated to "phases 1–5 ... are built ... phase 6+ (play loop, ghost mode, 501 opponent) remains design-only", its char/4 estimate unchanged (~22.9k, growth too small to shift the rounded bucket). No new `FINDINGS.md` entries — unlike phase 4, nothing in this phase's own plan tripped a gate or left a stale reference. No `DECISIONS.md` decision block — this phase confirms D220/D222 already cover a `DARTBOT`-typed seat; it weighs no new alternative. > **Version:** 1.32.0 (2026-09-01 — DartBot phase 4 (Seat Admission): admits `DARTBOT` as a third participant type through the seat-creation path, wired write-to-read: `ParticipantInput`/`ParticipantRef` (`pages/api/sessions/types.ts`) widen to `"PLAYER" | "GUEST" | "DARTBOT"` plus an optional `level` (1–15) and a returned `dartbot: { level, seed, levelSource: "MANUAL" }` payload; `SeatFact` (`rulesets/types.ts`) becomes a discriminated union with a `DARTBOT` branch carrying that same payload; `SeatPlan`/`CreateSessionResult` (`services/types.ts`) carry it server-side. New `generateBotSeed()` (`lib/id.ts`, Web Crypto `Uint32Array`, mirroring `generateId()`'s RNG) mints the seed; new `RULESET_DARTBOT`/`supportsDartbot()`/`DEFAULT_BOT_LEVEL = 8` (`rulesets/capabilities.ts`) is the shared frontend/backend admission policy, following the existing `RULESET_CAPABILITIES` placement precedent rather than living beside `SEAT_CAPS` as the architecture doc phrases it (`session-seats.service.ts` is backend-only; the setup screens need to read the same map). `session-seats.service.ts`'s `rejectSeatRequest` gates a bot seat on `supportsDartbot`; `composeSeatFacts` gains the `DARTBOT` branch. `session.service.ts`'s `loadCreateSessionLookups` resolves the `DARTBOT` participant-type id; `buildSeatPlan` mints level+seed and always sets `displayName: "DartBot"` server-side, ignoring a spoofed request value, mirroring how the `PLAYER` seat's name is already copied server-side. `session-mode-resolution.ts`'s `seatsFromParticipants()` — the "coerce anything-not-GUEST to PLAYER" two-way collapse the architecture doc names as the anti-pattern to remove — is deleted, replaced by a private `toSeatFacts()` shared by `startSessionInput`/`reseatSnapshot`; `participantsFromSeats`/`participantsFromGuests` gain a `DARTBOT` branch, `participantsFromGuests` a new optional `bot` parameter that wins over any guest (mutually exclusive by construction). `guest-list.ts` gains `addBotOpponent()`, mirroring `addTypedGuest()`'s single-opponent-slot refusal in both directions (a bot seat blocks a guest add and vice versa); `setup-controller.ts` gains `bot`/`showOpponentChooser` state and `addBot`/`removeBot`, `start()` now sends `participantsFromGuests(this.guests, this.bot)`. New `OpponentChooserModal.astro` — the setup screen's existing add-opponent button (`AddGuestButton.astro`) becomes a two-step Guest/DartBot chooser rather than a new screen; `GuestSection.astro` renders the seated bot's avatar (target icon) and remove badge; `UserSection.astro` forwards a new `allowDartbot` prop. **Scope decision, disclosed not silent:** `RULESET_DARTBOT` admits only `AROUND_THE_CLOCK_V1`/`BOBS27_V1`/`DOUBLES_TRAINING_V1` — 3 of the 5 rulesets `08-DartBot.md` names as already playing `DictatedStrategy` — because Shanghai V2 and Singles Training V2 can never create *any* 2-seat session today (F45, already open, explicitly deferred, not this task's to fix); only those three setup forms (`AroundTheClockSetupForm.astro`/`Bobs27SetupForm.astro`/`DoublesTrainingSetupForm.astro`) pass `allowDartbot={supportsDartbot(...)}`. Design: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-01-dartbot-v1-delivery-design.md`; plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-01-dartbot-4-seat-admission.md`. Execution found two plan-authoring gaps beyond the plan's own text, both logged as new findings rather than fixed silently: Task 4's `SeatFact` widening left `singles-training-results-title.ts`'s inline seat-shape param (`participantTypeKey: "PLAYER" | "GUEST"`) structurally incompatible, requiring a widen to `"PLAYER" | "GUEST" | "DARTBOT"` the plan never named (fixed in-scope, since Task 4's own typecheck step required it); and Task 7's `buildSeatPlan` code, committed verbatim, failed `npx fallow`'s health gate (cyclomatic 10, CRAP 31.6) — fixed by replacing its three interleaved ternaries with one early return per participant-type branch, same behavior — logged as **F49**, the second consecutive DartBot-series plan to trip this exact gate (F48, phase 2). Also logged **F50**: `participantsFromSeats`'s own docstring still names the just-deleted `seatsFromParticipants`. `00-File-Inventory.md` rows for `capabilities.ts`, `guest-list.ts`, `session-mode-resolution.ts`, `session-seats.service.ts`, and `08-DartBot.md` (its "no DartBot code exists yet" claim was already stale from phases 1–2 landing first) updated to match. Validation: `npm test` 3213/3213 across 150 files, `npx fallow` exits 0 (maintainability 92.0, 0 above threshold), `astro check --minimumFailingSeverity hint` 0 errors/0 warnings/0 hints, `check-context-map.sh`/`check-doc-links.sh`/`check-context-budget.sh`/`check-findings-log.sh`/`check-test-coverage.sh` and all ten `app/`-scoped structural gates pass; `db:status`/`db:migrate`/`db:introspect` could not run (no `DATABASE_URL`/Neon credentials in this sandboxed session — the established D193 precedent; this change touches no schema). Manual UI verification (Task 11) could only confirm both `/games/bobs27/setup` and `/games/501/setup` render at 200 with the new markup present in the raw HTML — full interactive click-through (opening the chooser, seating a bot) could not be exercised because every setup page redirects to `/login` before Alpine hydrates, this sandbox having no Neon Auth backend configured either (the same D193 precedent, extended to auth). > **Version:** 1.29.0 (2026-08-28 — Shanghai V2 Target Needed: new `SHANGHAI_V2` ruleset version adds a Normal/Hard difficulty toggle (`ShanghaiV2Config`) alongside the untouched `SHANGHAI_V1`, following the exact `121_V2` precedent — one shared `ShanghaiEngine` class via two `GameEngineFactory` registrations and a `difficultyOf()` config normalizer, `shanghaiV2Validator` registered in `services/rulesets/registry.ts` in the same commit as the engine change (`app/CLAUDE.md`-mandated), `SHANGHAI_V2: [DETAILED_DARTS, VISUAL_BOARD]` in `capabilities.ts`. > **Version:** 1.21.0 (2026-08-22 — whole-plan review fixes for the single-opponent-seat-remaining-engines plan, six findings closed on the same branch. (1) **Play Again was broken for every replayed 1v1 match across all nine games**: `createSession` was called with no `participants`, so the replacement session got one seat, and the new engine was built from the config captured BEFORE the store was reseated — every dart in the replayed match carried the finished session's `participantRef`s, which the batch endpoint rejects as `BATCH_REFERENCE_MISSING`. New `participantsFromSeats` (`session-mode-resolution.ts`, the inverse of `seatsFromParticipants`) derives the request from the finished match's own seats and returns `undefined` for a solo list; `runPlayAgain` plus the five bespoke `playAgain` copies (Bob's 27, 121, TUOD, Score Training, 501) now send it and build their engine from the reseated snapshot. Solo replays are byte-identical to before, asserted per game. (2) 121's `winningSideKey` gained the `seats.length === 1 ? null :` guard every other engine carries — a solo checkout at 170 had reported its own side as winner (the same defect F20 logs for Shanghai, which stays open: its `raceResult ?? compareResult` shape makes the guard's placement a judgment call).
