> **Version:** 1.7.51 (2026-08-19 — analytics-mode target section too small fix, owner-reported (#131): every ANALYTICS + `VISUAL_BOARD` play screen that also renders the darts-preview strip (`VisitPreview.astro` — Bob's 27, Singles Training, Doubles Training, Shanghai, Around the Clock) could shrink its `SinglePlayerDisplay` target/score section well below the intended 40% height, while 501/Score Training/121 (no `VisitPreview`, keypad-only capture) rendered correctly, per the owner's own two confirming comments. Root cause: `SinglePlayerDisplay`'s `max-h-2/5 h-full` class only ever set a *ceiling* — a `min-h-0` from the component's own base classes still won, so in a CSS flex-column whose total content (target + the extra `VisitPreview` strip + `BoardInputPanel`'s viewport-relative `w-[min(100%,60vh)]` dartboard) exceeded the available height, the browser's flexbox shrink algorithm gave the target *zero* weight (its `flex-1` resolves to `flex-basis: 0%`, so `flex-shrink`'s scaled factor — `flex-shrink × flex-basis` — is `0`, meaning it cannot shrink further and is left at its already-shrunk 0-basis size) while `VisitPreview`/the board (nonzero `flex-basis`, from auto/content sizing) absorbed none of it either, since nothing forced them to give the target headroom back. Fixed on both ends: (1) the five `VisitPreview`-rendering interfaces' `SinglePlayerDisplay` gained `min-h-2/5` alongside the existing `max-h-2/5` (`app/src/components/layout/games/interfaces/Bobs27.astro`, `SinglesTraining.astro`, `DoublesTraining.astro`, `Shanghai.astro`, `AroundTheClock.astro`) — min = max pins it to exactly 40%, so the CSS clamping pass now floors it there and pushes any remaining deficit back onto the flexible siblings; 501/Score Training/121 (`FiveOhOne`/`ScoreTraining`/`OneTwentyOne.astro`) were left untouched, already correct per the owner's confirmation. (2) `BoardInputPanel.astro`'s board is no longer sized off `w-[min(100%,60vh)]` (a viewport-relative *width*, invisible to the flex layout's actual leftover height): its outer `x-show` wrapper is now `flex min-h-0 flex-col items-center gap-3` and the board's own square wrapper is `flex-1 min-h-0 max-h-[60vh] max-w-full aspect-square` — a real flex participant whose rendered size (not just its layout box) now derives from whatever height the column's flex algorithm actually leaves it, still capped at 60vh for the no-deficit case. No engine/schema/API/store change — pure CSS layout fix over already-rendered facts, shared by all 8 play screens (`BoardInputPanel.astro`) plus the 5 affected interfaces; no new `decisions/**` entry, same precedent as 1.7.35/1.7.48/1.7.49/1.7.50's display-only fixes. No test change — `.astro` markup variant/sizing logic is untested per D101, and the full suite is unaffected by a pure class/layout edit (confirmed: still 122 files/2361 tests, 0 regressions); `astro check` 0 errors (5 pre-existing hints, unchanged baseline); `npm run build` prerenders all 21 routes cleanly, rendered HTML spot-checked to confirm the new classes land (`min-h-2/5 max-h-2/5 h-full` on Bob's 27's target, unchanged `max-h-2/5 h-full` on 501's); all 15 gate scripts pass; `db:status`/`db:migrate`/`db:introspect` and the manual browser smoke test could not run in this container (no `DATABASE_URL`, per the established D193 precedent — flagged rather than claimed, verified instead via the build's prerendered/rendered HTML output as above); branch `claude/issue-131-9orq5u` not yet merged to `main`, no PR opened as of this task; prior 1.7.50 (2026-08-19 — logo header-size regression fix (owner-reported): homepage header `<LogoLockup>` in `app/src/pages/index.astro`, moved into the header by 1.7.48's #126 fix, had its class changed from `size-40` to `h-8 w-auto` — but the SVG asset (`assets/logo-lockup.svg`) carries its own inline `style="height:auto"` on the root element, which always beats a stylesheet class for `height` (inline style wins the cascade), so `h-8` silently never applied; meanwhile `w-auto` did override the SVG's `width="1em"` presentation attribute, leaving neither axis with a real driving dimension — the logo rendered at a near-zero, "barely a mm high" size. Fixed by driving size from `width` alone (`class="w-24"`, 96px), letting the SVG's own baked-in `height:auto` compute a correctly proportioned ~33px height from its 230:80 viewBox — mirrors how the pre-move `size-40` happened to work (its height class was likewise silently overridden; only its width class was ever load-bearing). No schema/API/store/engine change; pure single-class CSS fix, no new `decisions/**` entry, same precedent as 1.7.35/1.7.49. Full suite 122 files/2361 tests unchanged (no new test — CSS-only display fix, D101); `astro check` 0 errors (5 pre-existing hints, unchanged baseline); `npx fallow` exits 0 (0 above threshold, maintainability 91.9); all 14 applicable gate scripts pass; `db:status`/`db:migrate`/`db:introspect` and manual browser smoke test could not run in this container (no `DATABASE_URL`, per the established D193 precedent — flagged rather than claimed; verified instead via `astro build`'s prerendered HTML output showing the corrected `class="w-24"` on the rendered `<svg>`); branch `claude/issue-logo-header-size` not yet merged to `main`, no PR opened as of this task; prior 1.7.49 — 2026-08-19 — two independent owner-triaged bug fixes shipped together on one branch: #128 (121 active score reverts to 121 instead of climbing to the new target after a checkout) — `one-twenty-one-play.data.ts`'s `remainingInAttempt()`/`visitsThisAttempt()` folded only `turnsInCurrentRound()` (the just-opened round's turns, empty immediately after a checkout) through `foldRoundState`, which starts from `initialOneTwentyOneState()`'s hardcoded `START_TARGET` (121) — so the instant a round closed, before its first visit, the hero score read back the stale 121 instead of the newly climbed target. Fixed by folding `$store.game.turns` (the whole session) instead, matching `currentTargetLabel()`'s own already-correct whole-session fold — `applyOneTwentyOneVisit`'s reducer already resets `remainingInAttempt` to the new target on every checkout when run over the full history, so no per-round scoping was needed at all; the now-dead `turnsInCurrentRound()` helper removed from both the factory and `OneTwentyOnePlayContext`. #129 (dartboard hover highlights the wrong ring) — `global.css`'s `.dartboard-group .segment:hover .single, .segment:hover .multiplier` and `.segment:hover path` rules keyed `:hover` off the parent `<g class="segment">`, which wraps all four rings (inner/outer single, treble, double) for one number — hovering any one ring (e.g. the inner single) fired `:hover` on the shared group and lit up every ring in it, including the treble, on every number. Fixed by moving `:hover` onto the specific `<path>` under the pointer (`.segment .single:hover`, `.segment .multiplier:hover`, `.segment path:hover`) so only the ring actually under the cursor highlights. No schema/API/store/engine change across either fix — pure display-layer corrections over already-recorded facts and already-existing markup; no new `decisions/**` entry, same precedent as 1.7.35's Bob's 27 bug triage. Full suite 122 files/2361 tests (1 new: a checkout-climb `remainingInAttempt`/`visitsThisAttempt`/`currentTargetLabel` regression test for #128, confirmed failing against the pre-fix code before the fix landed); `astro check` 0 errors (5 pre-existing hints, unchanged baseline); `npx fallow` exits 0 (0 above threshold, maintainability 91.9); all 15 gate scripts pass; `db:status`/`db:migrate`/`db:introspect` and the manual browser smoke test could not run in this container (no `DATABASE_URL`, per the established D193 precedent — flagged rather than claimed); branch `claude/issue-128-129-fixes` not yet merged to `main`, no PR opened as of this task; prior 1.7.48 — 2026-08-15 — three independent owner-triaged GitHub issue fixes shipped together on one branch, per the 1.7.42 precedent: #112 (uppercase non-analytics game inputs) — `SinglesRecreationalInput.astro`'s three literal-text tap-input labels (`Bull`/`Bullseye`/`Miss`) uppercased to `BULL`/`BULLSEYE`/`MISS`; the design spec and plan only named the first two, `Miss` was found during exploration and folded into Task 1's edit since it's explicitly named in issue #112's own wording (`isBullVisit()` left untouched — a method name, not display text). #113 (Singles Training session-summary stats) — `singles-training-play.data.ts`'s `uploadAndCompleteSession` widened its `resultsSnapshot` builder from `{points}` alone to also fold `misses`/`singles`/`doubles`/`trebles`/`hitPercentage` from `$store.game.turns` via the file's existing `countZoneKey` + `*_COUNT_ZONE_KEYS` helpers (already used by the play-screen's own live-stat getters, shipped 1.7.37) — `hitPercentage` follows `bobs27-play.data.ts`'s `computeStats()` precedent (`Math.round((hits/darts)*100)+'%'`, `"0%"` guard on zero darts); type widened in `lib/game/types.ts`; `SinglesTrainingResults.astro` gains five new `StatRow`s (Darts missed, Singles hit, Doubles hit, Trebles hit, Hit percentage). #114 (setup pages back button) — `AppLayout.astro` gains an optional `showBackButton` prop (default `false`, so its other 4 consumers — home, profile, statistics, games index — are unaffected), rendering a top-left icon-only `Button` navigating to `/games` via `chevron-down.svg` rotated 90° (reuse-by-rotation, the same trick `InfoSection.astro` already applies to the same icon for its own collapsed-state chevron — no new icon asset); wired into all 8 setup pages (`501`/`bobs27`/`121`/`singles-training`/`doubles-training`/`score-training`/`shanghai`/`around-the-clock`). Shanghai and Around the Clock needed only capability/validator/seed/frontend wiring — both engines already carried real `locationX`/`locationY` into dart facts, so `capabilities.ts` widened `SHANGHAI_V1`/`AROUND_THE_CLOCK_V1` to `[DETAILED_DARTS, VISUAL_BOARD]` and both validators were rewritten to the `isDetailedDartsOrVisualBoardCapture` dispatch shape `singles-training.validator.ts` already uses. 121 needed a real engine change since it records whole-visit totals, not darts: `OneTwentyOneEngine` gains a dual-shape `OneTwentyOneInput = OneTwentyOneVisitInput | DartObservation` `record()`/`undo()`/`wouldComplete()` dispatch mirroring `FiveOhOneEngine`, deliberately NOT a blind copy — 501 has no per-round visit cap, so folding an open (still-being-thrown) turn through its reducer only ever touches `remainingScore`, safe; 121's `applyOneTwentyOneVisit` treats every turn it folds as a FINISHED visit and always increments `visitsThisAttempt` on a non-checkout, so folding a still-open turn's partial total through it would prematurely count an unfinished visit against the round's 3-visit budget. `deriveState()` is split accordingly (`deriveClosedState` for closed turns, an open turn's running total only overlaid onto `remainingInAttempt` for live display), and `recordDart`'s round-stage-opening check gates on `settleVisit`'s own `resolved: boolean` return, not merely `visitsThisAttempt === 0`, to avoid a false positive on a fresh round's very first dart. `capabilities.ts` widened `"121_V1"` to `[QUICK_SCORE, VISUAL_BOARD]`; its validator rewritten to `five-oh-one.validator.ts`'s dispatch shape (`isQuickScoreOrVisualBoardCapture`/`validateQuickScoreTurns`/`validateVisualBoardTurns`). Frontend reuses `SinglesRecreationalInput.astro`/`VisitPreview.astro` unmodified (DETAILED_DARTS-only capture, Singles Training precedent over Bob's 27's dual-mode) and `play-lifecycle.ts` for its full lifecycle; play data's `previewSegmentsFor` is simpler than Shanghai's — no target-number comparison needed, since every tap this UI renders is already relative to whichever target was active the instant it was thrown, so a non-MISS dart is always a hit — and its `uploadAndCompleteSession` closes over `$store.game.turns` (captured before any store reset) to fold `{turns, hits, totalDarts}` at completion time, since the engine's own `state()` carries no hit-count fields; BULL-ring tap mapping follows the Singles Training precedent (SINGLE→OUTER_BULL, DOUBLE→INNER_BULL, TREBLE blocked). The board's 1.5s reveal-then-clear (hide a resolved visit's markers/preview 1.5s after its 3rd dart, only under `VISUAL_BOARD`) lives entirely in `commitDart`/`undoVisit`, overriding `boardInputData`'s own `visitMarkers()` after spreading it (object-literal key order). Final whole-branch review found and fixed one Critical bug the plan's own Task 1 code had introduced: `previewSegmentsFor` marked *any* on-board dart lacking `hitZoneKey === "MISS"` as a hit, so an off-target board dart (any zone besides the exact double/bull) rendered a false green checkmark — fixed by comparing the dart's actual `hitTargetNumber`/`hitZoneKey` against its own `intendedTargetNumber`/`intendedZoneKey` (what `Bobs27Engine.record()` already stamps from the target thrown at), the same equivalence `isHitOn` uses. Same review found resuming an already-terminal engine (e.g. a completion upload that saved the fact log but failed to mark the session COMPLETED) left `finished = false` with a live, throwing tap row — `init()` now checks `engine.isComplete()` and routes into the same completion path `commitDart` uses; `commitDart`'s `engine.record()` call also gained a defensive try/catch mirroring `five-oh-one-play.data.ts`'s established pattern. Same review flagged that the spec's Task 4 wording named only a target display, not a score display, for the play screen (`SinglePlayerDisplay`, target = current double/BULL) — surfaced to the product owner rather than silently added or silently omitted; decision: add it now, via a new `currentScore()` method + `SinglePlayerDisplay`'s existing `progress` slot (the same pattern `FiveOhOne.astro` already uses), not deferred. A Minor timer-leak (`commitDart` could overwrite `hiddenTimer` without clearing a still-pending one when two visits resolve within 1.5s) was fixed with a `clearTimeout` guard; its own regression test initially failed to actually guard the fix (both the leaked and replacement timer converged on the same end assertion) and was tightened to assert the intermediate state. No schema/decision-ledger change — mechanical extension of D198 (shape-dispatch)/D201 (marker lingering), per the design spec's cross-phase notes; this is the fourth and final phase, so the whole `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-12-bobs27-frontend-design.md` rollout is now complete. The `vPlayerSettings` entry was hand-written because introspect needs a live database and there is none in this container. It was verified against migration `0021` column-for-column and follows the file's existing view conventions, so the content is believed right and the violation is procedural. It must be diffed against a real introspect run before merge — if it disagrees, the next real run silently rewrites it and the mismatch becomes a runtime error rather than a type error (the same situation `locationX`/`locationY` hit in plan 1, D192). `0019`/`0020` were likewise never introspected. | `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-14-121-v1-design.md` | 121 v1 design: single-player X01-style checkout ladder from 121 to 170, double-out, 3 visits (9 darts) per attempt, fail-rule "stay" (miss the budget, retry the same target) — a checkout below the cap climbs the target by one, a checkout at 170 wins the session (2026-08-14) | historical |
| `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-08-14-121-v1-implementation.md` | The 6-task plan implementing that spec: config schema + capability declaration, engine + server validator + DB seed (one commit, same `scripts/check-game-engines.sh` atomicity constraint as Shanghai), games-index card, setup flow, play flow, final gates. `OneTwentyOneEngine` reuses the previously-unused `ROUND` `StageTypeKey`, one per attempt, mirroring 501's per-leg `LEG` stage model; `OneTwentyOneState` gained a `remainingInAttempt` field the spec's own sketch omitted (mechanical application of the already-canonical fact-log-fold pattern `FiveOhOneState.remainingScore` established, not a new decision). Play data is bespoke (`one-twenty-one-play.data.ts`, double-confirm + session-finish-confirm dialogs mirroring `five-oh-one-play.data.ts`), deliberately not `play-lifecycle.ts` — already out of scope per D209's own `GameEngine<DartObservation, unknown>` constraint, same reason 501/Score Training stay out. Four plan-quality bugs caught and fixed during execution, none of them architectural: the plan's own engine/play-data tests climbed the ladder to 170 via a fixed `scoreAttempted: 2` per visit instead of the actual per-target checkout amount (`target`), and one play-data test asserted a bust off `scoreAttempted: 41` where the setup visit left a remaining score of 80, not 41 (needed `81`, mirroring the sibling test that already got this right) — both are copy/typo-class test-authoring errors, not implementation bugs, caught by running the tests rather than trusting the plan's pre-written expected output; `capabilities.test.ts`'s hardcoded ruleset-key array and `database/README.md`'s Seed Order/verification-script table were both missing a `121_V1`/`0009` entry the plan never scheduled a step for; and neither `oneTwentyOneSetup` nor `oneTwentyOnePlay` was wired into `register-route-data.ts`'s `Alpine.data()` calls, a step no task in the plan named even though `03-Alpine-Patterns.md` already documents the requirement — without it neither route's `x-data` resolves at runtime. `db:status`/`db:migrate`/`db:seed` and the seed-0009/0007 live verification script could not run in this container (no `DATABASE_URL`, per the established D193 precedent — flagged rather than claimed), nor could the manual browser smoke test that depends on a live session-creation API; `database/README.md`'s Seed Order and verification-script table updated for `0009` (2026-08-15) | historical |
| `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-15-around-the-clock-v1-design.md` | Around the Clock v1 design: fixed 21-target path (1→20 then BULL), any hit (single/double/treble) advances the target immediately, mid-visit — the classic pub-rule shape, not the fixed-target-per-visit shape existing engines use; BULL (either ring) ends the session immediately, even mid-visit; `intendedTargetNumber`/`intendedZoneKey` stay null on every dart, same convention as Singles Training/Shanghai, since the active target is still exactly recoverable by replaying `facts()`; DETAILED_DARTS-only capture, `SinglesRecreationalInput.astro`/`VisitPreview.astro` reused unmodified (2026-08-15) | historical |
| `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-08-15-around-the-clock-v1.md` | The 5-task plan implementing that spec: config schema, engine + server validator + capability (one commit, same `scripts/check-game-engines.sh` atomicity constraint as Shanghai/121), DB seed (`0010`, capability row appended to `0007`'s ledger), setup flow, play flow. `AroundTheClockEngine` advances `targetIndex` on every hit before the 3-dart visit-boundary check and short-circuits to `COMPLETE` on a BULL hit regardless of `dartsThisVisit`, so a turn can close permanently short of 3 darts; play data's `previewSegmentsFor` needs no target-number comparison (every tap is already relative to the target active at throw time) and its `uploadAndCompleteSession` closes over `$store.game.turns` to fold `{turns, hits, totalDarts}`, since the engine's `state()` carries no hit-count fields; BULL-ring tap mapping follows the Singles Training precedent (SINGLE→OUTER_BULL, DOUBLE→INNER_BULL, TREBLE blocked). One plan-quality gap caught and fixed during the final task, the same class the 121 v1 rollout hit: neither `aroundTheClockSetup` nor `aroundTheClockPlay` was wired into `register-route-data.ts`'s `Alpine.data()` calls by the setup-flow task, a step no task in the plan named — without it neither route's `x-data` resolves at runtime; both registered together in the play-flow task's commit. `db:status`/`db:migrate`/`db:seed`/`db:verify` and the seed-0010/0007 live verification script could not run in this container (no `DATABASE_URL`, per the established D193 precedent — flagged rather than claimed); `database/README.md`'s Seed Order and verification-script table updated for `0010` (2026-08-15) | historical |

Full suite 117 files/2332 tests, 0 regressions; `npx fallow` exits 0; `db:status`/`db:migrate`/`db:introspect`, the seed-0007 live verification script, and the manual browser smoke test could not run in this container (no `DATABASE_URL`, per the established D193 precedent — flagged rather than claimed) (2026-08-15) | historical |

| `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-19-governance-design.md` | Spec 2 of the three-spec agent-context program: a finding is logged, not acted on. Establishes one rule (a root `CLAUDE.md` Hard Invariant, permission required to act on any incidental discovery), one log (`FINDINGS.md`, `DECISIONS.md`'s block shape with the opposite lifecycle — resolved entries are deleted, so ids come from a `highest-issued:` mark rather than a scan) and one gate (`scripts/check-findings-log.sh`, shape and evidence-liveness only, with its inability to prove an agent logged rather than fixed stated plainly). Absorbs D107's self-learning gate rather than running it alongside. Ships seeded with the five live contradictions found while writing it and fixes none of them — the restraint is load-bearing, not an omission (2026-08-19) | historical |

Three plan gaps found and fixed: `Seated<T>` had to become a conditional type because three snapshots are `Record<string, never>` (an intersection narrows `seats` to `never`); `record()` derived its returned state before opening the next leg's stage, reporting the winning seat at 0 and the others un-reset; and the seat-rejection test's regex did not match the plan's own message.
